
I'll begin processing this comprehensive Hospital ERP database architecture. Given the extensive scope (54 domains), I'll follow your incremental domain-driven generation approach as instructed.

## Domain 1: Core Infrastructure

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-tenancy: This is a multi-hospital system with tenant isolation
- Geographic scope: Global deployment with multi-branch support
- Regulatory compliance: HIPAA, GDPR, ISO 27001 requirements assumed
- Audit requirements: Full audit trail for all operational tables
- Performance: High concurrency expected (1000+ concurrent users)

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Foundation for all other domains - tenant management, user authentication, role-based access control, and organizational structure.

Sub-modules:

- Tenant Management
- Branch/Department Structure
- User & Role Management
- Geographic Reference Data

Entity List:

- tenants
- branches
- departments
- designations
- specializations
- users
- roles
- permissions
- user_roles
- countries
- states
- cities

### 3. Entity Design

tenants

- Purpose: Top-level organizational unit (hospital group/network)
- Attributes:
    - tenant_id (UUID PK) - Unique identifier
    - tenant_code (VARCHAR(10)) - Short code for business references
    - tenant_name (VARCHAR(255)) - Full organization name
    - legal_name (VARCHAR(255)) - Legal entity name
    - tax_id (VARCHAR(50)) - Encrypted tax identifier
    - address (TEXT) - Primary address
    - contact_email (email_type) - Primary contact
    - contact_phone (phone_type) - Primary phone
    - is_active (BOOLEAN) - Active status
    - activation_date (DATE) - When tenant was activated
    - deactivation_date (DATE) - When tenant was deactivated

branches

- Purpose: Individual hospital locations within a tenant
- Attributes:
    - branch_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK) - Parent organization
    - branch_code (VARCHAR(10)) - Location code
    - branch_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - address (TEXT)
    - city_id (UUID FK) - Reference to cities
    - postal_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - phone (phone_type)
    - emergency_phone (phone_type)
    - is_24_hours (BOOLEAN) - 24/7 operation
    - bed_capacity (INTEGER) - Total bed capacity
    - license_number (VARCHAR(100)) - Healthcare license
    - accreditation_status (SMALLINT) - JCI, NABH, etc.

departments

- Purpose: Organizational units within branches (Cardiology, Radiology, etc.)
- Attributes:
    - department_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - department_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - department_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - department_type (SMALLINT) - Clinical/Non-clinical
    - parent_department_id (UUID FK) - Hierarchical structure
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

users

- Purpose: System users with authentication
- Attributes:
    - user_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - username (VARCHAR(100)) - Unique per tenant
    - password_hash (BYTEA) - Encrypted
    - email (email_type)
    - first_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - last_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - date_of_birth (DATE)
    - gender (SMALLINT) - 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other
    - employee_id (VARCHAR(50)) - HR employee ID
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - designation_id (UUID FK)
    - specialization_id (UUID FK) - Medical specialty
    - license_number (VARCHAR(100)) - Medical license
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - last_login_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- tenants (1) → branches (N)
- branches (1) → departments (N)
- departments (1) → users (N) [via department_id]
- countries (1) → states (N)
- states (1) → cities (N)

N:N Relationships:

- users ↔ roles (via user_roles junction table)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for all FKs (prevent accidental deletion)
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for code updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- This domain has NO external dependencies
- ALL other domains depend on this foundation

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: Strict 3NF compliance

- No JSONB in this domain (structured relational data)
- Lookup tables for enumerations (department_types, user_statuses)
- Hierarchical data handled via parent_id self-reference

Data Types:

- UUID for all primary keys
- Domain types for validation (email_type, phone_type)
- SMALLINT for status fields with CHECK constraints

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- tenants: Small (10-100 rows)
- branches: Small-Medium (100-1,000 rows)
- users: Medium-Large (10,000-100,000 rows)
- departments: Small (100-500 rows)

Indexing Strategy:

- Unique indexes on all business keys (tenant_code, branch_code, username)
- Composite indexes for common queries (tenant_id + is_active)
- Partial indexes for active records

Partitioning: Not required for this domain (small tables)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Three-tier role hierarchy: permissions → roles → users
- Fine-grained permission control
- Tenant isolation at application level

RLS Design:

- RLS enabled on all tables
- Policies based on tenant_id isolation
- Super admin can cross-tenant (with careful auditing)

Sensitive Data:

- Passwords: bcrypt hashing with salt
- Tax IDs: cell-level encryption
- Personal data: encryption at rest

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100+ tenants
- 1000+ branches globally
- 1M+ users across system

Cross-domain Impact:

- All domains inherit tenant/branch structure
- User authentication centralized
- Role permissions affect all operational domains

---
## Domain 2: Patient Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Patient Identification: MRN (Medical Record Number) must be unique per tenant
- Demographic Data: Comprehensive international address support
- Consent Management: Explicit consent tracking for GDPR/HIPAA compliance
- Emergency Contacts: Multiple emergency contacts per patient
- Insurance: Support for multiple insurance policies with membership details
- Data Retention: Clinical data retention of 10+ years required

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Central repository for all patient demographic, contact, and administrative information.

Sub-modules:

- Patient Demographics
- Address Management
- Contact Information
- Emergency Contacts
- Insurance Memberships
- Consent Management
- Patient Identifiers

Entity List:

- patients
- patient_addresses
- patient_contacts
- patient_emergency_contacts
- patient_insurance_memberships
- patient_consents
- patient_identifiers

### 3. Entity Design

patients

- Purpose: Core patient demographic information
- Attributes:
    - patient_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK) - Multi-tenancy
    - mrn (VARCHAR(20)) - Medical Record Number (unique per tenant)
    - first_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - last_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - date_of_birth (DATE)
    - gender (SMALLINT) - 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other, 4=Unknown
    - marital_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Single, 2=Married, 3=Divorced, 4=Widowed
    - blood_group (blood_group_type) - Custom domain
    - nationality (UUID FK) - Reference to countries
    - ethnicity (VARCHAR(100))
    - preferred_language (VARCHAR(50))
    - is_deceased (BOOLEAN)
    - date_of_death (DATE)
    - cause_of_death (TEXT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

patient_addresses

- Purpose: Multiple addresses per patient (SCD Type 2 for history)
- Attributes:
    - address_id (UUID PK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - address_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Primary, 2=Secondary, 3=Temporary
    - address_line1 (VARCHAR(255))
    - address_line2 (VARCHAR(255))
    - city_id (UUID FK)
    - postal_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - is_current (BOOLEAN) - Current active address
    - valid_from (DATE)
    - valid_until (DATE)

patient_contacts

- Purpose: Multiple contact methods per patient
- Attributes:
    - contact_id (UUID PK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - contact_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Mobile, 2=Home Phone, 3=Work Phone, 4=Email
    - contact_value (VARCHAR(255))
    - is_primary (BOOLEAN)
    - is_verified (BOOLEAN)

patient_emergency_contacts

- Purpose: Emergency contact information
- Attributes:
    - emergency_contact_id (UUID PK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - first_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - last_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - relationship (VARCHAR(50)) - Spouse, Parent, Child, etc.
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - contact_email (email_type)
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Primary, 2=Secondary
    - is_verified (BOOLEAN)

patient_insurance_memberships

- Purpose: Insurance coverage details
- Attributes:
    - insurance_id (UUID PK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - insurance_provider (VARCHAR(255))
    - policy_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - group_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - effective_date (DATE)
    - expiration_date (DATE)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - verification_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Verified, 3=Rejected
    - copay_amount (currency_type)
    - deductible_amount (currency_type)
    - out_of_pocket_max (currency_type)

patient_consents

- Purpose: Consent management for treatment and data sharing
- Attributes:
    - consent_id (UUID PK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - consent_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Treatment, 2=Data Sharing, 3=Research
    - consent_version (VARCHAR(20))
    - granted (BOOLEAN)
    - granted_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - revoked_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - consent_text (TEXT) - Full consent text
    - witnessed_by (UUID FK) - User who witnessed consent

patient_identifiers

- Purpose: External identifiers (government IDs, etc.)
- Attributes:
    - identifier_id (UUID PK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - identifier_type (SMALLINT) - 1=National ID, 2=Passport, 3=Driver License
    - identifier_value (VARCHAR(100)) - Encrypted
    - issuing_country (UUID FK)
    - issue_date (DATE)
    - expiration_date (DATE)
    - is_verified (BOOLEAN)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- patients (1) → patient_addresses (N)
- patients (1) → patient_contacts (N)
- patients (1) → patient_emergency_contacts (N)
- patients (1) → patient_insurance_memberships (N)
- patients (1) → patient_consents (N)
- patients (1) → patient_identifiers (N)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for patient deletion (require soft delete)
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for patient_id updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure (tenants, countries, cities)
- Required by: ALL clinical domains (OPD, IPD, RX, Lab, etc.)

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with SCD Type 2 for addresses

- Separate tables for different entity types
- JSONB not used (structured relational data preferred)
- Lookup tables for enumerations

Data Types:

- Encrypted fields for sensitive data (identifiers)
- Custom domains for validation
- Arrays not used (separate tables for multiple values)

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- patients: Large (500,000-5,000,000 rows)
- patient_addresses: Large (1-2 addresses per patient)
- patient_contacts: Medium (2-3 contacts per patient)
- patient_insurance: Medium (1-2 policies per patient)

Indexing Strategy:

- Unique index on (tenant_id, mrn)
- Indexes on all foreign keys
- Partial indexes for active records
- Composite indexes for common searches (last_name, date_of_birth)

Partitioning:

- patients: Partition by created_at (quarterly)
- patient_addresses: Partition by patient_id range

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Read access: Clinical staff
- Write access: Registration staff
- Full access: Patient administration

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Additional patient-level privacy policies
- Consent-based access restrictions

Sensitive Data:

- National IDs: encrypted at rest
- Addresses: visible to authorized staff only
- Insurance: financial data protection

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10M+ patients
- International address formats
- Multiple consent versions tracking
- Insurance plan complexity increase

Cross-domain Impact:

- Foundation for all patient-related transactions
- Performance critical for patient lookup
- Data quality affects billing and clinical care
---
## Domain 3: RX Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- e-Prescribing: Digital signatures required for electronic prescriptions
- Controlled Substances: DEA/MCI number validation and witness requirements
- Refill Management: Strict enforcement of refill limits
- Prior Authorization: Required for high-cost drugs with approval tracking
- Medication Reconciliation: Required at admission, transfer, and discharge
- Polypharmacy: Alert triggered at ≥5 concurrent medications
- Drug Interactions: Real-time checking with override logging

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive electronic prescription management with safety controls and audit trails.

Sub-modules:

- e-Prescribing
- Prescription Items
- Refill Management
- Prior Authorization
- Medication History
- Drug Interaction Checking

Entity List:

- rx_prescriptions
- rx_prescription_items
- rx_refill_requests
- rx_prior_auth
- rx_medication_history
- rx_drug_interaction_alerts

### 3. Entity Design

rx_prescriptions

- Purpose: Master prescription record with digital signature
- Attributes:
    - prescription_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - rx_number (VARCHAR(30)) - Business identifier
    - prescription_type (SMALLINT) - 1=OPD, 2=IPD, 3=Emergency
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - encounter_id (UUID FK) - Links to clinical encounter
    - prescriber_id (UUID FK) - Licensed prescriber
    - prescribed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - valid_until (TIMESTAMPTZ) - Typically 30 days
    - is_electronic (BOOLEAN)
    - digital_signature_hash (BYTEA) - For e-prescriptions
    - signed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - signing_certificate_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - prescription_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Partially Dispensed, 3=Fully Dispensed, 4=Cancelled, 5=Expired
    - cancellation_reason (TEXT)
    - cancelled_by (UUID FK)
    - cancelled_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - max_refills_allowed (SMALLINT)
    - refills_used (SMALLINT)
    - clinical_notes (TEXT)
    - diagnosis_codes (TEXT[]) - ICD-10 codes
    - requires_prior_auth (BOOLEAN)
    - prior_auth_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - prior_auth_approved_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - is_reconciled (BOOLEAN)
    - reconciled_by (UUID FK)
    - reconciled_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - polypharmacy_flag (BOOLEAN) - Auto-set by trigger

rx_prescription_items

- Purpose: Individual drug line items
- Attributes:
    - item_id (UUID PK)
    - prescription_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_id (UUID FK) - Reference to drug master
    - drug_name (VARCHAR(255)) - Denormalized snapshot
    - drug_generic_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - drug_code (VARCHAR(50)) - ATC/NDC code
    - dose_amount (NUMERIC(10,3))
    - dose_unit (VARCHAR(30))
    - dose_form (VARCHAR(50))
    - route_of_admin (SMALLINT) - 1=Oral, 2=IV, 3=IM, 4=SC, 5=Topical, 6=Inhaled, 7=SL, 8=Rectal
    - frequency_code (VARCHAR(20)) - BD, TDS, OD, QID
    - frequency_per_day (SMALLINT)
    - duration_days (SMALLINT)
    - instructions (TEXT)
    - qty_prescribed (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - qty_dispensed (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - qty_remaining (NUMERIC(10,2)) - Generated column
    - is_controlled_substance (BOOLEAN)
    - controlled_substance_schedule (VARCHAR(10))
    - witness_user_id (UUID FK)
    - witness_signature_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - requires_prior_auth (BOOLEAN)
    - prior_auth_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied
    - item_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Dispensed, 3=Cancelled, 4=On Hold
    - substitution_allowed (BOOLEAN)

rx_refill_requests

- Purpose: Track refill requests against original prescriptions
- Attributes:
    - refill_id (UUID PK)
    - prescription_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - refill_number (SMALLINT) - Sequence number
    - requested_by (UUID FK) - Patient or pharmacist
    - requested_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - refill_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied, 4=Dispensed
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - denial_reason (TEXT)
    - valid_from (DATE)
    - valid_until (DATE)
    - clinical_notes (TEXT)

rx_prior_auth

- Purpose: Prior authorization requests and approvals
- Attributes:
    - prior_auth_id (UUID PK)
    - prescription_item_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - insurance_id (UUID FK) - Reference to patient insurance
    - request_date (DATE)
    - approval_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - approval_date (DATE)
    - expiration_date (DATE)
    - requested_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - approved_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied, 4=Partially Approved
    - denial_reason (TEXT)
    - submitted_by (UUID FK)
    - processed_by (UUID FK) - Insurance processor

rx_medication_history

- Purpose: Unified medication history across all encounters
- Attributes:
    - history_id (UUID PK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - prescription_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - drug_generic_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - dose_amount (NUMERIC(10,3))
    - dose_unit (VARCHAR(30))
    - route_of_admin (SMALLINT)
    - frequency_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - is_ongoing (BOOLEAN)
    - discontinuation_reason (TEXT)
    - prescribed_by (UUID FK)
    - source_system (VARCHAR(50)) - HMS or external EHR
    - reconciliation_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Unreconciled, 2=Reconciled

rx_drug_interaction_alerts

- Purpose: Drug interaction checking and override logging
- Attributes:
    - alert_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - prescription_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_a_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_a_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - drug_b_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_b_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - interaction_severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe
    - interaction_description (TEXT)
    - clinical_significance (TEXT)
    - alert_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Fired, 2=Overridden, 3=Prescription Modified
    - override_by (UUID FK)
    - override_reason (TEXT)
    - override_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - alerted_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- rx_prescriptions (1) → rx_prescription_items (N)
- rx_prescriptions (1) → rx_refill_requests (N)
- rx_prescription_items (1) → rx_prior_auth (N)
- patients (1) → rx_medication_history (N)
- patients (1) → rx_drug_interaction_alerts (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- rx_prescriptions (N) → patients (1)
- rx_prescriptions (N) → users (1) [prescriber]
- rx_refill_requests (N) → users (1) [requested_by]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: CASCADE for prescription items when prescription is deleted
- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for prescription deletion (soft delete preferred)
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for prescription_id updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Pharmacy (dispensing), Clinical domains (medication orders)

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with strategic denormalization

- Drug names denormalized for historical accuracy
- JSONB for diagnosis codes array
- Generated columns for calculated quantities

Data Types:

- NUMERIC for precise dosing calculations
- Custom domains for validation
- Arrays for diagnosis codes
- BYTEA for digital signatures

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- rx_prescriptions: Very Large (2M+ rows/year)
- rx_prescription_items: Very Large (3-5 items per prescription)
- rx_medication_history: Large (grows with patient lifetime)
- rx_drug_interaction_alerts: Medium (alert volume)

Indexing Strategy:

- Partition rx_prescriptions by prescribed_at (monthly)
- Index on (tenant_id, patient_id, prescription_status) for active meds
- Index on (tenant_id, prescriber_id) for doctor workload
- GIN index on diagnosis_codes array
- Partial indexes for pending approvals and refills

Partitioning:

- rx_prescriptions: Range partitioning by prescribed_at (monthly)
- rx_medication_history: Range partitioning by start_date (quarterly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Prescribers: Create/modify prescriptions
- Pharmacists: View and dispense
- Nurses: View medication orders
- Patients: View own prescriptions via portal

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient-level privacy policies
- Prescriber scope restrictions

Sensitive Data:

- Digital signatures protected
- Controlled substance tracking audited
- Drug interaction overrides logged

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10M+ prescriptions annually
- Real-time drug interaction checking
- Integration with external drug databases
- Advanced analytics on prescribing patterns

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for patient safety
- Integration with pharmacy dispensing
- Medication reconciliation across care continuum
- Billing and insurance claims

---
## Domain 4: Reagent Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- FEFO Enforcement: First Expiry First Out mandatory for all reagent usage
- QC Gate: Quality Control verification required before clinical use
- Westgard Rules: Automated QC rule violation detection and blocking
- Traceability: Every test result must trace back to specific reagent lot
- Temperature Control: Strict storage temperature monitoring required
- Calibration: Regular instrument calibration with validation
- Shelf-life: Both unopened and in-use stability tracking

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive management of laboratory reagents with full traceability, quality control, and compliance tracking.

Sub-modules:

- Reagent Master Catalog
- Lot/Batch Management
- Quality Control Testing
- Usage Tracking
- Calibration Management
- Inventory Control

Entity List:

- reagent_master
- reagent_lots
- reagent_qc_logs
- reagent_usage_logs
- reagent_calibration_logs
- reagent_inventory_transactions

### 3. Entity Design

reagent_master

- Purpose: Master catalog of all laboratory reagents
- Attributes:
    - reagent_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - reagent_code (VARCHAR(50)) - Unique identifier
    - reagent_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - reagent_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Assay Reagent, 2=QC Material, 3=Calibrator, 4=Diluent, 5=Wash Solution
    - analyzer_id (UUID FK) - Reference to lab equipment
    - analyzer_compatible (TEXT[]) - Compatible instrument models
    - test_ids (UUID[]) - Tests using this reagent
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30)) - ml, test-count, kit, etc.
    - reorder_level (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reorder_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - lead_time_days (SMALLINT)
    - storage_temperature_min (temperature_c_type)
    - storage_temperature_max (temperature_c_type)
    - requires_refrigeration (BOOLEAN)
    - requires_dark_storage (BOOLEAN)
    - hazmat_classification (VARCHAR(50)) - Biohazard/chemical classification
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

reagent_lots

- Purpose: Individual reagent lots with expiry and QC tracking
- Attributes:
    - lot_id (UUID PK)
    - reagent_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - lot_number (VARCHAR(100)) - Manufacturer lot number
    - manufacturer_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - supplier_id (UUID FK) - Procurement supplier
    - manufacture_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - received_date (DATE)
    - opened_date (DATE)
    - open_stability_days (SMALLINT) - Stability after opening
    - in_use_expiry_date (DATE) - Generated: opened_date + stability_days
    - effective_expiry_date (DATE) - Generated: LEAST(expiry_date, in_use_expiry_date)
    - quantity_received (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - quantity_available (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30))
    - qc_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending QC, 2=QC Passed, 3=QC Failed, 4=Released for Use
    - qc_verified_by (UUID FK)
    - qc_verified_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - qc_failure_reason (TEXT)
    - lot_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Quarantine, 2=Released, 3=In Use, 4=Exhausted, 5=Recalled
    - recall_reason (TEXT)
    - discarded_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - discarded_by (UUID FK)
    - storage_location (VARCHAR(100))
    - refrigerator_id (UUID FK) - Storage equipment
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - currency_code (VARCHAR(3))

reagent_qc_logs

- Purpose: Quality control testing and Westgard rules evaluation
- Attributes:
    - qc_log_id (UUID PK)
    - lot_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - analyzer_id (UUID FK)
    - qc_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Level 1 (Low), 2=Level 2 (Normal), 3=Level 3 (High)
    - measured_value (NUMERIC(15,6))
    - unit (VARCHAR(30))
    - mean_value (NUMERIC(15,6))
    - sd_value (NUMERIC(15,6))
    - cv_percent (percentage_type) - Generated: (sd/mean)*100
    - westgard_1_2s (BOOLEAN) - Warning rule
    - westgard_1_3s (BOOLEAN) - Rejection rule
    - westgard_2_2s (BOOLEAN) - Rejection rule
    - westgard_r_4s (BOOLEAN) - Rejection rule
    - westgard_4_1s (BOOLEAN) - Warning rule
    - westgard_10x (BOOLEAN) - Rejection rule
    - qc_passed (BOOLEAN)
    - failure_rules (TEXT[]) - Which rules fired
    - action_taken (TEXT)
    - clinical_use_blocked (BOOLEAN)
    - blocked_until (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - unblocked_by (UUID FK)
    - unblocked_reason (TEXT)
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - performed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

reagent_usage_logs

- Purpose: Trace reagent consumption to specific test results
- Attributes:
    - usage_id (UUID PK)
    - lot_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - lab_result_id (UUID FK) - Trace to specific result
    - lab_order_id (UUID FK)
    - analyzer_id (UUID FK)
    - quantity_used (NUMERIC(10,4))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30))
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - total_cost (currency_type) - Generated: quantity_used * unit_cost
    - used_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - used_by (UUID FK)

reagent_calibration_logs

- Purpose: Instrument calibration events and validation
- Attributes:
    - calibration_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - analyzer_id (UUID FK)
    - calibrator_lot_id (UUID FK) - Reference to reagent lot
    - calibration_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Daily startup, 2=New lot verification, 3=Corrective
    - calibration_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Passed, 2=Failed
    - failure_reason (TEXT)
    - corrective_action (TEXT)
    - analyzer_held (BOOLEAN) - Clinical use blocked
    - held_until (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - performed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - valid_until (TIMESTAMPTZ) - Calibration validity period

reagent_inventory_transactions

- Purpose: Inventory movements and adjustments
- Attributes:
    - transaction_id (UUID PK)
    - lot_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - transaction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Receipt, 2=Issue, 3=Adjustment, 4=Wastage
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30))
    - reason (TEXT) - For adjustments/wastage
    - reference_number (VARCHAR(100)) - PO number, etc.
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - performed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- reagent_master (1) → reagent_lots (N)
- reagent_lots (1) → reagent_qc_logs (N)
- reagent_lots (1) → reagent_usage_logs (N)
- reagent_lots (1) → reagent_inventory_transactions (N)
- analyzer (1) → reagent_calibration_logs (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- reagent_usage_logs (N) → lab_results (1) [traceability]
- reagent_calibration_logs (N) → reagent_lots (1) [calibrator]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for master records
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for lot updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure, Inventory Management
- Required by: Laboratory (test processing)
- Integrates with: Procurement, Asset Management

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Effective expiry date calculated from multiple factors
- Cost calculations generated for reporting
- Arrays for compatible analyzers and test references

Data Types:

- NUMERIC(15,6) for precise QC measurements
- Custom domains for temperature and percentages
- Arrays for Westgard rule tracking
- Generated columns for calculated values

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- reagent_master: Medium (5,000-50,000 rows)
- reagent_lots: Medium (10,000-100,000 rows)
- reagent_usage_logs: Very Large (millions of rows annually)
- reagent_qc_logs: Large (daily QC per instrument)

Indexing Strategy:

- Unique index on (tenant_id, branch_id, reagent_code)
- Index on reagent_lots (effective_expiry_date ASC) for FEFO
- GIN index on analyzer_compatible array
- Partial indexes for QC status and lot status
- BRIN index on reagent_usage_logs by used_at

Partitioning:

- reagent_usage_logs: Range partitioning by used_at (monthly)
- reagent_qc_logs: Range partitioning by performed_at (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Lab Technicians: Perform QC and usage logging
- Lab Managers: Approve QC results and releases
- Procurement: View inventory levels
- Quality Assurance: Audit access

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Branch-level restrictions where applicable
- Read-only access for some roles

Sensitive Data:

- Cost information restricted to authorized roles
- QC failure details protected
- Supplier pricing confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 1000+ reagent types
- 100+ analyzers across network
- Real-time QC monitoring
- Integration with automated analyzers

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for laboratory test accuracy
- Direct impact on patient results quality
- Inventory and cost management integration
- Regulatory compliance (ISO 15189)

---
## Domain 5: Kitchen / Nutrition Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Diet Orders: Must be linked to active IPD admissions only
- Allergen Control: Real-time allergen checking against patient allergies at meal delivery
- NBM Enforcement: Nil-by-mouth orders must physically block meal delivery
- Doctor Approval: Therapeutic diets require physician authorization
- Nutritional Analysis: Required for all menu items and meal plans
- Meal Confirmation: Electronic or manual delivery confirmation mandatory
- Texture Modifications: Support for pureed, minced, normal textures

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive patient nutrition management with allergy safety, therapeutic diet support, and meal delivery tracking.

Sub-modules:

- Menu Management
- Diet Type Catalog
- Patient Diet Orders
- Meal Planning
- Meal Delivery & Confirmation
- Kitchen Inventory
- Allergen Management

Entity List:

- menu_categories
- menu_items
- menu_item_allergens
- diet_types
- diet_orders
- meal_plans
- meal_deliveries
- kitchen_stock
- kitchen_stock_transactions

### 3. Entity Design

menu_categories

- Purpose: Organize menu items into logical groups
- Attributes:
    - category_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - category_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Breakfast, Lunch, Dinner, Snacks, Therapeutic
    - serving_time_start (TIME) - Default serving time
    - serving_time_end (TIME)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - display_order (SMALLINT)

menu_items

- Purpose: Master catalog of all food items with nutritional information
- Attributes:
    - item_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - category_id (UUID FK)
    - item_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - item_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - description (TEXT)
    - portion_size (VARCHAR(100))
    - calories (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - protein_g (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - carbs_g (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - fat_g (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - fiber_g (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - sodium_mg (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - sugar_g (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - is_vegetarian (BOOLEAN)
    - is_vegan (BOOLEAN)
    - is_gluten_free (BOOLEAN)
    - is_halal (BOOLEAN)
    - is_kosher (BOOLEAN)
    - preparation_time_min (SMALLINT)
    - holding_temp_min (temperature_c_type)
    - holding_temp_max (temperature_c_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

menu_item_allergens

- Purpose: Junction table for menu item allergens (M:N relationship)
- Attributes:
    - item_allergen_id (UUID PK)
    - menu_item_id (UUID FK)
    - allergen_code (VARCHAR(20)) - Reference to allergen master
    - severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Contains, 2=May Contain, 3=Processed in same facility

diet_types

- Purpose: Therapeutic diet definitions and restrictions
- Attributes:
    - diet_type_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - diet_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - diet_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Diabetic, Renal, Cardiac, Low-Sodium, etc.
    - description (TEXT)
    - calorie_range_min (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - calorie_range_max (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - protein_range_min (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - protein_range_max (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - sodium_range_max (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - fluid_restriction_max_ml (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - allowed_allergens (TEXT[]) - Allergens permitted
    - restricted_allergens (TEXT[]) - Allergens prohibited
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

diet_orders

- Purpose: Patient-specific diet prescriptions
- Attributes:
    - diet_order_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK) - Must be active IPD admission
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - diet_type_id (UUID FK)
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - is_nil_by_mouth (BOOLEAN) - Critical safety flag
    - texture_modification (SMALLINT) - 1=Normal, 2=Minced, 3=Pureed, 4=Liquid
    - fluid_restriction_ml (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - special_instructions (TEXT)
    - doctor_approved_by (UUID FK)
    - doctor_approved_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - allergen_check_passed (BOOLEAN) - Computed from patient allergies
    - allergen_checked_by (UUID FK)
    - allergen_checked_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - order_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=On Hold, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled

meal_plans

- Purpose: Daily/weekly meal assignments per diet order
- Attributes:
    - meal_plan_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - diet_order_id (UUID FK)
    - plan_date (DATE)
    - meal_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Breakfast, 2=Lunch, 3=Dinner, 4=Snack
    - menu_item_id (UUID FK)
    - planned_quantity (SMALLINT)
    - actual_quantity (SMALLINT) - Served quantity
    - planned_calories (NUMERIC(6,1)) - Generated from menu item
    - planned_protein (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - planned_carbs (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - planned_fat (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - is_served (BOOLEAN)
    - served_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

meal_deliveries

- Purpose: Actual meal delivery tracking with confirmation
- Attributes:
    - delivery_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - diet_order_id (UUID FK)
    - meal_plan_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - delivered_by (UUID FK) - Kitchen staff
    - delivered_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - received_by (UUID FK) - Nurse or patient
    - received_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - temperature_at_delivery (temperature_c_type)
    - is_accepted (BOOLEAN)
    - rejection_reason (TEXT)
    - delivery_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Prepared, 2=In Transit, 3=Delivered, 4=Rejected

kitchen_stock

- Purpose: Raw material inventory for kitchen
- Attributes:
    - stock_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - ingredient_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - ingredient_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30))
    - current_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reorder_level (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reorder_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - storage_temperature_min (temperature_c_type)
    - storage_temperature_max (temperature_c_type)
    - shelf_life_days (SMALLINT)
    - is_perishable (BOOLEAN)

kitchen_stock_transactions

- Purpose: Inventory movements and consumption
- Attributes:
    - transaction_id (UUID PK)
    - stock_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - transaction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Receipt, 2=Consumption, 3=Adjustment, 4=Wastage
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30))
    - reference_id (UUID) - Link to meal plan or PO
    - transaction_date (DATE)
    - performed_by (UUID FK)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- menu_categories (1) → menu_items (N)
- diet_types (1) → diet_orders (N)
- admissions (1) → diet_orders (N)
- diet_orders (1) → meal_plans (N)
- diet_orders (1) → meal_deliveries (N)
- kitchen_stock (1) → kitchen_stock_transactions (N)

N:N Relationships:

- menu_items ↔ allergens (via menu_item_allergens junction table)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active diet orders
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for menu item updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management (admissions, allergies), Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Clinical care (nutrition support)
- Integrates with: Inventory Management

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with strategic denormalization

- Nutritional values denormalized for historical accuracy
- Arrays for allergen restrictions
- Generated columns for nutritional calculations

Data Types:

- NUMERIC for precise nutritional values
- Custom domains for temperature
- Arrays for allergen tracking
- Boolean flags for dietary restrictions

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- menu_items: Medium (500-2,000 rows)
- diet_orders: Medium (concurrent IPD patients)
- meal_deliveries: Large (3 meals/day × IPD patients)
- meal_plans: Large (daily planning for all patients)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on diet_orders (admission_id, is_active) for active orders
- Index on meal_deliveries (delivered_at) for delivery tracking
- Partial indexes for active records and NBM status
- GIN index on allergen arrays

Partitioning:

- meal_deliveries: Range partitioning by delivered_at (monthly)
- meal_plans: Range partitioning by plan_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Dietitians: Create diet orders and meal plans
- Kitchen Staff: Prepare and deliver meals
- Nurses: Confirm meal delivery and acceptance
- Doctors: Approve therapeutic diets

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Branch-level kitchen separation
- Patient privacy for diet information

Sensitive Data:

- Patient dietary restrictions protected
- Allergy information highly sensitive
- Nutritional health data confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 1000+ concurrent IPD patients
- Multiple kitchen locations per branch
- Advanced nutritional analytics
- Integration with patient monitoring systems

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for patient recovery and health
- Allergy safety directly impacts patient safety
- Integration with clinical care processes
- Inventory management for food supplies

## Domain 6: Canteen Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- POS Operations: Multiple canteen outlets with point-of-sale systems
- Shift Balancing: Daily cash balancing with variance tracking
- Staff Subsidies: Meal entitlements linked to HR payroll
- Stock Management: Separate from main inventory with expiry control
- Vendor Management: Supplier invoicing with GRN matching
- Expiry Enforcement: Block issuance of expired food items
- Daily Closing: End-of-shift settlement with denomination counting

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive staff and visitor food service management with financial controls and inventory tracking.

Sub-modules:

- Canteen Outlet Management
- Menu & Pricing
- Point-of-Sale Transactions
- Payment Processing
- Shift Settlement
- Stock Management
- Vendor & Invoice Management
- Staff Subsidy Management

Entity List:

- canteen_outlets
- canteen_menus
- canteen_menu_categories
- canteen_orders
- canteen_order_items
- canteen_payments
- canteen_shift_closings
- canteen_stock
- canteen_stock_transactions
- canteen_vendor_invoices
- canteen_staff_subsidies

### 3. Entity Design

canteen_outlets

- Purpose: Physical canteen locations and configurations
- Attributes:
    - outlet_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - outlet_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - outlet_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - location (VARCHAR(255))
    - outlet_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Staff Cafeteria, 2=Visitor Cafe, 3=Patient Family
    - operating_hours_start (TIME)
    - operating_hours_end (TIME)
    - is_24_hours (BOOLEAN)
    - pos_terminal_count (SMALLINT)
    - manager_id (UUID FK) - Staff manager
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

canteen_menus

- Purpose: Menu items with pricing and availability
- Attributes:
    - menu_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - outlet_id (UUID FK)
    - item_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - item_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - description (TEXT)
    - category_id (UUID FK)
    - base_price (currency_type)
    - selling_price (currency_type)
    - cost_price (currency_type)
    - tax_rate (percentage_type)
    - is_available (BOOLEAN)
    - preparation_time_min (SMALLINT)
    - is_vegetarian (BOOLEAN)
    - is_vegan (BOOLEAN)
    - contains_allergens (TEXT[]) - Major allergens present

canteen_menu_categories

- Purpose: Menu item categorization
- Attributes:
    - category_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - category_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Hot Food, Beverages, Bakery, etc.
    - display_order (SMALLINT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

canteen_orders

- Purpose: POS transaction master records
- Attributes:
    - order_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - outlet_id (UUID FK)
    - order_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Sequential business number
    - order_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Dine-in, 2=Takeaway, 3=Delivery
    - customer_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Staff, 2=Visitor, 3=Patient Family
    - staff_id (UUID FK) - If staff member
    - visitor_id (UUID FK) - If registered visitor
    - order_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - total_items (SMALLINT)
    - subtotal_amount (currency_type)
    - tax_amount (currency_type)
    - discount_amount (currency_type)
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - order_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Placed, 2=Preparing, 3=Ready, 4=Completed, 5=Cancelled
    - served_by (UUID FK) - Cashier/staff
    - completed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

canteen_order_items

- Purpose: Individual line items within orders
- Attributes:
    - order_item_id (UUID PK)
    - order_id (UUID FK)
    - menu_id (UUID FK)
    - quantity (SMALLINT)
    - unit_price (currency_type)
    - item_total (currency_type) - Generated: quantity * unit_price
    - tax_amount (currency_type)
    - discount_amount (currency_type)
    - net_amount (currency_type)
    - special_instructions (TEXT)
    - is_ready (BOOLEAN)
    - ready_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

canteen_payments

- Purpose: Payment settlement per order
- Attributes:
    - payment_id (UUID PK)
    - order_id (UUID FK)
    - payment_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Cash, 2=Card, 3=Wallet, 4=Subsidy
    - payment_amount (currency_type)
    - card_last4 (VARCHAR(4)) - If card payment
    - card_type (VARCHAR(20))
    - wallet_reference (VARCHAR(100))
    - subsidy_amount (currency_type) - Staff subsidy portion
    - cash_tendered (currency_type) - For cash payments
    - change_given (currency_type)
    - payment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Completed, 3=Failed, 4=Refunded
    - processed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - processed_by (UUID FK)

canteen_shift_closings

- Purpose: End-of-shift financial reconciliation
- Attributes:
    - closing_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - outlet_id (UUID FK)
    - shift_date (DATE)
    - shift_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Morning, 2=Afternoon, 3=Night
    - opening_cash_balance (currency_type)
    - total_cash_sales (currency_type)
    - total_card_sales (currency_type)
    - total_wallet_sales (currency_type)
    - total_subsidy_sales (currency_type)
    - total_sales (currency_type) - Generated sum
    - expected_cash_in_hand (currency_type) - Generated: opening_balance + cash_sales
    - actual_cash_in_hand (currency_type)
    - cash_variance (currency_type) - Generated: actual - expected
    - denomination_breakdown (JSONB) - Cash count by denomination
    - closed_by (UUID FK)
    - closed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Open, 2=Pending Verification, 3=Closed, 4=Discrepancy

canteen_stock

- Purpose: Canteen-specific inventory
- Attributes:
    - stock_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - outlet_id (UUID FK)
    - item_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - item_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30))
    - current_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reorder_level (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reorder_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - storage_location (VARCHAR(100))
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

canteen_stock_transactions

- Purpose: Inventory movements and consumption
- Attributes:
    - transaction_id (UUID PK)
    - stock_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - outlet_id (UUID FK)
    - transaction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Receipt, 2=Issue, 3=Adjustment, 4=Wastage
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reference_number (VARCHAR(100)) - PO, Order, etc.
    - reason (TEXT) - For adjustments/wastage
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - performed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

canteen_vendor_invoices

- Purpose: Supplier invoicing and GRN matching
- Attributes:
    - invoice_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - invoice_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - invoice_date (DATE)
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - tax_amount (currency_type)
    - grn_reference (VARCHAR(100)) - Goods Received Note
    - payment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Partially Paid, 3=Paid
    - due_date (DATE)
    - paid_amount (currency_type)
    - paid_date (DATE)

canteen_staff_subsidies

- Purpose: Staff meal entitlement management
- Attributes:
    - subsidy_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - staff_id (UUID FK)
    - subsidy_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Daily, 2=Monthly, 3=Per Meal
    - entitlement_amount (currency_type)
    - used_amount (currency_type)
    - remaining_amount (currency_type) - Generated: entitlement - used
    - cycle_start_date (DATE)
    - cycle_end_date (DATE)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- canteen_outlets (1) → canteen_menus (N)
- canteen_outlets (1) → canteen_orders (N)
- canteen_orders (1) → canteen_order_items (N)
- canteen_orders (1) → canteen_payments (N)
- canteen_outlets (1) → canteen_shift_closings (N)
- canteen_outlets (1) → canteen_stock (N)
- canteen_stock (1) → canteen_stock_transactions (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- canteen_orders (N) → users (1) [staff]
- canteen_payments (N) → canteen_staff_subsidies (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active transactions
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for menu price updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure, HR Management
- Required by: Staff operations
- Integrates with: Procurement, Financial systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Financial calculations generated for accuracy
- JSONB for denomination breakdowns
- Arrays for allergen tracking

Data Types:

- currency_type for all financial amounts
- Custom domains for percentages
- JSONB for flexible data structures
- Generated columns for balance calculations

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- canteen_orders: Very Large (1M+ transactions/year)
- canteen_order_items: Very Large (2-3 items per order)
- canteen_payments: Large (1:1 with orders)
- canteen_shift_closings: Small (3 shifts/day × outlets)

Indexing Strategy:

- Partition canteen_orders by order_datetime (monthly)
- Index on canteen_orders (staff_id, order_datetime) for staff reports
- Index on canteen_payments (payment_method) for settlement
- Partial indexes for active stock and open shifts

Partitioning:

- canteen_orders: Range partitioning by order_datetime (monthly)
- canteen_stock_transactions: Range partitioning by performed_at (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Cashiers: Process orders and payments
- Managers: Oversee shifts and settlements
- Kitchen Staff: Update item availability
- Finance: Review settlements and invoices

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Outlet-level restrictions for staff
- Financial data access controls

Sensitive Data:

- Payment card information protected
- Staff subsidy details confidential
- Financial settlement data restricted

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100+ canteen outlets
- 10,000+ daily transactions
- Multiple payment gateway integrations
- Advanced analytics on sales patterns

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for staff welfare and operations
- Financial reconciliation important for accounting
- Inventory management for food cost control
- HR integration for staff benefits

## Domain 7: Laundry Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Item Tracking: Every linen item must have barcode/RFID for traceability
- Biohazard Separation: Infected/soiled linen requires separate workflow with SOP compliance
- SLA Monitoring: 24-hour turnaround time with automatic escalation on breach
- QC Mandatory: Quality inspection required after every wash cycle
- Vendor Integration: External laundry service support with billing reconciliation
- Inventory Accuracy: Item count issued must match returned count
- Infection Control: Biohazard protocols strictly enforced

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive hospital linen management with infection control, quality assurance, and vendor management.

Sub-modules:

- Linen Type Catalog
- Item-level Tracking
- Collection & Batch Management
- Wash Cycle Processing
- Quality Control
- Vendor Management
- SLA Monitoring

Entity List:

- laundry_item_types
- laundry_items
- laundry_collection_logs
- laundry_batches
- laundry_batch_items
- laundry_vendors
- laundry_vendor_trips
- laundry_qc_checks
- laundry_sla_breaches

### 3. Entity Design

laundry_item_types

- Purpose: Master catalog of linen types and specifications
- Attributes:
    - item_type_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - type_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - type_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Bed sheet, pillow cover, scrubs, OT gown, towel, etc.
    - description (TEXT)
    - standard_weight_grams (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - expected_lifecycle_washes (SMALLINT)
    - wash_temperature_min (temperature_c_type)
    - wash_temperature_max (temperature_c_type)
    - drying_temperature_min (temperature_c_type)
    - drying_temperature_max (temperature_c_type)
    - is_reusable (BOOLEAN)
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - replacement_cost (currency_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

laundry_items

- Purpose: Individual trackable linen items with barcode/RFID
- Attributes:
    - item_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - item_type_id (UUID FK)
    - barcode (VARCHAR(100)) - Unique identifier
    - rfid_tag (VARCHAR(100)) - Alternative tracking
    - purchase_date (DATE)
    - purchase_cost (currency_type)
    - total_washes (SMALLINT) - Lifetime wash count
    - current_condition (SMALLINT) - 1=New, 2=Good, 3=Fair, 4=Poor, 5=Retired
    - last_wash_date (DATE)
    - last_used_date (DATE)
    - current_location (SMALLINT) - 1=In Use, 2=Soiled, 3=In Laundry, 4=Quarantine
    - assigned_to_department (UUID FK)
    - assigned_to_room (VARCHAR(50))
    - is_retired (BOOLEAN)
    - retirement_reason (TEXT)
    - retired_date (DATE)

laundry_collection_logs

- Purpose: Tracking soiled linen collection from wards/OT
- Attributes:
    - collection_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK) - Source department
    - collected_by (UUID FK) - Laundry staff
    - collection_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - total_items_collected (SMALLINT)
    - biohazard_items_count (SMALLINT)
    - collection_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Emergency, 3=Biohazard
    - is_biohazard (BOOLEAN)
    - biohazard_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Blood, bodily fluids, infectious
    - sop_reference (VARCHAR(100)) - Biohazard handling SOP
    - notes (TEXT)

laundry_batches

- Purpose: Wash batch management with SLA tracking
- Attributes:
    - batch_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - batch_number (VARCHAR(50)) - Sequential business number
    - batch_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Normal, 2=Infected/Biohazard (CRITICAL separation)
    - is_biohazard (BOOLEAN)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK) - External laundry service
    - wash_started_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - wash_completed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - collection_timestamp (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - expected_return_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_return_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - sla_hours (SMALLINT) - Contractual SLA (e.g., 24 hours)
    - sla_breach_threshold_minutes (INTEGER)
    - sla_breached (BOOLEAN) - Generated: actual_return_at > expected_return_at
    - qc_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Passed, 3=Failed, 4=Rewash Required
    - total_items_sent (SMALLINT)
    - total_items_returned (SMALLINT)
    - items_missing_count (SMALLINT) - Generated: total_items_sent - total_items_returned
    - batch_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Collected, 2=Sent to Vendor, 3=Washing, 4=QC, 5=Returned
    - wash_temperature (temperature_c_type)
    - drying_temperature (temperature_c_type)
    - detergent_used (VARCHAR(100))
    - disinfectant_used (VARCHAR(100))

laundry_batch_items

- Purpose: Items included in each wash batch
- Attributes:
    - batch_item_id (UUID PK)
    - batch_id (UUID FK)
    - item_id (UUID FK)
    - item_condition_pre (SMALLINT) - Condition before wash
    - item_condition_post (SMALLINT) - Condition after wash
    - wash_count_increment (SMALLINT) - Typically 1
    - requires_repair (BOOLEAN)
    - repair_notes (TEXT)
    - is_missing (BOOLEAN)
    - missing_reason (TEXT)

laundry_vendors

- Purpose: External laundry service providers
- Attributes:
    - vendor_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - vendor_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - contact_person (VARCHAR(100))
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - contact_email (email_type)
    - address (TEXT)
    - service_contract_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - contract_start_date (DATE)
    - contract_end_date (DATE)
    - sla_agreed_hours (SMALLINT)
    - quality_rating (percentage_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

laundry_vendor_trips

- Purpose: Vendor pickup and delivery logs
- Attributes:
    - trip_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - trip_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Pickup, 2=Delivery
    - scheduled_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - driver_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - vehicle_number (VARCHAR(20))
    - batches_count (SMALLINT)
    - items_count (SMALLINT)
    - trip_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Delayed
    - delay_reason (TEXT)
    - received_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)

laundry_qc_checks

- Purpose: Post-wash quality inspection
- Attributes:
    - qc_id (UUID PK)
    - batch_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - inspected_by (UUID FK)
    - inspection_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - total_items_inspected (SMALLINT)
    - items_passed (SMALLINT)
    - items_failed (SMALLINT)
    - failure_reasons (TEXT[]) - Stains, damage, odor, etc.
    - overall_quality_score (percentage_type)
    - requires_rewash (BOOLEAN)
    - rewash_reason (TEXT)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

laundry_sla_breaches

- Purpose: SLA violation tracking and escalation
- Attributes:
    - breach_id (UUID PK)
    - batch_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - breach_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Time, 2=Quality, 3=Quantity
    - breach_minutes (INTEGER)
    - breach_reason (TEXT)
    - escalation_level (SMALLINT) - 1=First, 2=Second, 3=Final
    - action_taken (TEXT)
    - resolved (BOOLEAN)
    - resolved_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - resolved_by (UUID FK)
    - created_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- laundry_item_types (1) → laundry_items (N)
- laundry_vendors (1) → laundry_batches (N)
- laundry_batches (1) → laundry_batch_items (N)
- laundry_batches (1) → laundry_qc_checks (N)
- laundry_batches (1) → laundry_sla_breaches (N)
- laundry_vendors (1) → laundry_vendor_trips (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- laundry_items (N) → departments (1) [assignment]
- laundry_collection_logs (N) → departments (1) [source]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active batches
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for batch status updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure, Asset Management
- Required by: IPD (bed management), OT (surgical linen)
- Integrates with: Vendor Management, Quality Assurance

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- SLA breach detection automated
- Item counts calculated
- Condition tracking with history

Data Types:

- Custom domains for temperature
- Arrays for failure reasons
- Boolean flags for critical statuses
- Generated columns for SLA calculations

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- laundry_items: Large (100,000+ trackable items)
- laundry_batches: Medium (50-100 batches/day)
- laundry_batch_items: Large (100-200 items/batch)
- laundry_qc_checks: Medium (1 per batch)

Indexing Strategy:

- Unique index on laundry_items (barcode) for quick scanning
- Index on laundry_batches (expected_return_at) for SLA monitoring
- Partial indexes for active items and open batches
- GIN index on failure_reasons array

Partitioning:

- laundry_batch_items: Range partitioning by batch creation date
- laundry_collection_logs: Range partitioning by collection_datetime

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Laundry Staff: Item collection and processing
- QC Inspectors: Quality checks
- Managers: SLA monitoring and vendor management
- Infection Control: Biohazard oversight

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Department-level restrictions for item assignment
- Biohazard data access controls

Sensitive Data:

- Biohazard information protected
- Vendor contract details confidential
- Quality performance data restricted

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 500,000+ trackable items
- Multiple vendor management
- Real-time RFID tracking integration
- Advanced predictive maintenance

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for infection control compliance
- Direct impact on patient safety
- Operational efficiency for bed turnover
- Vendor relationship management

## Domain 8: Blood Bank

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- TTI Testing: Mandatory HIV, HBsAg, HCV, VDRL, Malaria testing before release
- Crossmatch Required: ABO/Rh typing and antibody screening before transfusion
- Cold Chain: Continuous temperature monitoring required for all blood products
- Unit Uniqueness: Each blood unit can only be issued once
- Expiry Enforcement: Automatic blocking of expired units
- Donor Eligibility: Strict donor screening with inter-donation interval enforcement
- Transfusion Reaction: Mandatory reporting and investigation

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive blood product management from donation to transfusion with full safety controls and traceability.

Sub-modules:

- Donor Management
- Blood Donation
- Blood Component Processing
- TTI Testing
- Inventory Management
- Crossmatch & Issuance
- Transfusion Administration
- Temperature Monitoring

Entity List:

- blood_donor_master
- blood_donations
- blood_units
- blood_unit_components
- blood_tti_tests
- blood_crossmatches
- blood_requests
- blood_issuances
- blood_transfusions
- transfusion_reactions
- blood_temperature_logs
- blood_discards

### 3. Entity Design

blood_donor_master

- Purpose: Donor demographic and eligibility information
- Attributes:
    - donor_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - donor_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Unique identifier
    - first_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - last_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - date_of_birth (DATE)
    - gender (SMALLINT) - 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other
    - blood_group (blood_group_type) - ABO/Rh
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - contact_email (email_type)
    - address (TEXT)
    - nationality (UUID FK)
    - donor_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Voluntary, 2=Replacement, 3=Paid
    - total_donations (SMALLINT)
    - last_donation_date (DATE)
    - next_eligible_date (DATE) - Generated: last_donation + inter-donation interval
    - is_active_donor (BOOLEAN)
    - permanent_deferral (BOOLEAN)
    - deferral_reason (TEXT)
    - emergency_contact (VARCHAR(255))
    - emergency_phone (phone_type)

blood_donations

- Purpose: Individual donation events
- Attributes:
    - donation_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - donor_id (UUID FK)
    - donation_number (VARCHAR(20))
    - donation_date (DATE)
    - donation_time (TIME)
    - donation_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Whole Blood, 2=Apheresis
    - collection_volume_ml (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - collection_site (VARCHAR(100))
    - phlebotomist_id (UUID FK)
    - donor_weight_kg (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - donor_hemoglobin (NUMERIC(4,1))
    - donor_bp_systolic (SMALLINT)
    - donor_bp_diastolic (SMALLINT)
    - donor_temperature (temperature_c_type)
    - eligibility_questions (JSONB) - Donor screening questionnaire
    - donation_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Completed, 2=Deferred, 3=Stopped
    - deferral_reason (TEXT)

blood_units

- Purpose: Blood component inventory management
- Attributes:
    - unit_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - donation_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_number (VARCHAR(50)) - Unique barcode
    - component_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Whole Blood, 2=PRBC, 3=FFP, 4=Platelets, 5=Cryo, 6=Granulocytes
    - blood_group (blood_group_type) - Tested ABO/Rh
    - volume_ml (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - collection_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE) - Varies by component type
    - storage_temp_min (temperature_c_type)
    - storage_temp_max (temperature_c_type)
    - tti_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Negative/Passed, 3=Positive/Rejected
    - unit_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Quarantine, 2=Available, 3=Reserved, 4=Issued, 5=Transfused, 6=Discarded, 7=Expired
    - is_voluntary (BOOLEAN)
    - issued_to_patient_id (UUID FK)
    - issued_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - issued_by (UUID FK)
    - transfusion_id (UUID FK) - After transfusion
    - current_location (VARCHAR(100)) - Refrigerator, ward, etc.
    - special_testing (TEXT[]) - Additional tests performed

blood_unit_components

- Purpose: Component separation from single donation
- Attributes:
    - component_id (UUID PK)
    - donation_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - component_type (SMALLINT)
    - volume_ml (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - separation_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - separated_by (UUID FK)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - unit_id (UUID FK) - Reference to blood_units

blood_tti_tests

- Purpose: Transfusion Transmissible Infection testing
- Attributes:
    - tti_id (UUID PK)
    - donation_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - test_type (SMALLINT) - 1=HIV, 2=HBsAg, 3=HCV, 4=VDRL, 5=Malaria
    - test_method (VARCHAR(100))
    - test_kit_lot (VARCHAR(100))
    - test_date (DATE)
    - test_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Negative, 2=Positive, 3=Indeterminate
    - result_value (VARCHAR(100)) - Quantitative if applicable
    - tested_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

blood_crossmatches

- Purpose: Pre-transfusion compatibility testing
- Attributes:
    - crossmatch_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - request_id (UUID FK)
    - abo_rh_compatible (BOOLEAN)
    - antibody_screen_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Negative, 2=Positive
    - crossmatch_method (VARCHAR(100)) - Immediate spin, AHG, etc.
    - crossmatch_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Compatible, 2=Incompatible
    - incompatibility_reason (TEXT)
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - performed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - valid_until (TIMESTAMPTZ) - Typically 72 hours
    - is_emergency (BOOLEAN) - Emergency release without full crossmatch

blood_requests

- Purpose: Transfusion requests from clinical teams
- Attributes:
    - request_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - requested_by (UUID FK)
    - requested_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=Emergency
    - component_type (SMALLINT)
    - quantity_units (SMALLINT)
    - blood_group (blood_group_type)
    - clinical_indication (TEXT)
    - hemoglobin_level (NUMERIC(4,1))
    - platelet_count (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - request_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Crossmatched, 3=Issued, 4=Cancelled
    - cancelled_reason (TEXT)

blood_issuances

- Purpose: Blood unit issuance from blood bank
- Attributes:
    - issuance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_id (UUID FK)
    - request_id (UUID FK)
    - issued_by (UUID FK)
    - issued_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - received_by (UUID FK) - Ward staff
    - received_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - transport_temp (temperature_c_type)
    - issue_notes (TEXT)

blood_transfusions

- Purpose: Bedside transfusion administration
- Attributes:
    - transfusion_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - started_by (UUID FK)
    - started_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - completed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - volume_transfused_ml (NUMERIC(6,1))
    - transfusion_duration_min (SMALLINT)
    - vital_signs_pre (JSONB) - Pre-transfusion vitals
    - vital_signs_post (JSONB) - Post-transfusion vitals
    - transfusion_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Started, 2=Completed, 3=Stopped, 4=Reaction
    - stopped_reason (TEXT)
    - witnessed_by (UUID FK)

transfusion_reactions

- Purpose: Adverse reaction reporting and investigation
- Attributes:
    - reaction_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - transfusion_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - reaction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Febrile, 2=Allergic, 3=Hemolytic, 4=TRALI, 5=TACO
    - reaction_severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe
    - onset_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - symptoms (TEXT[])
    - treatment_given (TEXT)
    - outcome (SMALLINT) - 1=Resolved, 2=Ongoing, 3=Serious
    - reported_by (UUID FK)
    - reported_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - investigated_by (UUID FK)
    - investigation_notes (TEXT)

blood_temperature_logs

- Purpose: Cold chain temperature monitoring
- Attributes:
    - temp_log_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_id (UUID FK)
    - refrigerator_id (UUID FK)
    - temperature_c (temperature_c_type)
    - logged_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - is_in_range (BOOLEAN) - Generated: temp between min/max
    - out_of_range_duration_min (SMALLINT)
    - alert_generated (BOOLEAN)
    - alert_acknowledged_by (UUID FK)
    - alert_acknowledged_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

blood_discards

- Purpose: Unit discard tracking with reason
- Attributes:
    - discard_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_id (UUID FK)
    - discard_reason (SMALLINT) - 1=Expired, 2=TTI Positive, 3=QC Fail, 4=Breakage, 5=Other
    - discard_date (DATE)
    - discarded_by (UUID FK)
    - witness_id (UUID FK)
    - documentation (TEXT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- blood_donor_master (1) → blood_donations (N)
- blood_donations (1) → blood_units (N)
- blood_donations (1) → blood_tti_tests (N)
- blood_units (1) → blood_crossmatches (N)
- blood_units (1) → blood_issuances (N)
- blood_units (1) → blood_transfusions (N)
- blood_units (1) → blood_temperature_logs (N)
- blood_requests (1) → blood_issuances (N)
- blood_transfusions (1) → transfusion_reactions (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- blood_units (N) → patients (1) [transfused to]
- blood_issuances (N) → users (1) [received by]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for all blood products
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: IPD, OT, Emergency (transfusions)
- Integrates with: Laboratory (testing), Inventory

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Temperature range validation automated
- Expiry dates calculated by component type
- Status transitions enforced

Data Types:

- Custom domains for temperature and blood groups
- JSONB for vital signs and questionnaires
- Arrays for symptoms and special testing
- Generated columns for eligibility and validation

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- blood_units: Medium (10,000-100,000 units annually)
- blood_temperature_logs: Very Large (continuous monitoring)
- blood_transfusions: Medium (matches units transfused)
- blood_tti_tests: Medium (5 tests per donation)

Indexing Strategy:

- Unique index on blood_units (unit_number)
- Index on blood_units (blood_group, component_type, unit_status) for inventory
- Index on blood_temperature_logs (logged_at) for monitoring
- Partial indexes for available units and pending tests

Partitioning:

- blood_temperature_logs: Range partitioning by logged_at (daily)
- blood_units: Range partitioning by collection_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Blood Bank Technicians: Processing and testing
- Medical Officers: Approval and release
- Nurses: Transfusion administration
- Quality Officers: Audit and compliance

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient privacy for transfusion records
- Donor confidentiality protection

Sensitive Data:

- Donor information highly protected
- TTI test results confidential
- Patient transfusion history private

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 50,000+ annual donations
- Multiple component processing
- Advanced testing technologies
- Regional blood bank networking

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for patient safety and survival
- Regulatory compliance essential
- Integration with clinical workflows
- Emergency response capability
