## Domain 9: Ambulance Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- GPS Tracking: Continuous location logging at ≤30-second intervals during active trips
- Maintenance Enforcement: Vehicles with overdue maintenance blocked from dispatch
- Certification Validation: Paramedic certifications must be current for assignment
- ETA Communication: Estimated arrival time must be communicated to receiving facilities
- Handover Requirement: Post-trip handover report mandatory before trip closure
- Billing Integration: Trip charges linked to distance and resource usage
- Fleet Management: Multiple vehicle types with equipment tracking

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive emergency medical services management with real-time tracking, maintenance oversight, and billing integration.

Sub-modules:

- Fleet Management
- Equipment Inventory
- Staff Certification
- Trip Dispatch & Tracking
- GPS Monitoring
- Maintenance Management
- Billing & Insurance

Entity List:

- ambulance_fleet
- ambulance_equipment
- ambulance_staff
- ambulance_trips
- ambulance_gps_logs
- ambulance_maintenance
- ambulance_billing
- ambulance_dispatch_log

### 3. Entity Design

ambulance_fleet

- Purpose: Vehicle inventory and specifications
- Attributes:
    - ambulance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vehicle_number (VARCHAR(20)) - License plate
    - vehicle_type (SMALLINT) - 1=BLS, 2=ALS, 3=Mobile ICU, 4=Air, 5=Neonatal
    - make (VARCHAR(100))
    - model (VARCHAR(100))
    - year (SMALLINT)
    - vin (VARCHAR(50)) - Vehicle Identification Number
    - insurance_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - insurance_expiry (DATE)
    - capacity_patients (SMALLINT)
    - capacity_staff (SMALLINT)
    - odometer_reading (NUMERIC(10,1))
    - fuel_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Petrol, 2=Diesel, 3=Hybrid, 4=Electric
    - current_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Dispatched, 3=Maintenance, 4=Retired
    - base_location (VARCHAR(255))
    - gps_device_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

ambulance_equipment

- Purpose: Medical equipment inventory per vehicle
- Attributes:
    - equipment_id (UUID PK)
    - ambulance_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - equipment_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Defibrillator, 2=Ventilator, 3=Monitor, 4=Oxygen, 5=Stretcher
    - equipment_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - serial_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - calibration_date (DATE)
    - next_calibration_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE) - For consumables
    - current_condition (SMALLINT) - 1=Excellent, 2=Good, 3=Needs Service, 4=Out of Service
    - last_service_date (DATE)
    - is_available (BOOLEAN)

ambulance_staff

- Purpose: Paramedics, EMTs, and drivers with certification tracking
- Attributes:
    - staff_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK) - Link to users table
    - staff_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Paramedic, 2=EMT, 3=Driver, 4=Nurse
    - certification_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - certification_type (VARCHAR(100)) - ACLS, PALS, BLS, etc.
    - certification_issue_date (DATE)
    - certification_expiry_date (DATE)
    - is_certification_valid (BOOLEAN) - Generated: expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE
    - training_completed (TEXT[]) - Specialized trainings
    - shift_schedule (JSONB) - Weekly schedule
    - current_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=On Trip, 3=Off Duty, 4=Training
    - total_trips_completed (INTEGER)

ambulance_trips

- Purpose: Trip master records with status tracking
- Attributes:
    - trip_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - trip_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Sequential business number
    - ambulance_id (UUID FK)
    - driver_id (UUID FK)
    - paramedic_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK) - May be NULL initially
    - call_received_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - trip_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Emergency, 2=Inter-facility Transfer, 3=Scheduled, 4=Discharge
    - pickup_address (TEXT)
    - pickup_lat (latitude_type)
    - pickup_lon (longitude_type)
    - pickup_geom (GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326))
    - destination_address (TEXT)
    - destination_lat (latitude_type)
    - destination_lon (longitude_type)
    - destination_geom (GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326))
    - dispatched_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - arrived_at_pickup (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - departed_pickup_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - arrived_at_destination_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - trip_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Dispatched, 2=En Route to Pickup, 3=At Pickup, 4=Transporting, 5=Completed, 6=Cancelled
    - odometer_start (NUMERIC(10,1))
    - odometer_end (NUMERIC(10,1))
    - distance_km (NUMERIC(8,2)) - Generated from GPS or odometer
    - receiving_facility_id (UUID FK) - If inter-facility
    - receiving_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - handover_notes (TEXT)
    - estimated_arrival_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - eta_communicated_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - eta_communicated_to (VARCHAR(255))
    - trip_billing_id (UUID FK)

ambulance_gps_logs

- Purpose: High-frequency GPS location tracking
- Attributes:
    - gps_id (UUID PK)
    - trip_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - ambulance_id (UUID FK)
    - latitude (latitude_type)
    - longitude (longitude_type)
    - location_geom (GEOGRAPHY(POINT,4326))
    - speed_kmph (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - heading_degrees (SMALLINT) - 0-359
    - logged_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - signal_quality (SMALLINT) - 1=Excellent, 2=Good, 3=Poor, 4=Lost
    - device_id (VARCHAR(50))

ambulance_maintenance

- Purpose: Vehicle maintenance scheduling and history
- Attributes:
    - maintenance_id (UUID PK)
    - ambulance_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - maintenance_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Preventive, 2=Corrective, 3=Emergency
    - scheduled_date (DATE)
    - performed_date (DATE)
    - odometer_at_service (NUMERIC(10,1))
    - service_provider (VARCHAR(255))
    - service_cost (currency_type)
    - work_performed (TEXT[])
    - parts_replaced (TEXT[])
    - next_service_date (DATE)
    - next_service_odometer (NUMERIC(10,1))
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - maintenance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Overdue

ambulance_billing

- Purpose: Trip billing and insurance claims
- Attributes:
    - billing_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - trip_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - billing_date (DATE)
    - base_fare (currency_type)
    - distance_charge (currency_type)
    - time_charge (currency_type)
    - equipment_usage_charge (currency_type)
    - medication_charge (currency_type)
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - insurance_covered (currency_type)
    - patient_payable (currency_type)
    - billing_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Sent, 3=Paid, 4=Insurance Claim
    - insurance_claim_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - claim_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Submitted, 2=Approved, 3=Rejected, 4=Partially Paid
    - paid_date (DATE)

ambulance_dispatch_log

- Purpose: Dispatch center operations and call tracking
- Attributes:
    - dispatch_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - call_received_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - caller_number (phone_type)
    - caller_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - caller_location (VARCHAR(255))
    - emergency_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Medical, 2=Trauma, 3=Psychiatric, 4=OB/GYN
    - emergency_severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Critical, 2=Serious, 3=Moderate, 4=Minor
    - dispatch_priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Immediate, 2=High, 3=Medium, 4=Low
    - dispatched_ambulance_id (UUID FK)
    - dispatch_time_sec (INTEGER) - Time to dispatch
    - response_time_sec (INTEGER) - Time to scene
    - call_recording_ref (VARCHAR(100)) - Call recording storage reference
    - dispatcher_id (UUID FK)
    - dispatch_notes (TEXT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- ambulance_fleet (1) → ambulance_equipment (N)
- ambulance_fleet (1) → ambulance_trips (N)
- ambulance_fleet (1) → ambulance_maintenance (N)
- ambulance_trips (1) → ambulance_gps_logs (N)
- ambulance_trips (1) → ambulance_billing (N)
- ambulance_staff (1) → ambulance_trips (N) [as driver/paramedic]

N:1 Relationships:

- ambulance_trips (N) → patients (1)
- ambulance_trips (N) → facilities (1) [receiving]
- ambulance_gps_logs (N) → ambulance_fleet (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active trips
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Emergency Department, IPD Transfers
- Integrates with: Billing, Asset Management

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Distance calculations automated
- Certification validation automated
- Maintenance scheduling enforced

Data Types:

- GEOGRAPHY for spatial operations
- Custom domains for coordinates
- JSONB for schedules and notes
- Arrays for work and parts tracking

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- ambulance_gps_logs: Extremely Large (120 logs/hour × 24/7 operations)
- ambulance_trips: Large (20,000+ trips annually)
- ambulance_maintenance: Medium (regular service intervals)
- ambulance_billing: Medium (1:1 with trips)

Indexing Strategy:

- Partition ambulance_gps_logs by logged_at (daily)
- GIST index on location_geom for spatial queries
- Index on ambulance_trips (trip_status, dispatched_at) for dispatch monitoring
- Partial indexes for active vehicles and staff

Partitioning:

- ambulance_gps_logs: Range partitioning by logged_at (daily)
- ambulance_trips: Range partitioning by dispatched_at (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Dispatchers: Trip assignment and monitoring
- Paramedics: Patient care documentation
- Fleet Managers: Maintenance scheduling
- Billing Staff: Invoice processing

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient privacy for trip details
- Location data access controls

Sensitive Data:

- Patient medical information protected
- GPS location data confidential
- Call recordings highly secure

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100+ vehicle fleet
- Real-time GPS analytics
- Advanced routing algorithms
- Integration with traffic systems

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for emergency response
- Direct impact on patient outcomes
- Operational efficiency for fleet management
- Financial sustainability through billing

---
## Domain 10: Clinical OPD (Outpatient Department)

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Appointment-Based: Majority of visits scheduled, with walk-in support
- Vital Signs: Mandatory capture at every visit
- Clinical Documentation: SOAP notes format with structured data
- Referral Management: Internal and external referral tracking
- Follow-up Scheduling: Automated follow-up appointment generation
- Multi-consultant: Support for specialist consultations
- Queue Management: Real-time patient flow tracking

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive outpatient care management with appointment scheduling, clinical documentation, and referral coordination.

Sub-modules:

- Appointment Scheduling
- Patient Registration
- Vital Signs Monitoring
- Clinical Consultation
- Diagnosis & Treatment
- Referral Management
- Follow-up Planning

Entity List:

- opd_appointments
- opd_visits
- opd_vital_signs
- opd_consultations
- opd_diagnoses
- opd_prescriptions
- opd_referrals
- opd_follow_ups
- opd_queue_management

### 3. Entity Design

opd_appointments

- Purpose: Scheduled patient appointments
- Attributes:
    - appointment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_date (DATE)
    - appointment_time (TIME)
    - appointment_type (SMALLINT) - 1=New, 2=Follow-up, 3=Review, 4=Procedure
    - appointment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=Checked-in, 3=In Consultation, 4=Completed, 5=Cancelled, 6=No Show
    - reason_for_visit (TEXT)
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=Emergency
    - scheduled_by (UUID FK)
    - cancelled_reason (TEXT)
    - cancelled_by (UUID FK)
    - cancelled_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - reminder_sent (BOOLEAN)
    - reminder_sent_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

opd_visits

- Purpose: Master visit record for each OPD encounter
- Attributes:
    - visit_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK) - Null for walk-ins
    - visit_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Sequential visit ID
    - visit_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=Walk-in, 3=Emergency
    - visit_date (DATE)
    - visit_time (TIME)
    - registration_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - triage_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - consultation_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - completion_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - visit_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Registered, 2=Triaged, 3=Waiting, 4=In Consultation, 5=Completed
    - payment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Partially Paid, 3=Paid
    - is_insurance_verified (BOOLEAN)

opd_vital_signs

- Purpose: Vital signs captured during OPD visit
- Attributes:
    - vital_id (UUID PK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - temperature (temperature_c_type)
    - heart_rate (SMALLINT)
    - blood_pressure_systolic (SMALLINT)
    - blood_pressure_diastolic (SMALLINT)
    - respiratory_rate (SMALLINT)
    - oxygen_saturation (percentage_type)
    - pain_score (SMALLINT) - 0-10 scale
    - height_cm (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - weight_kg (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - bmi (NUMERIC(4,1)) - Generated: weight/(height/100)^2
    - captured_by (UUID FK)
    - captured_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - vital_location (VARCHAR(100)) - Triage, Consultation, etc.

opd_consultations

- Purpose: Clinical consultation notes (SOAP format)
- Attributes:
    - consultation_id (UUID PK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - subjective (TEXT) - Patient complaints, history
    - objective (TEXT) - Physical findings, observations
    - assessment (TEXT) - Clinical assessment, diagnosis
    - plan (TEXT) - Treatment plan, recommendations
    - consultation_start_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - consultation_end_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - duration_minutes (SMALLINT) - Generated
    - consultation_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Completed, 3=Signed
    - signed_by (UUID FK)
    - signed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - template_used (VARCHAR(100)) - Consultation template

opd_diagnoses

- Purpose: Structured diagnosis documentation
- Attributes:
    - diagnosis_id (UUID PK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - diagnosis_code (VARCHAR(20)) - ICD-10/11 code
    - diagnosis_description (VARCHAR(255))
    - diagnosis_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Primary, 2=Secondary, 3=Provisional
    - diagnosis_certainty (SMALLINT) - 1=Confirmed, 2=Suspected, 3=Ruled Out
    - onset_date (DATE)
    - is_chronic (BOOLEAN)
    - affected_body_system (VARCHAR(100))
    - severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe

opd_prescriptions

- Purpose: Outpatient medication prescriptions (links to RX Management)
- Attributes:
    - opd_prescription_id (UUID PK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - prescription_id (UUID FK) - Link to main rx_prescriptions
    - is_eprescription (BOOLEAN)
    - printed_copy_provided (BOOLEAN)
    - provided_by (UUID FK)
    - provided_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

opd_referrals

- Purpose: Internal and external referral management
- Attributes:
    - referral_id (UUID PK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - referring_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - referred_to_department (UUID FK) - Internal
    - referred_to_doctor_id (UUID FK) - Internal
    - external_facility_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - external_doctor_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - external_contact (VARCHAR(255))
    - referral_reason (TEXT)
    - referral_priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=Emergency
    - referral_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Requested, 2=Accepted, 3=Completed, 4=Rejected
    - appointment_given (BOOLEAN)
    - appointment_date (DATE)
    - feedback_received (TEXT)
    - created_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

opd_follow_ups

- Purpose: Follow-up appointment planning
- Attributes:
    - follow_up_id (UUID PK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - follow_up_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Review, 2=Procedure, 3=Test Review
    - recommended_interval_days (SMALLINT)
    - scheduled_date (DATE)
    - scheduled_time (TIME)
    - follow_up_reason (TEXT)
    - tests_to_review (UUID[]) - Lab/radiology tests
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Scheduled, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    - created_by (UUID FK)
    - created_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

opd_queue_management

- Purpose: Real-time patient flow tracking
- Attributes:
    - queue_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - queue_number (SMALLINT)
    - queue_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Waiting, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Skipped
    - wait_time_minutes (INTEGER) - Generated
    - estimated_wait_time (INTEGER)
    - current_position (SMALLINT)
    - called_by (UUID FK)
    - called_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - completed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- opd_visits (1) → opd_vital_signs (N)
- opd_visits (1) → opd_consultations (N)
- opd_consultations (1) → opd_diagnoses (N)
- opd_consultations (1) → opd_prescriptions (N)
- opd_consultations (1) → opd_referrals (N)
- opd_consultations (1) → opd_follow_ups (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- opd_visits (N) → opd_appointments (1)
- opd_prescriptions (N) → rx_prescriptions (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: CASCADE for consultation-related records
- ON UPDATE: RESTRICT for completed consultations

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: RX Management, Laboratory, Radiology
- Integrates with: Billing, Appointment Scheduling

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- BMI calculation automated
- Wait time calculations
- Duration tracking

Data Types:

- Custom domains for medical measurements
- Arrays for test references
- JSONB for flexible documentation
- Generated columns for calculations

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- opd_visits: Very Large (500,000+ annually)
- opd_consultations: Large (1:1 with visits)
- opd_vital_signs: Large (multiple per visit)
- opd_queue_management: High volume (real-time updates)

Indexing Strategy:

- Partition opd_visits by visit_date (monthly)
- Index on opd_appointments (appointment_date, doctor_id)
- Index on opd_queue_management (queue_status, department_id)
- Partial indexes for active visits and appointments

Partitioning:

- opd_visits: Range partitioning by visit_date (monthly)
- opd_appointments: Range partitioning by appointment_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Doctors: Consultation documentation
- Nurses: Vital signs capture
- Receptionists: Appointment scheduling
- Patients: Portal access to own records

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient privacy for clinical data
- Department-level restrictions

Sensitive Data:

- Clinical notes protected
- Diagnosis information confidential
- Patient flow data secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 1M+ annual visits
- Real-time queue management
- Mobile app integration
- Advanced analytics on patient flow

Cross-domain Impact:

- Primary point of patient care
- Gateway to specialist services
- Revenue generation through consultations
- Quality metrics for clinical care
---
## Domain 11: Clinical IPD (Inpatient Department)

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Admission Management: Comprehensive admission process with bed assignment
- Ward Management: Multiple wards with different specialties and acuity levels
- Bed Tracking: Real-time bed occupancy and availability monitoring
- Transfer Management: Patient transfers between wards/beds with audit trail
- Discharge Process: Structured discharge with planning and summary
- Rounds Documentation: Clinical ward rounds with progress notes
- Length of Stay: Tracking and optimization

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive inpatient care management with bed utilization, clinical progression, and discharge planning.

Sub-modules:

- Admission Management
- Bed & Ward Management
- Patient Transfers
- Discharge Planning
- Ward Rounds
- Nursing Care
- Length of Stay Tracking

Entity List:

- ipd_admissions
- ipd_beds
- ipd_wards
- ipd_bed_assignments
- ipd_transfers
- ipd_discharges
- ipd_rounds
- ipd_nursing_notes
- ipd_length_of_stay

### 3. Entity Design

ipd_admissions

- Purpose: Master admission record for inpatient stays
- Attributes:
    - admission_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Sequential business number
    - admission_date (DATE)
    - admission_time (TIME)
    - admission_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Emergency, 2=Elective, 3=Transfer
    - admission_source (SMALLINT) - 1=OPD, 2=ER, 3=Other Hospital, 4=Direct
    - referring_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - admitting_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - primary_diagnosis (VARCHAR(255))
    - secondary_diagnoses (TEXT[])
    - admission_notes (TEXT)
    - expected_length_of_stay_days (SMALLINT)
    - admission_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Admitted, 2=Transferred, 3=Discharged, 4=Absconded
    - is_insurance_verified (BOOLEAN)
    - insurance_authorization_number (VARCHAR(100))

ipd_wards

- Purpose: Ward/master unit management
- Attributes:
    - ward_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - ward_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - ward_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - ward_type (SMALLINT) - 1=General, 2=Specialty, 3=ICU, 4=Step-down, 5=Isolation
    - specialty (UUID FK) - Department specialty
    - total_beds (SMALLINT)
    - available_beds (SMALLINT) - Generated
    - occupied_beds (SMALLINT) - Generated
    - nurse_station_phone (phone_type)
    - charge_nurse_id (UUID FK)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

ipd_beds

- Purpose: Individual bed management
- Attributes:
    - bed_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - ward_id (UUID FK)
    - bed_number (VARCHAR(10))
    - bed_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Regular, 2=ICU, 3=Pediatric, 4=Bariatric, 5=Isolation
    - equipment_available (TEXT[]) - Oxygen, suction, monitor, etc.
    - current_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Occupied, 3=Cleaning, 4=Maintenance
    - is_isolated (BOOLEAN)
    - isolation_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Airborne, contact, etc.
    - last_cleaned_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - next_maintenance_date (DATE)

ipd_bed_assignments

- Purpose: Bed assignment history for patients
- Attributes:
    - assignment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - bed_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - assignment_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - assignment_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - assigned_by (UUID FK)
    - assignment_reason (TEXT)
    - assignment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Completed
    - duration_hours (INTEGER) - Generated

ipd_transfers

- Purpose: Patient transfer between beds/wards
- Attributes:
    - transfer_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - from_bed_id (UUID FK)
    - to_bed_id (UUID FK)
    - from_ward_id (UUID FK)
    - to_ward_id (UUID FK)
    - transfer_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - transfer_reason (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Bed Availability, 3=Patient Request
    - transfer_notes (TEXT)
    - initiated_by (UUID FK)
    - accepted_by (UUID FK) - Receiving nurse/doctor
    - transfer_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Requested, 2=Approved, 3=Completed, 4=Rejected

ipd_discharges

- Purpose: Discharge management and planning
- Attributes:
    - discharge_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - discharge_date (DATE)
    - discharge_time (TIME)
    - discharge_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=AMA, 3=Transfer, 4=Expired
    - discharge_disposition (SMALLINT) - 1=Home, 2=Rehab, 3=Other Facility, 4=Hospice
    - discharge_summary (TEXT)
    - follow_up_instructions (TEXT)
    - discharge_medications (JSONB) - Reconciliation
    - discharging_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - discharge_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=Completed, 3=Cancelled
    - bed_cleared_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - final_bill_generated (BOOLEAN)

ipd_rounds

- Purpose: Clinical ward rounds documentation
- Attributes:
    - round_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - round_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Morning, 2=Evening, 3=Specialty, 4=Multidisciplinary
    - round_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - attending_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - participants (UUID[]) - Doctors, nurses, therapists
    - subjective (TEXT) - Patient report
    - objective (TEXT) - Clinical findings
    - assessment (TEXT) - Clinical assessment
    - plan (TEXT) - Treatment plan
    - orders_given (JSONB) - New orders during round
    - round_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Completed, 2=Pending
    - signed_by (UUID FK)
    - signed_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

ipd_nursing_notes

- Purpose: Nursing assessments and documentation
- Attributes:
    - nursing_note_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - note_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - note_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Assessment, 2=Progress, 3=Incident, 4=Handover
    - vital_signs (JSONB) - Current vitals
    - intake_output (JSONB) - Fluid balance
    - pain_assessment (JSONB)
    - skin_assessment (JSONB)
    - nursing_interventions (TEXT[])
    - patient_response (TEXT)
    - nurse_id (UUID FK)
    - note_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Completed

ipd_length_of_stay

- Purpose: Length of stay tracking and analytics
- Attributes:
    - los_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_date (DATE)
    - discharge_date (DATE)
    - actual_length_of_stay_days (INTEGER) - Generated
    - expected_length_of_stay_days (INTEGER)
    - variance_days (INTEGER) - Generated: actual - expected
    - ward_type (SMALLINT)
    - diagnosis_group (VARCHAR(100))
    - drg_code (VARCHAR(10)) - Diagnosis Related Group
    - is_planned_admission (BOOLEAN)
    - readmission_within_30_days (BOOLEAN)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- ipd_admissions (1) → ipd_bed_assignments (N)
- ipd_admissions (1) → ipd_transfers (N)
- ipd_admissions (1) → ipd_discharges (N)
- ipd_admissions (1) → ipd_rounds (N)
- ipd_admissions (1) → ipd_nursing_notes (N)
- ipd_wards (1) → ipd_beds (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- ipd_bed_assignments (N) → ipd_beds (1)
- ipd_transfers (N) → ipd_wards (1) [from/to]
- ipd_rounds (N) → users (1) [attending doctor]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active admissions
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for bed status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Laboratory, Radiology, Pharmacy
- Integrates with: Billing, Kitchen, Laundry

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Bed availability calculations automated
- Length of stay calculations
- Status tracking with validation

Data Types:

- JSONB for clinical assessments
- Arrays for diagnoses and participants
- Generated columns for durations
- Custom domains for medical data

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- ipd_admissions: Large (50,000+ annually)
- ipd_bed_assignments: Large (multiple per admission)
- ipd_nursing_notes: Very Large (multiple daily per patient)
- ipd_rounds: Large (daily rounds for all patients)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on ipd_admissions (admission_status, admission_date)
- Index on ipd_beds (current_status, ward_id) for bed management
- Index on ipd_bed_assignments (assignment_status, assignment_start)
- Partial indexes for active admissions and beds

Partitioning:

- ipd_admissions: Range partitioning by admission_date (monthly)
- ipd_nursing_notes: Range partitioning by note_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Doctors: Round documentation and orders
- Nurses: Nursing notes and assessments
- Ward Clerks: Bed assignment and transfers
- Administrators: LOS analytics

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Ward-level restrictions for staff
- Patient privacy for clinical data

Sensitive Data:

- Clinical progress notes protected
- Patient location information secured
- Discharge summaries confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 1000+ concurrent inpatients
- Real-time bed management
- Advanced predictive analytics for bed demand
- Integration with patient flow systems

Cross-domain Impact:

- Largest consumer of hospital resources
- Primary driver of hospital revenue
- Key quality indicator through LOS metrics
- Central coordination point for patient care

---
## Domain 12: Pharmacy

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Inventory Control: Strict stock management with FEFO (First Expiry First Out) enforcement
- Dispensing Rules: Cannot dispense without valid prescription; quantity validation
- Drug Interactions: Mandatory real-time interaction checking before dispensing
- Controlled Substances: Special handling with witness requirements
- Expiry Management: Automatic blocking of expired drugs
- Pricing Tiers: Different pricing for cash vs insurance patients
- Supplier Management: Multiple suppliers with performance tracking

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive medication management from procurement to patient dispensing with safety controls and inventory optimization.

Sub-modules:

- Drug Master Catalog
- Inventory Management
- Prescription Dispensing
- Drug Interaction Checking
- Supplier Management
- Pricing & Billing
- Regulatory Compliance

Entity List:

- drug_master
- drug_categories
- drug_suppliers
- pharmacy_inventory
- inventory_transactions
- prescription_dispensing
- dispensing_details
- drug_interaction_checks
- pharmacy_billing
- controlled_substance_logs

### 3. Entity Design

drug_master

- Purpose: Central drug catalog with comprehensive information
- Attributes:
    - drug_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_code (VARCHAR(50)) - NDC, ATC, or internal code
    - drug_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - generic_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - brand_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - drug_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Tablet, 2=Capsule, 3=Syrup, 4=Injection, 5=Topical, 6=Inhaler
    - strength (VARCHAR(100)) - 500mg, 10mg/ml, etc.
    - strength_unit (VARCHAR(30))
    - form (VARCHAR(50))
    - route (SMALLINT) - 1=Oral, 2=IV, 3=IM, 4=SC, 5=Topical, 6=Inhaled
    - therapeutic_class (VARCHAR(100))
    - pharmacological_class (VARCHAR(100))
    - schedule (VARCHAR(10)) - Controlled substance schedule
    - storage_conditions (TEXT)
    - shelf_life_months (SMALLINT)
    - manufacturer (VARCHAR(255))
    - supplier_id (UUID FK)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - requires_prescription (BOOLEAN)

drug_categories

- Purpose: Drug classification and categorization
- Attributes:
    - category_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - category_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - category_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Antibiotics, CVS, CNS, etc.
    - parent_category_id (UUID FK) - Hierarchical structure
    - description (TEXT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

drug_suppliers

- Purpose: Pharmaceutical supplier management
- Attributes:
    - supplier_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - supplier_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - supplier_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - contact_person (VARCHAR(100))
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - contact_email (email_type)
    - address (TEXT)
    - license_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - license_expiry (DATE)
    - lead_time_days (SMALLINT)
    - quality_rating (percentage_type)
    - is_preferred (BOOLEAN)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

pharmacy_inventory

- Purpose: Stock management with lot tracking
- Attributes:
    - inventory_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_id (UUID FK)
    - lot_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - manufacturer_lot (VARCHAR(100))
    - manufacture_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - received_date (DATE)
    - current_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30)) - Tablets, bottles, ml, etc.
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - selling_price (currency_type)
    - reorder_level (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reorder_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - shelf_location (VARCHAR(100))
    - inventory_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Quarantine, 3=Expired, 4=Recalled
    - is_available (BOOLEAN) - Generated: current_quantity > 0 AND expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE

inventory_transactions

- Purpose: Stock movement tracking
- Attributes:
    - transaction_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - inventory_id (UUID FK)
    - transaction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Receipt, 2=Dispensing, 3=Adjustment, 4=Transfer, 5=Wastage
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reference_id (UUID) - PO, prescription, etc.
    - transaction_date (DATE)
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - reason (TEXT) - For adjustments/wastage
    - balance_after (NUMERIC(10,2)) - Generated

prescription_dispensing

- Purpose: Master dispensing record
- Attributes:
    - dispensing_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - prescription_id (UUID FK) - Link to RX Management
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - dispensing_date (DATE)
    - dispensing_time (TIME)
    - total_items (SMALLINT)
    - total_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - subtotal_amount (currency_type)
    - discount_amount (currency_type)
    - tax_amount (currency_type)
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - dispensing_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Partially Dispensed, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    - dispensed_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - payment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Paid, 3=Insurance Claim

dispensing_details

- Purpose: Individual drug line item dispensing
- Attributes:
    - detail_id (UUID PK)
    - dispensing_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - inventory_id (UUID FK)
    - prescription_item_id (UUID FK)
    - quantity_dispensed (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_price (currency_type)
    - total_price (currency_type) - Generated
    - lot_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - is_controlled (BOOLEAN)
    - witness_id (UUID FK) - For controlled substances
    - dispensing_notes (TEXT)

drug_interaction_checks

- Purpose: Interaction checking audit trail
- Attributes:
    - interaction_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - prescription_id (UUID FK)
    - dispensing_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_a_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_b_id (UUID FK)
    - interaction_severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Minor, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe
    - interaction_description (TEXT)
    - check_result (SMALLINT) - 1=No Interaction, 2=Interaction Found
    - override_reason (TEXT)
    - override_by (UUID FK)
    - checked_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - system_used (VARCHAR(100)) - Interaction database

pharmacy_billing

- Purpose: Pharmacy-specific billing
- Attributes:
    - pharmacy_bill_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - dispensing_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - bill_date (DATE)
    - bill_time (TIME)
    - total_drug_cost (currency_type)
    - dispensing_fee (currency_type)
    - tax_amount (currency_type)
    - discount_amount (currency_type)
    - net_amount (currency_type)
    - insurance_covered (currency_type)
    - patient_payable (currency_type)
    - payment_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Cash, 2=Card, 3=Insurance, 4=Wallet
    - bill_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Generated, 2=Paid, 3=Partially Paid

controlled_substance_logs

- Purpose: Special logging for controlled drugs
- Attributes:
    - control_log_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - dispensing_detail_id (UUID FK)
    - drug_id (UUID FK)
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - witness_id (UUID FK)
    - witness_signature (BYTEA)
    - patient_identification (VARCHAR(100))
    - log_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - balance_after (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- drug_master (1) → pharmacy_inventory (N)
- pharmacy_inventory (1) → inventory_transactions (N)
- pharmacy_inventory (1) → dispensing_details (N)
- prescription_dispensing (1) → dispensing_details (N)
- prescription_dispensing (1) → drug_interaction_checks (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- dispensing_details (N) → rx_prescription_items (1)
- drug_interaction_checks (N) → rx_prescriptions (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active inventory
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for drug updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: RX Management, Patient Management
- Required by: Clinical domains for medication administration
- Integrates with: Inventory, Billing, Procurement

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Inventory availability automated
- Pricing calculations generated
- Expiry validation enforced

Data Types:

- NUMERIC for precise quantity tracking
- Custom domains for financial amounts
- Generated columns for business rules
- BYTEA for digital signatures

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- pharmacy_inventory: Medium (10,000-50,000 items)
- inventory_transactions: Very Large (all stock movements)
- prescription_dispensing: Large (matches prescription volume)
- dispensing_details: Large (multiple items per prescription)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on pharmacy_inventory (expiry_date, current_quantity) for FEFO
- Index on prescription_dispensing (dispensing_date, patient_id)
- Partial indexes for active inventory and pending dispensing
- GIN index on drug therapeutic classes

Partitioning:

- inventory_transactions: Range partitioning by transaction_date (monthly)
- prescription_dispensing: Range partitioning by dispensing_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Pharmacists: Dispensing and verification
- Pharmacy Technicians: Inventory management
- Cashiers: Billing and payments
- Managers: Supplier and pricing management

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Branch-level inventory separation
- Patient privacy for dispensing records

Sensitive Data:

- Controlled substance logs highly protected
- Pricing information confidential
- Patient medication history private

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 5,000+ drug items
- Multiple pharmacy locations
- Automated dispensing systems
- Real-time inventory synchronization

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for patient medication safety
- Major revenue center for hospital
- Regulatory compliance essential
- Integration with clinical workflows

---
## Domain 13: Laboratory (LIS)

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Order Management: Comprehensive test ordering with priority levels
- Specimen Tracking: Full chain of custody from collection to processing
- Result Entry: Structured result capture with validation rules
- TAT Monitoring: Turnaround time tracking with alerts for delays
- QC Integration: Quality control with Westgard rules enforcement
- Panic Values: Critical result notification and acknowledgment
- Instrument Integration: Automated instrument data interfaces

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive laboratory information management with full test lifecycle tracking, quality assurance, and result reporting.

Sub-modules:

- Test Master Catalog
- Test Order Management
- Specimen Collection & Tracking
- Result Entry & Validation
- Quality Control
- Instrument Management
- Report Generation

Entity List:

- lab_test_master
- lab_test_panels
- lab_orders
- lab_order_items
- lab_specimens
- lab_specimen_aliquots
- lab_results
- lab_result_details
- lab_qc_runs
- lab_instruments
- lab_panic_values
- lab_reports

### 3. Entity Design

lab_test_master

- Purpose: Central test catalog with specifications
- Attributes:
    - test_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - test_code (VARCHAR(50)) - LOINC or internal code
    - test_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - test_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Chemistry, 2=Hematology, 3=Microbiology, 4=Immunology, 5=Molecular
    - specimen_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Blood, urine, tissue, etc.
    - container_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Tube, cup, slide, etc.
    - volume_required_ml (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - storage_conditions (TEXT)
    - stability_hours (SMALLINT)
    - methodology (VARCHAR(100))
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30))
    - reference_range_low (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reference_range_high (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reference_range_text (TEXT) - For textual results
    - tat_target_minutes (INTEGER) - Turnaround time target
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - requires_special_handling (BOOLEAN)

lab_test_panels

- Purpose: Test groupings and profiles
- Attributes:
    - panel_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - panel_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - panel_name (VARCHAR(255)) - CBC, LFT, RFT, etc.
    - panel_description (TEXT)
    - test_ids (UUID[]) - Component tests
    - panel_price (currency_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

lab_orders

- Purpose: Test request master records
- Attributes:
    - order_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - order_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Sequential business number
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - requesting_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - ordering_department (UUID FK)
    - order_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - order_priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=STAT
    - clinical_notes (TEXT)
    - diagnosis_codes (TEXT[]) - ICD-10 codes
    - order_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Ordered, 2=Collected, 3=Received, 4=In Progress, 5=Completed, 6=Cancelled
    - order_location (SMALLINT) - 1=OPD, 2=IPD, 3=ER, 4=External
    - is_insurance_approved (BOOLEAN)
    - authorization_number (VARCHAR(100))

lab_order_items

- Purpose: Individual test requests within orders
- Attributes:
    - order_item_id (UUID PK)
    - order_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - test_id (UUID FK)
    - test_code (VARCHAR(50)) - Denormalized
    - test_name (VARCHAR(255)) - Denormalized
    - priority (SMALLINT) - Inherited from order
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Ordered, 2=Collected, 3=Received, 4=In Progress, 5=Completed, 6=Cancelled
    - result_entered_by (UUID FK)
    - result_entered_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - result_verified_by (UUID FK)
    - result_verified_at (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - tat_actual_minutes (INTEGER) - Generated: result_entered_at - order_datetime
    - tat_status (SMALLINT) - 1=On Time, 2=Delayed - Generated based on target TAT

lab_specimens

- Purpose: Specimen collection and tracking
- Attributes:
    - specimen_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - order_item_id (UUID FK)
    - specimen_number (VARCHAR(50)) - Barcode
    - specimen_type (VARCHAR(100))
    - container_type (VARCHAR(100))
    - collection_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - collected_by (UUID FK)
    - collection_site (VARCHAR(100)) - Venipuncture, catheter, etc.
    - collection_notes (TEXT)
    - received_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - received_by (UUID FK)
    - volume_ml (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - appearance (VARCHAR(100)) - Clear, turbid, hemolyzed, etc.
    - specimen_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Collected, 2=In Transit, 3=Received, 4=Processing, 5=Stored, 6=Discarded
    - storage_location (VARCHAR(100))
    - discard_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - discard_reason (TEXT)

lab_specimen_aliquots

- Purpose: Specimen aliquot management
- Attributes:
    - aliquot_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - specimen_id (UUID FK)
    - aliquot_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - aliquot_volume_ml (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - created_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - created_by (UUID FK)
    - storage_location (VARCHAR(100))
    - used_for_test (UUID FK) - Which test used this aliquot
    - used_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - aliquot_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Used, 3=Discarded

lab_results

- Purpose: Test result master records
- Attributes:
    - result_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - order_item_id (UUID FK)
    - test_id (UUID FK)
    - specimen_id (UUID FK)
    - result_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - result_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Numeric, 2=Text, 3=Image, 4=File
    - result_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Preliminary, 2=Final, 3=Corrected, 4=Cancelled
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - instrument_id (UUID FK) - If automated
    - reagent_lot_id (UUID FK) - Link to reagent management
    - qc_run_id (UUID FK) - Quality control reference

lab_result_details

- Purpose: Detailed result values
- Attributes:
    - result_detail_id (UUID PK)
    - result_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - parameter_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - numeric_value (NUMERIC(15,6))
    - text_value (TEXT)
    - unit (VARCHAR(30))
    - reference_range_low (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reference_range_high (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - flags (VARCHAR(10)) - H=High, L=Low, A=Abnormal
    - is_critical (BOOLEAN)
    - comments (TEXT)

lab_qc_runs

- Purpose: Quality control runs and results
- Attributes:
    - qc_run_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - test_id (UUID FK)
    - instrument_id (UUID FK)
    - qc_material_lot (VARCHAR(100))
    - run_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - expected_value (NUMERIC(15,6))
    - measured_value (NUMERIC(15,6))
    - sd_value (NUMERIC(15,6))
    - cv_percent (percentage_type)
    - westgard_rules (JSONB) - Rule violations
    - qc_status (SMALLINT) - 1=In Control, 2=Out of Control
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - reviewed_by (UUID FK)
    - reviewed_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

lab_instruments

- Purpose: Laboratory instrument management
- Attributes:
    - instrument_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - instrument_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - instrument_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - manufacturer (VARCHAR(255))
    - model (VARCHAR(100))
    - serial_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - installation_date (DATE)
    - last_calibration_date (DATE)
    - next_calibration_date (DATE)
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Operational, 2=Maintenance, 3=Out of Service
    - interface_type (VARCHAR(100)) - HL7, ASTM, etc.
    - is_connected (BOOLEAN)

lab_panic_values

- Purpose: Critical result notification tracking
- Attributes:
    - panic_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - result_id (UUID FK)
    - order_item_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - test_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - result_value (VARCHAR(255))
    - reference_range (VARCHAR(100))
    - notified_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - notified_to (UUID FK) - Doctor/nurse
    - notification_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Phone, 2=Message, 3=System
    - acknowledgment_received (BOOLEAN)
    - acknowledgment_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - acknowledgment_by (UUID FK)
    - follow_up_action (TEXT)

lab_reports

- Purpose: Final report generation
- Attributes:
    - report_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - order_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - report_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - report_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Interim, 2=Final, 3=Amended
    - report_format (VARCHAR(50)) - PDF, HTML, Text
    - generated_by (UUID FK)
    - released_by (UUID FK)
    - released_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - report_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Released, 3=Archived
    - storage_path (VARCHAR(500))
    - digital_signature (BYTEA)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- lab_orders (1) → lab_order_items (N)
- lab_order_items (1) → lab_specimens (N)
- lab_specimens (1) → lab_specimen_aliquots (N)
- lab_order_items (1) → lab_results (N)
- lab_results (1) → lab_result_details (N)
- lab_instruments (1) → lab_qc_runs (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- lab_results (N) → reagent_lots (1) [from reagent management]
- lab_panic_values (N) → users (1) [notified to]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active orders
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for result updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Reagent Management
- Required by: All clinical domains for diagnostics
- Integrates with: Radiology, Pharmacy, Billing

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- TAT calculations automated
- Result flagging automated
- Status transitions validated

Data Types:

- NUMERIC for precise result values
- JSONB for Westgard rules
- Arrays for test panels
- Generated columns for business logic

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- lab_orders: Very Large (1M+ annually)
- lab_results: Extremely Large (multiple results per order)
- lab_specimens: Large (1:1 with order items)
- lab_qc_runs: Medium (daily QC per instrument)

Indexing Strategy:

- Partition lab_orders by order_datetime (monthly)
- Index on lab_results (result_datetime, test_id)
- Index on lab_specimens (specimen_status, collection_datetime)
- Partial indexes for pending results and critical values

Partitioning:

- lab_orders: Range partitioning by order_datetime (monthly)
- lab_results: Range partitioning by result_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Lab Technicians: Result entry and QC
- Pathologists: Result verification
- Phlebotomists: Specimen collection
- Doctors: Ordering and result viewing

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient privacy for results
- Department-level restrictions

Sensitive Data:

- Patient results highly protected
- QC data for regulatory compliance
- Instrument calibration records secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10,000+ daily tests
- High-volume automated instruments
- Molecular diagnostics integration
- Real-time analytics on TAT

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for clinical decision making
- Major diagnostic revenue center
- Quality metrics through TAT monitoring
- Integration with EMR for result delivery

## Domain 14: Radiology (RIS)

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Modality Integration: Support for multiple imaging modalities (CT, MRI, X-ray, USG)
- DICOM Compliance: Full DICOM standard support with PACS integration
- Appointment Scheduling: Resource-based scheduling for equipment and radiologists
- Procedure Workflow: Complete procedure lifecycle from order to report
- Radiation Safety: Dose tracking and monitoring for patients and staff
- Contrast Management: Contrast agent usage and reaction tracking
- Report Generation: Structured reporting with templates and voice recognition

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive radiology information management with imaging workflow, resource scheduling, and diagnostic reporting.

Sub-modules:

- Modality Management
- Procedure Catalog
- Order Management
- Appointment Scheduling
- Imaging Workflow
- Contrast Management
- Report Generation
- Radiation Safety

Entity List:

- radiology_modalities
- radiology_procedures
- radiology_orders
- radiology_appointments
- radiology_procedure_steps
- radiology_contrast_usage
- radiology_reports
- radiology_dose_monitoring
- radiology_technician_notes
- radiology_pacs_integration

### 3. Entity Design

radiology_modalities

- Purpose: Imaging equipment management
- Attributes:
    - modality_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - modality_code (VARCHAR(20)) - CT, MRI, XR, US, etc.
    - modality_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - manufacturer (VARCHAR(255))
    - model (VARCHAR(100))
    - serial_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - installation_date (DATE)
    - room_number (VARCHAR(20))
    - aetitle (VARCHAR(50)) - DICOM AE Title
    - ip_address (VARCHAR(45))
    - port (INTEGER)
    - last_calibration_date (DATE)
    - next_calibration_date (DATE)
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Operational, 2=Maintenance, 3=Out of Service
    - max_patients_per_day (SMALLINT)
    - slot_duration_minutes (SMALLINT)
    - is_dicom_compliant (BOOLEAN)

radiology_procedures

- Purpose: Radiology procedure catalog
- Attributes:
    - procedure_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - procedure_code (VARCHAR(50)) - CPT/RVU codes
    - procedure_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - modality_type (SMALLINT) - 1=CT, 2=MRI, 3=X-Ray, 4=USG, 5=Mammo, 6=Fluoro
    - body_part (VARCHAR(100))
    - contrast_required (BOOLEAN)
    - contrast_type (VARCHAR(100)) - IV, Oral, Rectal
    - preparation_instructions (TEXT)
    - duration_minutes (SMALLINT)
    - radiation_dose_typical (NUMERIC(8,2)) - mSv
    - professional_fee (currency_type)
    - technical_fee (currency_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

radiology_orders

- Purpose: Imaging order management
- Attributes:
    - order_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - order_number (VARCHAR(20))
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - requesting_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - procedure_id (UUID FK)
    - clinical_indication (TEXT)
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=STAT
    - order_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - order_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Ordered, 2=Scheduled, 3=In Progress, 4=Completed, 5=Cancelled
    - pre_procedure_instructions (TEXT)
    - contraindications (TEXT[])
    - pregnancy_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Not Pregnant, 2=Pregnant, 3=Unknown
    - creatinine_level (NUMERIC(5,2)) - For contrast safety
    - gfr_level (NUMERIC(5,2))

radiology_appointments

- Purpose: Resource scheduling for imaging
- Attributes:
    - appointment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - order_id (UUID FK)
    - modality_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - scheduled_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - duration_minutes (SMALLINT)
    - technologist_id (UUID FK)
    - radiologist_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=Checked-in, 3=In Progress, 4=Completed, 5=No Show
    - checkin_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - start_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - end_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - room_id (UUID FK)
    - preparation_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Not Started, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed

radiology_procedure_steps

- Purpose: Detailed procedure workflow tracking
- Attributes:
    - step_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK)
    - step_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Preparation, 2=Positioning, 3=Imaging, 4=Contrast, 5=Post-Processing
    - step_start_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - step_end_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - step_duration_minutes (SMALLINT) - Generated
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - step_notes (TEXT)
    - step_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Skipped
    - images_taken (SMALLINT)
    - sequences_performed (TEXT[])

radiology_contrast_usage

- Purpose: Contrast agent administration tracking
- Attributes:
    - contrast_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK)
    - contrast_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Iohexol, Gadolinium, Barium, etc.
    - contrast_lot_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - volume_ml (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - concentration (VARCHAR(50))
    - route (SMALLINT) - 1=IV, 2=Oral, 3=Rectal
    - injection_rate_ml_sec (NUMERIC(4,2))
    - administered_by (UUID FK)
    - administration_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - patient_reaction (BOOLEAN)
    - reaction_details (TEXT)
    - premedication_given (BOOLEAN)
    - premedication_details (TEXT)

radiology_reports

- Purpose: Diagnostic report generation
- Attributes:
    - report_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK)
    - order_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - radiologist_id (UUID FK)
    - report_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - report_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Preliminary, 2=Final, 3=Addendum, 4=Correction
    - clinical_history (TEXT)
    - comparison (TEXT) - Prior studies
    - technique (TEXT)
    - findings (TEXT)
    - impression (TEXT)
    - recommendations (TEXT)
    - report_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Completed, 3=Verified, 4=Released
    - dictated_by (UUID FK)
    - transcribed_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - dicom_study_uid (VARCHAR(100))
    - pacs_accession_number (VARCHAR(100))

radiology_dose_monitoring

- Purpose: Radiation dose tracking
- Attributes:
    - dose_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - procedure_id (UUID FK)
    - modality_id (UUID FK)
    - dose_type (SMALLINT) - 1=CTDI, 2=DLP, 3=Effective Dose
    - dose_value (NUMERIC(10,4)) - mGy or mSv
    - dose_unit (VARCHAR(20))
    - exposure_parameters (JSONB) - kVp, mAs, time, etc.
    - body_region (VARCHAR(100))
    - patient_size (VARCHAR(50)) - Small, Medium, Large
    - dose_reference_level (NUMERIC(10,4))
    - is_within_limits (BOOLEAN) - Generated
    - recorded_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - recorded_by (UUID FK)

radiology_technician_notes

- Purpose: Technologist procedure notes
- Attributes:
    - note_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK)
    - technologist_id (UUID FK)
    - note_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - note_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Technical, 2=Patient, 3=Equipment, 4=Quality
    - note_text (TEXT)
    - images_reference (TEXT[])
    - quality_issues (TEXT[])
    - patient_cooperation (SMALLINT) - 1=Excellent, 2=Good, 3=Fair, 4=Poor
    - note_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Submitted

radiology_pacs_integration

- Purpose: PACS system integration tracking
- Attributes:
    - pacs_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK)
    - study_uid (VARCHAR(100))
    - accession_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - series_count (SMALLINT)
    - image_count (INTEGER)
    - storage_size_mb (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - pacs_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Sent, 2=Received, 3=Archived, 4=Retrieved
    - sent_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - received_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - archived_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - retrieval_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - pacs_server (VARCHAR(100))
    - integration_notes (TEXT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- radiology_orders (1) → radiology_appointments (N)
- radiology_appointments (1) → radiology_procedure_steps (N)
- radiology_appointments (1) → radiology_contrast_usage (N)
- radiology_appointments (1) → radiology_reports (N)
- radiology_appointments (1) → radiology_dose_monitoring (N)
- radiology_appointments (1) → radiology_technician_notes (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- radiology_appointments (N) → radiology_modalities (1)
- radiology_reports (N) → users (1) [radiologist]
- radiology_dose_monitoring (N) → radiology_procedures (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active appointments
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: All clinical domains for diagnostic imaging
- Integrates with: PACS, Billing, Laboratory

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Dose limit validation automated
- Duration calculations generated
- Status transitions validated

Data Types:

- NUMERIC for precise dose tracking
- JSONB for exposure parameters
- Arrays for sequences and references
- Generated columns for business rules

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- radiology_orders: Large (200,000+ annually)
- radiology_appointments: Large (1:1 with orders)
- radiology_reports: Large (1:1 with appointments)
- radiology_dose_monitoring: Medium (dose per procedure)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on radiology_appointments (scheduled_datetime, modality_id)
- Index on radiology_reports (report_datetime, radiologist_id)
- Partial indexes for pending appointments and unreported studies
- GIN index on body_parts and procedure types

Partitioning:

- radiology_orders: Range partitioning by order_datetime (monthly)
- radiology_appointments: Range partitioning by scheduled_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Radiologists: Report generation and verification
- Technologists: Procedure performance and notes
- Schedulers: Appointment management
- Physicists: Dose monitoring and safety

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient privacy for imaging data
- Modality-level access restrictions

Sensitive Data:

- Medical images highly protected
- Radiation dose records secured
- Patient identification in DICOM metadata

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 500+ daily procedures
- Multiple modality types
- Advanced 3D and AI processing
- Cloud PACS integration

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for diagnostic decision making
- High-revenue department
- Radiation safety compliance essential
- Integration with surgical planning

## Domain 15: Billing & Insurance

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-payer Support: Handling of cash, insurance, corporate, and government payers
- Charge Capture: Automated and manual charge capture from clinical activities
- Claim Management: HIPAA-compliant electronic claim submission and tracking
- Payment Posting: Reconciliation of payments and adjustments
- Denial Management: Tracking and appealing claim denials
- Price Master: Comprehensive service pricing with multiple tiers
- Regulatory Compliance: Adherence to local and international billing regulations

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive financial management of healthcare services with insurance processing, payment collection, and revenue cycle management.

Sub-modules:

- Service Catalog & Pricing
- Charge Capture
- Insurance Claim Management
- Payment Processing
- Accounts Receivable
- Denial Management
- Patient Billing
- TPA Management

Entity List:

- service_price_master
- billing_charges
- insurance_claims
- claim_line_items
- insurance_payments
- payment_adjustments
- patient_statements
- insurance_denials
- tpa_contracts
- insurance_eligibility_checks

### 3. Entity Design

service_price_master

- Purpose: Centralized service pricing catalog
- Attributes:
    - service_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - service_code (VARCHAR(50)) - CPT, HCPCS, or internal codes
    - service_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - service_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Consultation, 2=Procedure, 3=Test, 4=Room, 5=Pharmacy
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - base_price (currency_type)
    - insurance_price (currency_type)
    - corporate_price (currency_type)
    - government_price (currency_type)
    - cost_price (currency_type)
    - taxable (BOOLEAN)
    - tax_rate (percentage_type)
    - effective_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - requires_authorization (BOOLEAN)
    - authorization_days (SMALLINT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

billing_charges

- Purpose: Individual service charges capture
- Attributes:
    - charge_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - encounter_id (UUID FK) - OPD visit or IPD admission
    - service_id (UUID FK)
    - service_date (DATE)
    - service_time (TIME)
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_price (currency_type)
    - total_amount (currency_type) - Generated: quantity * unit_price
    - tax_amount (currency_type)
    - net_amount (currency_type)
    - performing_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - charge_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Posted, 3=Cancelled, 4=Adjusted
    - posted_by (UUID FK)
    - posted_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - insurance_billable (BOOLEAN)
    - prior_authorization_number (VARCHAR(100))

insurance_claims

- Purpose: Insurance claim master records
- Attributes:
    - claim_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - insurance_id (UUID FK) - Patient insurance reference
    - claim_number (VARCHAR(50)) - Unique claim identifier
    - claim_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Professional, 2=Institutional, 3=Pharmacy
    - claim_period_start (DATE)
    - claim_period_end (DATE)
    - total_claimed_amount (currency_type)
    - total_approved_amount (currency_type)
    - total_paid_amount (currency_type)
    - submission_date (DATE)
    - submission_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Electronic, 2=Paper
    - payer_claim_number (VARCHAR(100)) - Insurance assigned
    - claim_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Submitted, 3=Accepted, 4=Partially Paid, 5=Paid, 6=Denied, 7=Appealed
    - denial_reason (TEXT)
    - payment_date (DATE)
    - remittance_date (DATE)

claim_line_items

- Purpose: Individual line items within claims
- Attributes:
    - line_item_id (UUID PK)
    - claim_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - charge_id (UUID FK)
    - service_id (UUID FK)
    - service_date (DATE)
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - billed_amount (currency_type)
    - approved_amount (currency_type)
    - paid_amount (currency_type)
    - adjustment_amount (currency_type)
    - adjustment_reason (VARCHAR(100)) - CO, PR, OA, etc.
    - denial_code (VARCHAR(20)) denial_description (TEXT)
    - line_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Billed, 2=Approved, 3=Paid, 4=Denied, 5=Adjusted

insurance_payments

- Purpose: Insurance payment receipts
- Attributes:
    - payment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - claim_id (UUID FK)
    - payment_number (VARCHAR(100)) - EOB/ERA number
    - payment_date (DATE)
    - payment_amount (currency_type)
    - payment_method (SMALLINT) - 1=EFT, 2=Check, 3=Wire
    - check_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - deposit_date (DATE)
    - deposited_by (UUID FK)
    - payment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Received, 2=Deposited, 3=Reconciled
    - reconciliation_notes (TEXT)

payment_adjustments

- Purpose: Payment adjustments and write-offs
- Attributes:
    - adjustment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - claim_id (UUID FK)
    - line_item_id (UUID FK)
    - adjustment_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Contractual, 2=Write-off, 3=Bad Debt, 4=Courtesy
    - adjustment_amount (currency_type)
    - adjustment_reason_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - adjustment_reason_text (TEXT)
    - adjusted_by (UUID FK)
    - adjusted_date (DATE)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approval_date (DATE)

patient_statements

- Purpose: Patient billing statements
- Attributes:
    - statement_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - statement_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - statement_date (DATE)
    - due_date (DATE)
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - insurance_pending (currency_type)
    - patient_balance (currency_type)
    - previous_balance (currency_type)
    - payments_received (currency_type)
    - adjustments (currency_type)
    - new_charges (currency_type)
    - statement_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Generated, 2=Sent, 3=Partially Paid, 4=Paid
    - sent_date (DATE)
    - sent_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Email, 2=Post, 3=Portal
    - reminder_sent (BOOLEAN)
    - reminder_date (DATE)

insurance_denials

- Purpose: Claim denial tracking and management
- Attributes:
    - denial_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - claim_id (UUID FK)
    - line_item_id (UUID FK)
    - denial_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - denial_description (TEXT)
    - denial_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Technical, 2=Medical, 3=Administrative
    - denial_date (DATE)
    - appeal_deadline (DATE)
    - appeal_filed (BOOLEAN)
    - appeal_date (DATE)
    - appeal_reason (TEXT)
    - appeal_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Approved, 3=Denied
    - appeal_approved_amount (currency_type)
    - responsible_department (UUID FK)
    - corrective_action (TEXT)

tpa_contracts

- Purpose: Third Party Administrator contracts
- Attributes:
    - contract_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - tpa_id (UUID FK) - TPA organization
    - contract_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - effective_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - contract_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Full, 2=Partial, 3=Cashless
    - payment_terms_days (SMALLINT)
    - discount_percentage (percentage_type)
    - authorization_required (BOOLEAN)
    - authorization_window_days (SMALLINT)
    - claim_submission_days (SMALLINT)
    - contract_document (BYTEA)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

insurance_eligibility_checks

- Purpose: Insurance eligibility verification
- Attributes:
    - eligibility_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - insurance_id (UUID FK)
    - check_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - eligibility_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Inactive, 3=Pending
    - coverage_details (JSONB)
    - benefits_summary (JSONB)
    - copay_amount (currency_type)
    - deductible_met (currency_type)
    - deductible_remaining (currency_type)
    - out_of_pocket_met (currency_type)
    - out_of_pocket_remaining (currency_type)
    - checked_by (UUID FK)
    - verification_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Electronic, 2=Phone, 3=Portal

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- service_price_master (1) → billing_charges (N)
- billing_charges (1) → claim_line_items (N)
- insurance_claims (1) → claim_line_items (N)
- insurance_claims (1) → insurance_payments (N)
- insurance_claims (1) → insurance_denials (N)
- tpa_contracts (1) → insurance_claims (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- billing_charges (N) → encounters (1) [clinical context]
- insurance_claims (N) → patient_insurance (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for posted charges
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for claim status updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: All clinical domains (charge sources)
- Required by: Finance, Accounting
- Integrates with: Patient Management, Insurance

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Amount calculations automated
- Status transitions validated
- Financial reporting optimized

Data Types:

- currency_type for all financial amounts
- JSONB for insurance benefits
- Generated columns for balances
- Arrays for complex data structures

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- billing_charges: Extremely Large (millions annually)
- insurance_claims: Very Large (hundreds of thousands)
- claim_line_items: Extremely Large (multiple per claim)
- insurance_payments: Large (matches claim volume)

Indexing Strategy:

- Partition billing_charges by service_date (monthly)
- Index on insurance_claims (claim_status, submission_date)
- Index on patient_statements (statement_date, patient_id)
- Partial indexes for pending claims and unpaid statements

Partitioning:

- billing_charges: Range partitioning by service_date (monthly)
- insurance_claims: Range partitioning by submission_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Billing Staff: Charge posting and claim submission
- Cashiers: Payment processing
- Managers: Adjustments and write-offs
- Patients: Statement viewing and payment

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient financial privacy
- Department-level revenue restrictions

Sensitive Data:

- Financial transactions protected
- Insurance contract details confidential
- Patient payment information secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10M+ annual charges
- Multiple insurance payers
- Real-time eligibility checking
- Automated claim submission

Cross-domain Impact:

- Primary revenue cycle management
- Cash flow critical for operations
- Regulatory compliance essential
- Patient satisfaction through transparent billing
