## Domain 16: Inventory & Stores

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-category Inventory: Medical, surgical, pharmaceutical, and general supplies
- FEFO Enforcement: First Expiry First Out for perishable and dated items
- Stock Levels: Automatic reordering based on par levels and consumption patterns
- Multiple Locations: Central store, department substores, and point-of-use inventory
- Vendor Management: Performance tracking and rating system
- Consumption Tracking: Usage linked to departments and patients
- Asset Tagging: Barcode/RFID integration for all inventory items

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive inventory management for medical supplies, equipment, and consumables with automated replenishment and consumption tracking.

Sub-modules:

- Item Master Catalog
- Stock Management
- Inventory Transactions
- Departmental Issuance
- Vendor Management
- Reorder Management
- Inventory Audits

Entity List:

- inventory_items
- item_categories
- inventory_stock
- inventory_transactions
- department_stores
- store_issuances
- inventory_vendors
- purchase_orders
- goods_received_notes
- inventory_audits

### 3. Entity Design

inventory_items

- Purpose: Master item catalog with specifications
- Attributes:
    - item_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - item_code (VARCHAR(50)) - Unique identifier
    - item_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - item_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Medical, 2=Surgical, 3=Pharmaceutical, 4=General, 5=Lab
    - category_id (UUID FK)
    - description (TEXT)
    - unit_of_measure (VARCHAR(30)) - Each, Box, Pack, ml, kg, etc.
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - selling_price (currency_type)
    - min_stock_level (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - max_stock_level (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - reorder_level (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - lead_time_days (SMALLINT)
    - shelf_life_days (SMALLINT)
    - storage_conditions (TEXT)
    - hazard_classification (VARCHAR(100))
    - is_sterile (BOOLEAN)
    - is_critical (BOOLEAN) - Life-saving equipment
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

item_categories

- Purpose: Inventory categorization
- Attributes:
    - category_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - category_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - category_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Drugs, Consumables, Equipment, etc.
    - parent_category_id (UUID FK)
    - abc_classification (CHAR(1)) - A=High value, B=Medium, C=Low
    - inventory_turnover_target (NUMERIC(5,2))
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

inventory_stock

- Purpose: Current stock levels with lot tracking
- Attributes:
    - stock_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - item_id (UUID FK)
    - store_id (UUID FK) - Location reference
    - lot_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - batch_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - manufacture_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - received_date (DATE)
    - current_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - committed_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2)) - Reserved for issues
    - available_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2)) - Generated: current - committed
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - total_value (currency_type) - Generated: current_quantity * unit_cost
    - shelf_location (VARCHAR(100))
    - stock_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Quarantine, 3=Expired, 4=Recall
    - is_available (BOOLEAN) - Generated: available_quantity > 0 AND expiry_date > CURRENT_DATE

inventory_transactions

- Purpose: All stock movements
- Attributes:
    - transaction_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - stock_id (UUID FK)
    - transaction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Receipt, 2=Issue, 3=Adjustment, 4=Transfer, 5=Return
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - total_cost (currency_type) - Generated: quantity * unit_cost
    - reference_number (VARCHAR(100)) - PO, GRN, Issue number
    - transaction_date (DATE)
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - reason (TEXT) - For adjustments/returns
    - balance_after (NUMERIC(10,2)) - Generated
    - department_id (UUID FK) - For issues
    - patient_id (UUID FK) - If patient-specific

department_stores

- Purpose: Departmental substore management
- Attributes:
    - store_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - store_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - store_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - location (VARCHAR(255))
    - store_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Main Store, 2=Substore, 3=Point of Use
    - store_in_charge (UUID FK)
    - contact_extension (VARCHAR(20))
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - par_level_enabled (BOOLEAN)
    - last_audit_date (DATE)
    - next_audit_date (DATE)

store_issuances

- Purpose: Departmental stock issuance
- Attributes:
    - issuance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - requisition_id (UUID FK) - Link to requisition
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - store_id (UUID FK) - Issuing store
    - issuance_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - issuance_date (DATE)
    - requested_by (UUID FK)
    - issued_by (UUID FK)
    - received_by (UUID FK)
    - total_items (SMALLINT)
    - total_value (currency_type)
    - issuance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Requested, 2=Approved, 3=Issued, 4=Received
    - issuance_purpose (TEXT)
    - patient_id (UUID FK) - If patient-specific

inventory_vendors

- Purpose: Supplier and vendor management
- Attributes:
    - vendor_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - vendor_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - vendor_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Manufacturer, 2=Distributor, 3=Local
    - contact_person (VARCHAR(100))
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - contact_email (email_type)
    - address (TEXT)
    - tax_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - payment_terms_days (SMALLINT)
    - delivery_lead_time_days (SMALLINT)
    - quality_rating (percentage_type)
    - on_time_delivery_rate (percentage_type)
    - is_preferred (BOOLEAN)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

purchase_orders

- Purpose: Purchase order management
- Attributes:
    - po_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - po_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - po_date (DATE)
    - expected_delivery_date (DATE)
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - po_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Approved, 3=Sent, 4=Partially Received, 5=Completed, 6=Cancelled
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_date (DATE)
    - terms_and_conditions (TEXT)
    - shipping_address (TEXT)

goods_received_notes

- Purpose: Goods receipt documentation
- Attributes:
    - grn_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - po_id (UUID FK)
    - grn_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - received_date (DATE)
    - received_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - total_items_received (SMALLINT)
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - receipt_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Partial, 2=Complete
    - quality_check_passed (BOOLEAN)
    - quality_notes (TEXT)
    - storage_assigned (BOOLEAN)

inventory_audits

- Purpose: Regular inventory audits
- Attributes:
    - audit_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - store_id (UUID FK)
    - audit_date (DATE)
    - audit_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Cycle Count, 2=Full Physical, 3=Random
    - auditor_id (UUID FK)
    - total_items_audited (SMALLINT)
    - items_matched (SMALLINT)
    - items_variance (SMALLINT)
    - variance_percentage (percentage_type)
    - variance_value (currency_type)
    - audit_status (SMALLINT) - 1=In Progress, 2=Completed, 3=Approved
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_date (DATE)
    - corrective_actions (TEXT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- inventory_items (1) → inventory_stock (N)
- inventory_stock (1) → inventory_transactions (N)
- department_stores (1) → inventory_stock (N)
- inventory_vendors (1) → purchase_orders (N)
- purchase_orders (1) → goods_received_notes (N)
- store_issuances (1) → inventory_transactions (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- inventory_transactions (N) → departments (1) [consumption]
- goods_received_notes (N) → purchase_orders (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active stock
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for quantity updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure, Procurement
- Required by: All clinical domains (supplies consumption)
- Integrates with: Pharmacy, Laboratory, CSSD

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Quantity calculations automated
- Stock status validation
- Financial valuation generated

Data Types:

- NUMERIC for precise quantity tracking
- currency_type for all financial amounts
- Generated columns for availability
- Arrays for complex data structures

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- inventory_items: Medium (10,000-50,000 items)
- inventory_stock: Large (multiple lots per item)
- inventory_transactions: Very Large (all stock movements)
- purchase_orders: Medium (hundreds monthly)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on inventory_stock (expiry_date, available_quantity) for FEFO
- Index on inventory_transactions (transaction_date, item_id)
- Partial indexes for low stock and near-expiry items
- GIN index on item categories and types

Partitioning:

- inventory_transactions: Range partitioning by transaction_date (monthly)
- inventory_stock: Range partitioning by expiry_date (quarterly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Store Managers: Inventory management
- Department Heads: Requisition approval
- Procurement: Vendor management
- Auditors: Inventory verification

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Store-level access restrictions
- Financial data protection

Sensitive Data:

- Cost information restricted
- Vendor pricing confidential
- Inventory valuation protected

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100,000+ inventory items
- Multiple storage locations
- Real-time inventory tracking
- Automated replenishment systems

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for clinical operations
- Major cost center for hospital
- Supply chain efficiency essential
- Patient safety through adequate stock

---
## Domain 17: Procurement

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-stage Process: Requisition → Approval → PO → Receiving → Payment
- Vendor Management: Comprehensive supplier qualification and performance tracking
- Tender Support: Formal tender processes for high-value purchases
- Contract Management: Contract terms, pricing, and compliance tracking
- Budget Control: Departmental budget enforcement and tracking
- Lead Time Management: Supplier performance monitoring for on-time delivery
- Quality Assurance: Goods receipt inspection and quality compliance

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: End-to-end procurement lifecycle management from requisition to payment with vendor management and contract compliance.

Sub-modules:

- Requisition Management
- Purchase Order Processing
- Vendor Management
- Tender Management
- Contract Administration
- Goods Receipt
- Quality Inspection
- Payment Processing

Entity List:

- procurement_requisitions
- requisition_items
- purchase_orders
- po_line_items
- procurement_vendors
- vendor_performance
- tender_notices
- tender_bids
- procurement_contracts
- goods_received_notes
- quality_inspections
- payment_advices

### 3. Entity Design

procurement_requisitions

- Purpose: Purchase requisition initiation
- Attributes:
    - requisition_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - requisition_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - requested_by (UUID FK)
    - request_date (DATE)
    - required_date (DATE)
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Normal, 2=Urgent, 3=Emergency
    - requisition_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Stock, 2=Capital, 3=Consumable, 4=Service
    - budget_code (VARCHAR(100))
    - budget_available (currency_type)
    - total_estimated_amount (currency_type)
    - justification (TEXT)
    - requisition_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Submitted, 3=Approved, 4=Rejected, 5=Converted to PO
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_date (DATE)
    - rejection_reason (TEXT)

requisition_items

- Purpose: Individual items in requisition
- Attributes:
    - requisition_item_id (UUID PK)
    - requisition_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - item_id (UUID FK) - Link to inventory items
    - item_description (VARCHAR(255))
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - estimated_unit_price (currency_type)
    - estimated_total (currency_type) - Generated: quantity * estimated_unit_price
    - specifications (TEXT)
    - preferred_vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - urgent_reason (TEXT)
    - item_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Requested, 2=Approved, 3=Rejected

purchase_orders

- Purpose: Purchase order master
- Attributes:
    - po_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - requisition_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - po_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - po_date (DATE)
    - po_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Local, 2=Import, 3=Service, 4=Capital
    - delivery_terms (VARCHAR(100))
    - payment_terms (VARCHAR(100))
    - incoterms (VARCHAR(20))
    - total_amount (currency_type)
    - tax_amount (currency_type)
    - net_amount (currency_type)
    - expected_delivery_date (DATE)
    - po_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Approved, 3=Sent, 4=Partially Received, 5=Completed, 6=Cancelled
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_date (DATE)
    - sent_date (DATE)
    - sent_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Email, 2=Portal, 3=Post

po_line_items

- Purpose: Individual PO line items
- Attributes:
    - po_line_id (UUID PK)
    - po_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - requisition_item_id (UUID FK)
    - item_id (UUID FK)
    - item_description (VARCHAR(255))
    - quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - unit_price (currency_type)
    - total_price (currency_type) - Generated: quantity * unit_price
    - delivery_date (DATE)
    - received_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - balance_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2)) - Generated: quantity - received_quantity
    - line_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Ordered, 2=Partially Received, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled

procurement_vendors

- Purpose: Extended vendor information
- Attributes:
    - vendor_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - legal_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - trade_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - vendor_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Manufacturer, 2=Distributor, 3=Importer, 4=Local
    - registration_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - tax_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - year_established (SMALLINT)
    - employee_count (INTEGER)
    - quality_certifications (TEXT[]) - ISO, GMP, etc.
    - bank_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - bank_account_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - bank_ifsc_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - credit_limit (currency_type)
    - credit_days (SMALLINT)
    - vendor_category (SMALLINT) - 1=A, 2=B, 3=C based on performance
    - blacklisted (BOOLEAN)
    - blacklist_reason (TEXT)
    - blacklist_date (DATE)

vendor_performance

- Purpose: Vendor performance tracking
- Attributes:
    - performance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - evaluation_period (VARCHAR(20)) - Monthly, Quarterly
    - evaluation_date (DATE)
    - total_orders (INTEGER)
    - total_order_value (currency_type)
    - on_time_delivery_rate (percentage_type)
    - quality_acceptance_rate (percentage_type)
    - price_compliance_rate (percentage_type)
    - documentation_compliance_rate (percentage_type)
    - overall_score (percentage_type)
    - performance_rating (SMALLINT) - 1=Excellent, 2=Good, 3=Fair, 4=Poor
    - improvement_areas (TEXT[])
    - evaluated_by (UUID FK)
    - next_review_date (DATE)

tender_notices

- Purpose: Tender announcement and management
- Attributes:
    - tender_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - tender_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - tender_title (VARCHAR(255))
    - tender_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Open, 2=Limited, 3=Single Source
    - tender_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Goods, 2=Services, 3=Works
    - estimated_value (currency_type)
    - publication_date (DATE)
    - closing_date (DATE)
    - opening_date (DATE)
    - tender_document (BYTEA)
    - terms_conditions (TEXT)
    - eligibility_criteria (TEXT)
    - tender_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Published, 3=Closed, 4=Awarded, 5=Cancelled
    - created_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)

tender_bids

- Purpose: Tender bid submissions
- Attributes:
    - bid_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - tender_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - bid_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - bid_date (DATE)
    - bid_amount (currency_type)
    - bid_validity_days (SMALLINT)
    - delivery_time_days (SMALLINT)
    - technical_score (percentage_type)
    - commercial_score (percentage_type)
    - total_score (percentage_type)
    - bid_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Submitted, 2=Technical Evaluation, 3=Commercial Evaluation, 4=Accepted, 5=Rejected
    - evaluation_notes (TEXT)
    - awarded (BOOLEAN)
    - award_date (DATE)

procurement_contracts

- Purpose: Contract management
- Attributes:
    - contract_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - tender_id (UUID FK)
    - contract_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - contract_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Fixed Price, 2=Time & Material, 3=Rate Contract
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - total_contract_value (currency_type)
    - payment_terms (TEXT)
    - delivery_terms (TEXT)
    - penalty_clause (TEXT)
    - termination_clause (TEXT)
    - contract_document (BYTEA)
    - contract_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Completed, 3=Terminated, 4=Suspended
    - signed_by (UUID FK)
    - signed_date (DATE)
    - reviewed_by (UUID FK)
    - review_date (DATE)

goods_received_notes

- Purpose: Goods receipt documentation
- Attributes:
    - grn_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - po_id (UUID FK)
    - grn_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - received_date (DATE)
    - received_by (UUID FK)
    - total_items (SMALLINT)
    - total_quantity (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - total_value (currency_type)
    - shipping_document_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - transport_details (TEXT)
    - receipt_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Partial, 2=Complete
    - store_id (UUID FK) - Where stored
    - acknowledged_by (UUID FK)

quality_inspections

- Purpose: Quality assurance at receipt
- Attributes:
    - inspection_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - grn_id (UUID FK)
    - inspection_date (DATE)
    - inspector_id (UUID FK)
    - sampling_method (VARCHAR(100))
    - samples_tested (SMALLINT)
    - samples_passed (SMALLINT)
    - samples_failed (SMALLINT)
    - failure_reasons (TEXT[])
    - inspection_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Accepted, 2=Conditionally Accepted, 3=Rejected
    - conditional_acceptance_terms (TEXT)
    - rejection_notes (TEXT)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approval_date (DATE)

payment_advices

- Purpose: Payment processing advice
- Attributes:
    - payment_advice_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - po_id (UUID FK)
    - grn_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - advice_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - advice_date (DATE)
    - total_invoice_amount (currency_type)
    - total_payable_amount (currency_type)
    - deductions (currency_type)
    - net_payable (currency_type)
    - payment_due_date (DATE)
    - payment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Due, 2=Partially Paid, 3=Paid
    - prepared_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - payment_date (DATE)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- procurement_requisitions (1) → requisition_items (N)
- purchase_orders (1) → po_line_items (N)
- procurement_vendors (1) → purchase_orders (N)
- tender_notices (1) → tender_bids (N)
- goods_received_notes (1) → quality_inspections (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- requisition_items (N) → inventory_items (1)
- po_line_items (N) → requisition_items (1)
- tender_bids (N) → procurement_vendors (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for approved requisitions and POs
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Inventory & Stores, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: All operational domains
- Integrates with: Finance, Accounts Payable

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Financial calculations automated
- Status validation enforced
- Performance metrics generated

Data Types:

- currency_type for all financial amounts
- Arrays for certifications and failure reasons
- Generated columns for scores and balances
- BYTEA for document storage

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- purchase_orders: Large (thousands monthly)
- po_line_items: Very Large (multiple items per PO)
- procurement_requisitions: Large (departmental requests)
- vendor_performance: Medium (periodic evaluations)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on purchase_orders (po_status, po_date)
- Index on procurement_requisitions (requisition_status, request_date)
- Partial indexes for pending approvals and active contracts
- GIN index on vendor certifications

Partitioning:

- purchase_orders: Range partitioning by po_date (monthly)
- goods_received_notes: Range partitioning by received_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Requisitioners: Department staff
- Approvers: Department heads and finance
- Procurement Officers: PO processing
- Quality Inspectors: Goods verification

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Departmental budget restrictions
- Vendor data confidentiality

Sensitive Data:

- Vendor bank details protected
- Contract terms confidential
- Pricing information secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 1,000+ active vendors
- Complex tender processes
- Global sourcing capabilities
- Automated contract management

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for supply chain continuity
- Major cost control point
- Quality assurance gateway
- Regulatory compliance essential

## Domain 18: Human Resources & Payroll

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Comprehensive Employee Lifecycle: Recruitment to retirement with complete tracking
- Multi-tier Organization Structure: Support for complex hierarchies and reporting lines
- Attendance Integration: Biometric and manual attendance tracking with shift management
- Payroll Processing: Complex salary structures with deductions, taxes, and benefits
- Compliance Management: Labor law compliance and statutory reporting
- Performance Management: Regular appraisals and competency tracking
- Training & Development: Skills management and training program tracking

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive human capital management with payroll processing, attendance tracking, and performance management.

Sub-modules:

- Employee Master Data
- Organization Structure
- Attendance & Leave Management
- Payroll Processing
- Performance Management
- Training & Development
- Compliance & Reporting

Entity List:

- employees
- employee_job_history
- departments
- designations
- shifts
- attendance_logs
- leave_requests
- payroll_periods
- salary_components
- payroll_processing
- payroll_deductions
- performance_appraisals
- training_programs
- employee_training
- statutory_compliance

### 3. Entity Design

employees

- Purpose: Core employee demographic and employment information
- Attributes:
    - employee_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - employee_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Unique identifier
    - user_id (UUID FK) - Link to users table
    - first_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - last_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - date_of_birth (DATE)
    - gender (SMALLINT) - 1=Male, 2=Female, 3=Other
    - nationality (UUID FK)
    - marital_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Single, 2=Married, 3=Divorced, 4=Widowed
    - date_of_joining (DATE)
    - employment_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Permanent, 2=Contract, 3=Temporary, 4=Consultant
    - employee_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Probation, 3=Suspended, 4=Terminated, 5=Retired
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - designation_id (UUID FK)
    - reporting_manager_id (UUID FK)
    - work_email (email_type)
    - work_phone (phone_type)
    - emergency_contact (VARCHAR(255))
    - emergency_phone (phone_type)
    - blood_group (blood_group_type)
    - biometric_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

employee_job_history

- Purpose: Employment history tracking (SCD Type 2)
- Attributes:
    - job_history_id (UUID PK)
    - employee_id (UUID FK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - designation_id (UUID FK)
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - salary (currency_type)
    - reporting_manager_id (UUID FK)
    - location (VARCHAR(255))
    - employment_status (SMALLINT)
    - reason_for_change (TEXT)
    - is_current (BOOLEAN) - Generated: end_date IS NULL OR end_date > CURRENT_DATE

departments

- Purpose: Organizational structure - departments
- Attributes:
    - department_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - department_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - department_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - parent_department_id (UUID FK)
    - department_head_id (UUID FK)
    - cost_center (VARCHAR(50))
    - budget_amount (currency_type)
    - employee_count (INTEGER) - Generated
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

designations

- Purpose: Job titles and grades
- Attributes:
    - designation_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - designation_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - designation_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - job_grade (VARCHAR(10))
    - pay_band (VARCHAR(20))
    - min_salary (currency_type)
    - max_salary (currency_type)
    - responsibilities (TEXT)
    - qualifications_required (TEXT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

shifts

- Purpose: Work shift definitions
- Attributes:
    - shift_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - shift_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - shift_name (VARCHAR(50)) - Morning, Evening, Night, etc.
    - start_time (TIME)
    - end_time (TIME)
    - duration_hours (NUMERIC(4,2)) - Generated
    - break_duration_minutes (SMALLINT)
    - is_night_shift (BOOLEAN)
    - overtime_eligible (BOOLEAN)
    - shift_allowance (currency_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

attendance_logs

- Purpose: Employee attendance tracking
- Attributes:
    - attendance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - employee_id (UUID FK)
    - attendance_date (DATE)
    - shift_id (UUID FK)
    - scheduled_in_time (TIME)
    - scheduled_out_time (TIME)
    - actual_in_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_out_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - hours_worked (NUMERIC(4,2)) - Generated
    - overtime_hours (NUMERIC(4,2))
    - late_minutes (SMALLINT)
    - early_departure_minutes (SMALLINT)
    - attendance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Present, 2=Absent, 3=Half-day, 4=Leave
    - approved_overtime (BOOLEAN)
    - attendance_regularized (BOOLEAN)
    - regularization_reason (TEXT)
    - regularization_approved_by (UUID FK)

leave_requests

- Purpose: Leave application and approval
- Attributes:
    - leave_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - employee_id (UUID FK)
    - leave_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Casual, 2=Sick, 3=Earned, 4=Maternity, 5=Paternity, 6=Unpaid
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - duration_days (NUMERIC(4,1)) - Generated
    - reason (TEXT)
    - contact_during_leave (phone_type)
    - leave_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Applied, 2=Approved, 3=Rejected, 4=Cancelled
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_date (DATE)
    - rejection_reason (TEXT)
    - medical_certificate_required (BOOLEAN)
    - medical_certificate_submitted (BOOLEAN)

payroll_periods

- Purpose: Payroll cycle management
- Attributes:
    - period_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - period_name (VARCHAR(50)) - April 2024, etc.
    - period_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Monthly, 2=Bi-weekly, 3=Weekly
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - payment_date (DATE)
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Open, 2=Processing, 3=Completed, 4=Locked
    - total_employees (INTEGER)
    - total_gross_salary (currency_type)
    - total_deductions (currency_type)
    - total_net_salary (currency_type)
    - processed_by (UUID FK)
    - processed_date (DATE)

salary_components

- Purpose: Salary structure components
- Attributes:
    - component_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - component_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - component_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Basic, HRA, DA, Conveyance, etc.
    - component_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Earning, 2=Deduction
    - calculation_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Fixed, 2=Percentage, 3=Formula
    - calculation_value (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - taxable (BOOLEAN)
    - statutory (BOOLEAN) - PF, ESI, PT, etc.
    - applies_to_all (BOOLEAN)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

payroll_processing

- Purpose: Individual payroll processing
- Attributes:
    - payroll_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - employee_id (UUID FK)
    - period_id (UUID FK)
    - basic_salary (currency_type)
    - total_earnings (currency_type)
    - total_deductions (currency_type)
    - net_salary (currency_type) - Generated: total_earnings - total_deductions
    - days_payable (NUMERIC(4,2))
    - lop_days (NUMERIC(4,2)) - Loss of pay days
    - attendance_summary (JSONB)
    - leave_summary (JSONB)
    - payroll_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Calculated, 2=Approved, 3=Paid
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - paid_date (DATE)
    - payment_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Bank Transfer, 2=Cash, 3=Cheque

payroll_deductions

- Purpose: Detailed deduction breakdown
- Attributes:
    - deduction_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - payroll_id (UUID FK)
    - component_id (UUID FK)
    - deduction_amount (currency_type)
    - calculation_basis (currency_type)
    - calculation_percentage (percentage_type)
    - statutory_period (VARCHAR(20)) - For statutory deductions
    - deduction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Statutory, 2=Loan, 3=Advance, 4=Other
    - recovery_installment (SMALLINT) - For loans/advances
    - total_installments (SMALLINT)

performance_appraisals

- Purpose: Employee performance evaluation
- Attributes:
    - appraisal_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - employee_id (UUID FK)
    - appraisal_period (VARCHAR(20)) - Q1 2024, Annual 2024, etc.
    - appraisal_date (DATE)
    - appraisal_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Self, 2=Manager, 3=Peer, 4=360-degree
    - technical_rating (percentage_type)
    - behavioral_rating (percentage_type)
    - overall_rating (percentage_type)
    - performance_grade (VARCHAR(5)) - A, B, C, D
    - strengths (TEXT[])
    - improvement_areas (TEXT[])
    - goals_next_period (TEXT[])
    - appraisal_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Reviewed, 3=Accepted, 4=Appealed
    - reviewed_by (UUID FK)
    - employee_comments (TEXT)

training_programs

- Purpose: Training program catalog
- Attributes:
    - program_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - program_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - program_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - program_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Technical, 2=Soft Skills, 3=Compliance, 4=Safety
    - duration_hours (NUMERIC(5,2))
    - trainer_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - trainer_qualifications (TEXT)
    - program_cost (currency_type)
    - max_participants (SMALLINT)
    - program_schedule (JSONB)
    - program_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=Ongoing, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    - is_mandatory (BOOLEAN)

employee_training

- Purpose: Employee training participation
- Attributes:
    - training_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - employee_id (UUID FK)
    - program_id (UUID FK)
    - training_date (DATE)
    - training_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=Attended, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    - attendance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Present, 2=Absent, 3=Partially Attended
    - score_percentage (percentage_type)
    - certificate_issued (BOOLEAN)
    - certificate_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - feedback_rating (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - feedback_comments (TEXT)

statutory_compliance

- Purpose: Labor law compliance tracking
- Attributes:
    - compliance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - compliance_type (SMALLINT) - 1=PF, 2=ESI, 3=PT, 4=Gratuity, 5=LWF
    - period (VARCHAR(20)) - April 2024, etc.
    - due_date (DATE)
    - submission_date (DATE)
    - amount_payable (currency_type)
    - amount_paid (currency_type)
    - payment_date (DATE)
    - challan_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - compliance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Due, 2=Submitted, 3=Paid, 4=Late
    - responsible_person (UUID FK)
    - remarks (TEXT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- employees (1) → employee_job_history (N)
- employees (1) → attendance_logs (N)
- employees (1) → leave_requests (N)
- employees (1) → payroll_processing (N)
- payroll_periods (1) → payroll_processing (N)
- payroll_processing (1) → payroll_deductions (N)
- employees (1) → performance_appraisals (N)
- training_programs (1) → employee_training (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- employees (N) → departments (1)
- employees (N) → designations (1)
- attendance_logs (N) → shifts (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active employees
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for department/designation changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure
- Required by: All operational domains
- Integrates with: Finance, Time Attendance systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Salary calculations automated
- Attendance hours computed
- Status transitions validated

Data Types:

- currency_type for all financial amounts
- JSONB for complex data structures
- Generated columns for calculations
- Arrays for multi-value fields

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- employees: Large (thousands of employees)
- attendance_logs: Very Large (daily records for all employees)
- payroll_processing: Large (monthly records for all employees)
- performance_appraisals: Medium (periodic evaluations)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on employees (employee_status, department_id)
- Index on attendance_logs (attendance_date, employee_id)
- Partial indexes for active records and current period
- GIN index on performance strengths and areas

Partitioning:

- attendance_logs: Range partitioning by attendance_date (monthly)
- payroll_processing: Range partitioning by period_id

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- HR Managers: Employee data management
- Department Heads: Team management
- Employees: Self-service access
- Finance: Payroll processing

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Departmental data segregation
- Personal information protection

Sensitive Data:

- Salary information highly protected
- Personal employee data confidential
- Performance reviews restricted

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10,000+ employees
- Multiple location payroll
- Complex compensation structures
- Advanced analytics on workforce

Cross-domain Impact:

- Largest cost center for hospital
- Critical for regulatory compliance
- Employee satisfaction and retention
- Operational efficiency through proper staffing

## Domain 19: Quality & Safety

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Incident Reporting: Comprehensive adverse event and near-miss reporting
- Root Cause Analysis: Structured RCA methodology for serious incidents
- CAPA Management: Corrective and Preventive Action tracking with effectiveness verification
- Regulatory Compliance: Adherence to NABH, JCI, ISO standards
- Risk Assessment: Proactive risk identification and mitigation
- Patient Safety: Focus on reducing preventable harm
- Continuous Improvement: PDCA cycle implementation across organization

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Systematic quality management and patient safety program with incident tracking, risk assessment, and continuous improvement.

Sub-modules:

- Incident Reporting
- Risk Assessment
- Corrective Actions
- Compliance Tracking
- Quality Indicators
- Safety Audits
- Patient Feedback

Entity List:

- incident_reports
- incident_investigations
- risk_assessments
- corrective_actions
- preventive_actions
- quality_indicators
- indicator_measurements
- safety_audits
- audit_findings
- patient_feedback
- compliance_requirements
- compliance_assessments

### 3. Entity Design

incident_reports

- Purpose: Adverse event and near-miss reporting
- Attributes:
    - incident_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - incident_number (VARCHAR(50)) - Sequential tracking number
    - incident_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Patient Safety, 2=Medication, 3=Fall, 4=Infection, 5=Equipment, 6=Near Miss
    - incident_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Adverse Event, 2=Sentinel Event, 3=Near Miss
    - severity_level (SMALLINT) - 1=No Harm, 2=Minor, 3=Moderate, 4=Major, 5=Catastrophic
    - occurrence_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - discovery_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - location_id (UUID FK) - Department, room, etc.
    - patient_id (UUID FK) - If patient involved
    - staff_involved (UUID[]) - Staff members involved
    - description (TEXT)
    - immediate_actions (TEXT)
    - reported_by (UUID FK)
    - report_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - incident_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Submitted, 3=Under Investigation, 4=Resolved, 5=Closed

incident_investigations

- Purpose: Detailed incident investigation
- Attributes:
    - investigation_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - incident_id (UUID FK)
    - investigation_start_date (DATE)
    - investigation_end_date (DATE)
    - investigation_team (UUID[]) - Team members
    - root_cause_methodology (SMALLINT) - 1=5 Whys, 2=Fishbone, 3=FMEA
    - root_causes (TEXT[])
    - contributing_factors (TEXT[])
    - human_factors (TEXT[]) - Training, fatigue, communication
    - system_factors (TEXT[]) - Process, equipment, environment
    - investigation_summary (TEXT)
    - recommendations (TEXT[])
    - investigation_status (SMALLINT) - 1=In Progress, 2=Completed, 3=Approved
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approval_date (DATE)

risk_assessments

- Purpose: Proactive risk identification and evaluation
- Attributes:
    - risk_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - risk_description (TEXT)
    - risk_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Operational, 3=Financial, 4=Strategic
    - likelihood (SMALLINT) - 1=Rare, 2=Unlikely, 3=Possible, 4=Likely, 5=Certain
    - impact (SMALLINT) - 1=Negligible, 2=Minor, 3=Moderate, 4=Major, 5=Catastrophic
    - risk_score (SMALLINT) - Generated: likelihood * impact
    - risk_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Extreme - Generated from score
    - existing_controls (TEXT[])
    - residual_risk_score (SMALLINT)
    - risk_owner (UUID FK)
    - assessment_date (DATE)
    - next_assessment_date (DATE)
    - risk_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Identified, 2=Assessed, 3=Mitigated, 4=Monitored

corrective_actions

- Purpose: Corrective actions from incidents and audits
- Attributes:
    - action_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - incident_id (UUID FK) - Source incident
    - audit_finding_id (UUID FK) - Source audit finding
    - action_description (TEXT)
    - action_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Immediate, 2=Corrective, 3=Preventive
    - responsible_person (UUID FK)
    - target_completion_date (DATE)
    - actual_completion_date (DATE)
    - resources_required (TEXT)
    - action_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Delayed, 5=Verification Pending
    - effectiveness_verification (BOOLEAN)
    - verification_date (DATE)
    - verification_method (TEXT)
    - verification_results (TEXT)
    - is_effective (BOOLEAN)

preventive_actions

- Purpose: Proactive preventive measures
- Attributes:
    - preventive_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - risk_id (UUID FK) - Source risk
    - action_description (TEXT)
    - objective (TEXT)
    - implementation_plan (TEXT)
    - responsible_person (UUID FK)
    - implementation_date (DATE)
    - monitoring_frequency (SMALLINT) - 1=Daily, 2=Weekly, 3=Monthly, 4=Quarterly
    - monitoring_metrics (TEXT[])
    - action_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=Implemented, 3=Monitoring, 4=Retired
    - effectiveness (SMALLINT) - 1=Effective, 2=Partially Effective, 3=Ineffective

quality_indicators

- Purpose: Quality performance measurement
- Attributes:
    - indicator_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - indicator_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - indicator_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - indicator_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Operational, 3=Patient Safety, 4=Service
    - numerator_definition (TEXT)
    - denominator_definition (TEXT)
    - measurement_unit (VARCHAR(50))
    - target_value (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - benchmark_value (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - measurement_frequency (SMALLINT) - 1=Daily, 2=Weekly, 3=Monthly, 4=Quarterly
    - responsible_department (UUID FK)
    - data_source (TEXT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

indicator_measurements

- Purpose: Quality indicator data collection
- Attributes:
    - measurement_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - indicator_id (UUID FK)
    - measurement_period (VARCHAR(20)) - April 2024, Q2 2024, etc.
    - measurement_date (DATE)
    - numerator_value (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - denominator_value (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - actual_value (NUMERIC(10,2)) - Generated: numerator/denominator
    - target_achievement (percentage_type) - Generated: (actual_value/target_value)*100
    - data_quality (SMALLINT) - 1=Excellent, 2=Good, 3=Fair, 4=Poor
    - measured_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verification_date (DATE)
    - comments (TEXT)

safety_audits

- Purpose: Safety and quality audits
- Attributes:
    - audit_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - audit_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - audit_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Internal, 2=External, 3=Regulatory, 4=Special
    - audit_standard (VARCHAR(100)) - NABH, JCI, ISO, etc.
    - audit_scope (TEXT)
    - audit_date (DATE)
    - auditor_names (TEXT[])
    - audited_department (UUID FK)
    - total_findings (SMALLINT)
    - major_findings (SMALLINT)
    - minor_findings (SMALLINT)
    - audit_score (percentage_type)
    - audit_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Reported
    - report_date (DATE)
    - next_audit_date (DATE)

audit_findings

- Purpose: Detailed audit findings
- Attributes:
    - finding_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - audit_id (UUID FK)
    - finding_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Major Non-conformity, 2=Minor Non-conformity, 3=Observation, 4=Opportunity
    - clause_reference (VARCHAR(100)) - Standard clause
    - finding_description (TEXT)
    - evidence (TEXT)
    - root_cause (TEXT)
    - recommendation (TEXT)
    - responsible_person (UUID FK)
    - target_date (DATE)
    - finding_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Open, 2=In Progress, 3=Resolved, 4=Verified
    - verification_date (DATE)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)

patient_feedback

- Purpose: Patient satisfaction and feedback
- Attributes:
    - feedback_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - encounter_id (UUID FK) - OPD visit or IPD admission
    - feedback_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Complaint, 2=Compliment, 3=Suggestion
    - feedback_date (DATE)
    - feedback_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical Care, 2=Service, 3=Facility, 4=Administrative
    - feedback_details (TEXT)
    - severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High
    - response_required (BOOLEAN)
    - response_provided (TEXT)
    - response_date (DATE)
    - responded_by (UUID FK)
    - patient_satisfaction (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - feedback_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Received, 2=Under Review, 3=Addressed, 4=Closed

compliance_requirements

- Purpose: Regulatory compliance requirements
- Attributes:
    - requirement_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - regulation_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - requirement_description (TEXT)
    - applicable_department (UUID FK)
    - compliance_frequency (SMALLINT) - 1=One-time, 2=Annual, 3=Quarterly, 4=Monthly
    - due_date (DATE)
    - documentation_required (TEXT)
    - responsible_person (UUID FK)
    - requirement_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Completed, 3=Archived
    - last_compliance_date (DATE)
    - next_due_date (DATE)

compliance_assessments

- Purpose: Compliance verification
- Attributes:
    - assessment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - requirement_id (UUID FK)
    - assessment_date (DATE)
    - assessment_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Document Review, 2=Observation, 3=Interview, 4=Test
    - assessment_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Compliant, 2=Partially Compliant, 3=Non-compliant
    - findings (TEXT)
    - evidence (TEXT)
    - assessed_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - verification_date (DATE)
    - follow_up_required (BOOLEAN)
    - follow_up_actions (TEXT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- incident_reports (1) → incident_investigations (N)
- incident_investigations (1) → corrective_actions (N)
- safety_audits (1) → audit_findings (N)
- quality_indicators (1) → indicator_measurements (N)
- compliance_requirements (1) → compliance_assessments (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- corrective_actions (N) → incident_reports (1)
- audit_findings (N) → safety_audits (1)
- indicator_measurements (N) → quality_indicators (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for completed investigations
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: All clinical and operational domains
- Required by: Regulatory compliance
- Integrates with: Risk Management, Patient Safety

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with generated columns

- Risk scores calculated automatically
- Compliance status tracked
- Indicator measurements computed

Data Types:

- Arrays for multi-value fields
- Generated columns for calculations
- TEXT for detailed descriptions
- JSONB for complex data structures

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- incident_reports: Medium (hundreds monthly)
- indicator_measurements: Large (regular measurements)
- safety_audits: Medium (periodic audits)
- patient_feedback: Medium (patient volume dependent)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on incident_reports (incident_status, occurrence_datetime)
- Index on quality_indicators (indicator_type, is_active)
- Partial indexes for open actions and pending verifications
- GIN index on root causes and factors

Partitioning:

- indicator_measurements: Range partitioning by measurement_date (monthly)
- incident_reports: Range partitioning by occurrence_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Quality Managers: Incident investigation and oversight
- Department Heads: Action implementation
- Staff: Incident reporting
- Patients: Feedback submission

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Department-level incident visibility
- Confidential incident protection

Sensitive Data:

- Patient safety incidents protected
- Staff performance issues confidential
- Regulatory compliance data secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for multiple accreditation standards
- Advanced analytics on quality trends
- Real-time incident monitoring
- Predictive risk modeling

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for patient safety
- Essential for regulatory compliance
- Drives continuous improvement
- Enhances organizational reputation
---

## Domain 20: Compliance & Audit

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-standard Compliance: Support for HIPAA, GDPR, NABH, JCI, ISO standards
- Immutable Audit Logs: Write-once read-many audit trails for all critical operations
- Consent Management: Comprehensive patient consent tracking with versioning
- Data Access Monitoring: Real-time monitoring of PHI/PII access
- Retention Policies: Automated data retention and archival processes
- Security Incident Response: Security breach detection and response tracking
- Policy Management: Organizational policy version control and acknowledgment

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive regulatory compliance management with audit trails, consent tracking, and security monitoring.

Sub-modules:

- Audit Logging
- Consent Management
- Data Access Monitoring
- Retention Management
- Security Incident Response
- Policy Management
- Regulatory Compliance Tracking

Entity List:

- audit_logs
- consent_records
- data_access_logs
- data_retention_policies
- security_incidents
- security_incident_response
- compliance_policies
- policy_acknowledgments
- regulatory_requirements
- compliance_assessments

### 3. Entity Design

audit_logs

- Purpose: Immutable audit trail for all critical operations
- Attributes:
    - log_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - table_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Affected table
    - record_id (UUID) - Affected record
    - operation_type (SMALLINT) - 1=INSERT, 2=UPDATE, 3=DELETE, 4=SELECT
    - old_values (JSONB) - Before change (for UPDATE)
    - new_values (JSONB) - After change
    - changed_columns (TEXT[]) - Columns modified
    - user_id (UUID FK) - Who performed the action
    - user_ip (INET)
    - user_session_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - application_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - log_timestamp (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - transaction_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - operation_context (TEXT) - Business context
    - is_sensitive (BOOLEAN) - PHI/PII involvement
    - log_level (SMALLINT) - 1=INFO, 2=WARNING, 3=ERROR, 4=SECURITY

consent_records

- Purpose: Patient consent management with version control
- Attributes:
    - consent_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - consent_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Treatment, 2=Research, 3=Data Sharing, 4=Marketing
    - consent_version (VARCHAR(20))
    - consent_language (VARCHAR(50))
    - consent_text (TEXT) - Full consent content
    - consent_summary (TEXT) - Patient-friendly summary
    - granted (BOOLEAN)
    - granted_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - granted_location (VARCHAR(255))
    - granted_ip (INET)
    - witness_id (UUID FK) - If witnessed
    - revocation_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - revocation_reason (TEXT)
    - revocation_ip (INET)
    - valid_until (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN) - Generated: granted AND NOT revoked AND valid_until > NOW()
    - related_encounter_id (UUID FK)

data_access_logs

- Purpose: PHI/PII access monitoring
- Attributes:
    - access_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - accessed_table (VARCHAR(100))
    - accessed_record_id (UUID)
    - access_type (SMALLINT) - 1=View, 2=Export, 3=Print, 4=Modify
    - access_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - access_ip (INET)
    - access_device (VARCHAR(100))
    - access_reason (TEXT)
    - accessed_columns (TEXT[])
    - is_authorized (BOOLEAN)
    - authorization_rule (VARCHAR(100))
    - data_sensitivity_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Public, 2=Internal, 3=Confidential, 4=Restricted
    - access_duration_ms (INTEGER) - For query performance
    - result_set_size (INTEGER) - Number of records returned

data_retention_policies

- Purpose: Data retention and archival rules
- Attributes:
    - policy_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - table_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - data_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Financial, 3=Operational, 4=Audit
    - retention_period_years (SMALLINT)
    - retention_trigger (SMALLINT) - 1=Creation, 2=Last Update, 3=Event Date
    - archival_required (BOOLEAN)
    - archival_period_years (SMALLINT)
    - disposal_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Secure Delete, 2=Physical Destruction, 3=Archive
    - legal_hold (BOOLEAN)
    - legal_hold_reason (TEXT)
    - legal_hold_until (DATE)
    - policy_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Suspended, 3=Retired
    - last_review_date (DATE)
    - next_review_date (DATE)

security_incidents

- Purpose: Security incident reporting
- Attributes:
    - incident_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - incident_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - incident_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Data Breach, 2=Unauthorized Access, 3=Malware, 4=Physical Security
    - severity_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Critical
    - detection_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - occurrence_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - affected_systems (TEXT[])
    - affected_data (TEXT[])
    - affected_patients (INTEGER)
    - initial_description (TEXT)
    - reported_by (UUID FK)
    - report_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - incident_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Reported, 2=Under Investigation, 3=Contained, 4=Resolved
    - regulatory_report_required (BOOLEAN)
    - regulatory_report_date (DATE)

security_incident_response

- Purpose: Incident response actions
- Attributes:
    - response_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - incident_id (UUID FK)
    - response_action (SMALLINT) - 1=Containment, 2=Eradication, 3=Recovery, 4=Notification
    - action_description (TEXT)
    - responsible_team (UUID[])
    - start_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - completion_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - action_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Failed
    - effectiveness_rating (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - lessons_learned (TEXT)
    - preventive_measures (TEXT[])

compliance_policies

- Purpose: Organizational policy management
- Attributes:
    - policy_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - policy_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - policy_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - policy_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Security, 2=Privacy, 3=Clinical, 4=Operational
    - policy_version (VARCHAR(20))
    - effective_date (DATE)
    - review_date (DATE)
    - policy_content (TEXT)
    - applicable_departments (UUID[])
    - applicable_roles (UUID[])
    - training_required (BOOLEAN)
    - training_frequency_months (SMALLINT)
    - policy_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Active, 3=Under Review, 4=Retired
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - approval_date (DATE)

policy_acknowledgments

- Purpose: Staff policy acknowledgment tracking
- Attributes:
    - acknowledgment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - policy_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK)
    - acknowledgment_date (DATE)
    - acknowledgment_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Digital, 2=Physical, 3=Training
    - acknowledgment_ip (INET)
    - quiz_score (percentage_type) - If training involved
    - next_acknowledgment_date (DATE)
    - acknowledgment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Acknowledged, 3=Overdue

regulatory_requirements

- Purpose: External regulatory requirements
- Attributes:
    - requirement_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - regulation_standard (VARCHAR(100)) - HIPAA, GDPR, NABH, etc.
    - requirement_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - requirement_description (TEXT)
    - requirement_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Technical, 2=Administrative, 3=Physical
    - implementation_deadline (DATE)
    - compliance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Not Started, 2=In Progress, 3=Compliant, 4=Non-compliant
    - evidence_required (TEXT)
    - responsible_department (UUID FK)
    - responsible_manager (UUID FK)
    - last_audit_date (DATE)
    - next_audit_date (DATE)

compliance_assessments

- Purpose: Regulatory compliance verification
- Attributes:
    - assessment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - requirement_id (UUID FK)
    - assessment_date (DATE)
    - assessment_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Document Review, 2=Testing, 3=Interview
    - assessment_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Compliant, 2=Partially Compliant, 3=Non-compliant
    - findings (TEXT)
    - evidence_references (TEXT[])
    - assessor_id (UUID FK)
    - validation_date (DATE)
    - validator_id (UUID FK)
    - gap_analysis (TEXT)
    - corrective_actions (TEXT[])

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- security_incidents (1) → security_incident_response (N)
- compliance_policies (1) → policy_acknowledgments (N)
- regulatory_requirements (1) → compliance_assessments (N)
- data_retention_policies (1) → audit_logs (N) [indirect via table_name]

N:1 Relationships:

- data_access_logs (N) → patients (1)
- policy_acknowledgments (N) → users (1)
- compliance_assessments (N) → regulatory_requirements (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for compliance records
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for policy updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: All domains (audit sources)
- Required by: Regulatory bodies
- Integrates with: Security, Quality, Legal

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with strategic denormalization

- Immutable audit logs (append-only)
- Versioned policies and consents
- Compliance status tracking

Data Types:

- JSONB for flexible data storage
- Arrays for multi-value fields
- INET for IP address tracking
- Generated columns for status calculations

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- audit_logs: Extremely Large (millions of entries)
- data_access_logs: Very Large (all PHI access)
- policy_acknowledgments: Large (all staff × policies)
- compliance_assessments: Medium (periodic assessments)

Indexing Strategy:

- Partition audit_logs by log_timestamp (daily)
- Index on data_access_logs (access_datetime, patient_id)
- Partial indexes for active policies and pending acknowledgments
- GIN index on JSONB columns for searching

Partitioning:

- audit_logs: Range partitioning by log_timestamp (daily)
- data_access_logs: Range partitioning by access_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Compliance Officers: Policy management
- IT Security: Incident response
- Staff: Policy acknowledgment
- Auditors: Read-only access to logs

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Sensitive log data protection
- Department-based policy access

Sensitive Data:

- Audit logs highly protected
- Security incident details confidential
- Patient consent records secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100M+ audit logs
- Multiple regulatory standards
- Real-time security monitoring
- Automated compliance reporting

Cross-domain Impact:

- Essential for regulatory compliance
- Critical for data security
- Foundation for trust and reputation
- Legal and financial risk mitigation

## Domain 21: Patient Engagement & CRM

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-channel Engagement: Support for portal, mobile app, SMS, email, and in-person interactions
- 360° Patient View: Unified patient profile across all touchpoints
- Marketing Automation: Campaign management with segmentation and personalization
- Service Request Management: Patient requests and complaint resolution tracking
- Health Education: Educational content delivery and tracking
- Loyalty Programs: Patient retention and loyalty initiatives
- Referral Management: Physician and partner referral tracking

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive patient relationship management with engagement tracking, marketing automation, and service request management.

Sub-modules:

- Patient Portal Management
- Communication Tracking
- Marketing Campaigns
- Service Requests
- Health Education
- Loyalty Programs
- Referral Management

Entity List:

- patient_portal_users
- patient_communications
- marketing_campaigns
- campaign_segments
- campaign_responses
- patient_service_requests
- health_education_content
- content_access_logs
- patient_loyalty_programs
- referral_sources
- patient_referrals

### 3. Entity Design

patient_portal_users

- Purpose: Patient portal authentication and preferences
- Attributes:
    - portal_user_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - username (VARCHAR(100))
    - password_hash (BYTEA)
    - last_login (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - login_count (INTEGER)
    - account_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Inactive, 3=Locked, 4=Pending
    - communication_preferences (JSONB) - Preferred channels, frequency
    - notification_preferences (JSONB) - Appointment reminders, results, etc.
    - language_preference (VARCHAR(10))
    - accessibility_preferences (JSONB) - Font size, contrast, etc.
    - terms_accepted (BOOLEAN)
    - terms_accepted_date (DATE)
    - privacy_settings (JSONB)

patient_communications

- Purpose: All patient communication tracking
- Attributes:
    - communication_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - communication_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Email, 2=SMS, 3=Push, 4=Phone, 5=Portal
    - direction (SMALLINT) - 1=Outbound, 2=Inbound
    - subject (VARCHAR(255))
    - message_body (TEXT)
    - sender_id (UUID FK) - Staff or system
    - recipient_address (VARCHAR(255)) - Email, phone, etc.
    - sent_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - delivered_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - read_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - delivery_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Sent, 2=Delivered, 3=Read, 4=Failed
    - failure_reason (TEXT)
    - related_encounter_id (UUID FK)
    - template_id (UUID FK) - If template-based

marketing_campaigns

- Purpose: Marketing campaign management
- Attributes:
    - campaign_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - campaign_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - campaign_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Acquisition, 2=Retention, 3=Win-back, 4=Education
    - target_audience (SMALLINT) - 1=All Patients, 2=Segment, 3=New, 4=Existing
    - channel (SMALLINT) - 1=Email, 2=SMS, 3=Social, 4=Direct Mail
    - budget (currency_type)
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - expected_reach (INTEGER)
    - actual_reach (INTEGER)
    - conversion_rate (percentage_type)
    - roi (percentage_type)
    - campaign_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Scheduled, 3=Running, 4=Completed, 5=Paused
    - created_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)

campaign_segments

- Purpose: Patient segmentation for campaigns
- Attributes:
    - segment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - campaign_id (UUID FK)
    - segment_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - segment_criteria (JSONB) - Age, gender, conditions, etc.
    - segment_size (INTEGER)
    - exclusion_criteria (JSONB)
    - created_by (UUID FK)
    - last_updated (TIMESTAMPTZ)

campaign_responses

- Purpose: Campaign response tracking
- Attributes:
    - response_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - campaign_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - response_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Click, 2=Reply, 3=Conversion, 4=Unsubscribe
    - response_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - response_details (JSONB)
    - converted_to_encounter (BOOLEAN)
    - encounter_id (UUID FK)
    - revenue_generated (currency_type)

patient_service_requests

- Purpose: Patient service request management
- Attributes:
    - request_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - request_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Complaint, 2=Compliment, 3=Suggestion, 4=Information
    - request_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Billing, 3=Administrative, 4=Technical
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Emergency
    - description (TEXT)
    - requested_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - assigned_to (UUID FK)
    - sla_deadline (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - resolution (TEXT)
    - resolved_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - satisfaction_rating (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - request_status (SMALLINT) - 1=New, 2=Assigned, 3=In Progress, 4=Resolved, 5=Closed

health_education_content

- Purpose: Educational content repository
- Attributes:
    - content_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - content_title (VARCHAR(255))
    - content_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Article, 2=Video, 3=Infographic, 4=PDF
    - medical_condition (VARCHAR(100))
    - specialty (UUID FK)
    - content_body (TEXT)
    - media_url (VARCHAR(500))
    - duration_minutes (SMALLINT) - For videos
    - reading_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Basic, 2=Intermediate, 3=Advanced
    - language (VARCHAR(10))
    - version (VARCHAR(20))
    - published_date (DATE)
    - review_date (DATE)
    - author (VARCHAR(100))
    - reviewer (VARCHAR(100))
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

content_access_logs

- Purpose: Content access tracking
- Attributes:
    - access_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - content_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - access_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - access_duration_seconds (INTEGER)
    - completion_status (percentage_type)
    - device_type (VARCHAR(50))
    - rating (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - feedback (TEXT)
    - shared_with (UUID[]) - Other patients shared with

patient_loyalty_programs

- Purpose: Patient loyalty and retention programs
- Attributes:
    - loyalty_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - program_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - enrollment_date (DATE)
    - points_balance (INTEGER)
    - points_earned (INTEGER)
    - points_redeemed (INTEGER)
    - tier_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Bronze, 2=Silver, 3=Gold, 4=Platinum
    - benefits_available (JSONB)
    - last_activity_date (DATE)
    - program_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Inactive, 3=Suspended

referral_sources

- Purpose: Referral source management
- Attributes:
    - source_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - source_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Physician, 2=Hospital, 3=Corporate, 4=Online
    - source_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - contact_person (VARCHAR(100))
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - contact_email (email_type)
    - address (TEXT)
    - referral_rate (percentage_type) - Conversion rate
    - total_referrals (INTEGER)
    - active_referral_agreement (BOOLEAN)
    - agreement_start_date (DATE)
    - agreement_end_date (DATE)
    - commission_structure (JSONB)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

patient_referrals

- Purpose: Patient referral tracking
- Attributes:
    - referral_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - source_id (UUID FK)
    - referral_date (DATE)
    - referral_reason (TEXT)
    - referral_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Received, 2=Contacted, 3=Scheduled, 4=Converted, 5=Lost
    - first_encounter_date (DATE)
    - conversion_date (DATE)
    - revenue_generated (currency_type)
    - commission_paid (currency_type)
    - commission_paid_date (DATE)
    - referral_notes (TEXT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- marketing_campaigns (1) → campaign_segments (N)
- marketing_campaigns (1) → campaign_responses (N)
- health_education_content (1) → content_access_logs (N)
- referral_sources (1) → patient_referrals (N)
- patients (1) → patient_service_requests (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- campaign_responses (N) → patients (1)
- content_access_logs (N) → patients (1)
- patient_referrals (N) → referral_sources (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active campaigns
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for patient updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Marketing, Patient Services
- Integrates with: Clinical, Billing, Appointment

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with JSONB for flexible data

- Communication preferences in JSONB
- Segmentation criteria in JSONB
- Campaign responses with detailed tracking

Data Types:

- JSONB for flexible preferences and criteria
- Arrays for multi-value fields
- Generated columns for calculated metrics
- currency_type for financial tracking

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- patient_communications: Very Large (millions of messages)
- campaign_responses: Large (campaign volume dependent)
- content_access_logs: Large (content engagement tracking)
- patient_service_requests: Medium (service demand dependent)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on patient_communications (patient_id, sent_datetime)
- Index on campaign_responses (campaign_id, response_datetime)
- Partial indexes for active campaigns and open requests
- GIN index on JSONB columns for segmentation

Partitioning:

- patient_communications: Range partitioning by sent_datetime (monthly)
- content_access_logs: Range partitioning by access_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Marketing Staff: Campaign management
- Patient Services: Request handling
- Patients: Portal self-service
- Managers: Analytics and reporting

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient data privacy enforcement
- Department-based campaign access

Sensitive Data:

- Patient communication history protected
- Marketing strategies confidential
- Referral commission details secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 1M+ patient communications monthly
- Advanced segmentation and personalization
- AI-driven engagement recommendations
- Omni-channel experience integration

Cross-domain Impact:

- Drives patient acquisition and retention
- Enhances patient satisfaction
- Generates revenue through conversions
- Builds brand loyalty and reputation
