## Domain 22: Data Warehouse & BI

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Star Schema Design: Optimized for analytical queries with facts and dimensions
- ETL Processes: Automated extraction, transformation, and loading from operational databases
- Historical Tracking: Support for Type 2 slowly changing dimensions
- Aggregate Tables: Pre-computed aggregates for performance optimization
- Multiple Data Sources: Integration from all operational domains
- Real-time Analytics: Near real-time data refresh capabilities
- Self-service BI: Support for ad-hoc querying and reporting

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Centralized data warehouse for business intelligence, reporting, and analytics across all hospital domains.

Sub-modules:

- Dimensional Modeling
- ETL Processes
- Fact Tables
- Dimension Tables
- Aggregate Tables
- Data Marts
- Report Definitions

Entity List:

- dw_fact_billing
- dw_fact_clinical_encounters
- dw_fact_lab_results
- dw_fact_pharmacy_dispensing
- dw_fact_operational_metrics
- dw_dim_patient
- dw_dim_time
- dw_dim_doctor
- dw_dim_department
- dw_dim_facility
- dw_etl_logs
- dw_report_definitions

### 3. Entity Design

dw_fact_billing

- Purpose: Financial and billing metrics
- Attributes:
    - fact_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - time_id (UUID FK) - Date dimension
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - facility_id (UUID FK)
    - encounter_id (UUID) - Source encounter
    - total_charges (currency_type)
    - total_payments (currency_type)
    - total_adjustments (currency_type)
    - outstanding_amount (currency_type)
    - insurance_claims_submitted (INTEGER)
    - claims_approved (INTEGER)
    - claims_denied (INTEGER)
    - average_collection_days (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - revenue_by_service_type (JSONB) - Breakdown by service category
    - load_batch_id (UUID FK) - ETL batch reference

dw_fact_clinical_encounters

- Purpose: Clinical activity metrics
- Attributes:
    - fact_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - time_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - facility_id (UUID FK)
    - encounter_type (SMALLINT) - 1=OPD, 2=IPD, 3=Emergency, 4=Day Care
    - encounter_count (INTEGER)
    - average_encounter_duration (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - readmission_count (INTEGER)
    - mortality_count (INTEGER)
    - complication_count (INTEGER)
    - procedure_count (INTEGER)
    - diagnosis_distribution (JSONB) - Top diagnoses
    - length_of_stay_days (NUMERIC(5,1)) - For IPD
    - load_batch_id (UUID FK)

dw_fact_lab_results

- Purpose: Laboratory performance metrics
- Attributes:
    - fact_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - time_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - facility_id (UUID FK)
    - test_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Chemistry, 2=Hematology, 3=Microbiology, 4=Immunology
    - test_count (INTEGER)
    - abnormal_result_count (INTEGER)
    - critical_result_count (INTEGER)
    - average_tat_minutes (NUMERIC(6,1)) - Turnaround time
    - reagent_consumption (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - quality_control_passed (INTEGER)
    - quality_control_failed (INTEGER)
    - load_batch_id (UUID FK)

dw_fact_pharmacy_dispensing

- Purpose: Pharmacy operations metrics
- Attributes:
    - fact_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - time_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - facility_id (UUID FK)
    - prescription_count (INTEGER)
    - items_dispensed (INTEGER)
    - total_dispensed_value (currency_type)
    - controlled_substances_count (INTEGER)
    - stock_out_events (INTEGER)
    - average_fill_time_minutes (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - drug_interaction_alerts (INTEGER)
    - compliance_rate (percentage_type)
    - load_batch_id (UUID FK)

dw_fact_operational_metrics

- Purpose: Hospital operational efficiency
- Attributes:
    - fact_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - time_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - facility_id (UUID FK)
    - bed_occupancy_rate (percentage_type)
    - average_length_of_stay (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - turnover_interval (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - staff_to_patient_ratio (NUMERIC(5,2))
    - equipment_utilization (percentage_type)
    - maintenance_downtime_hours (NUMERIC(8,2))
    - patient_wait_times (JSONB) - By department
    - resource_utilization (JSONB) - Rooms, equipment, staff
    - load_batch_id (UUID FK)

dw_dim_patient

- Purpose: Patient demographic dimensions (Type 2 SCD)
- Attributes:
    - patient_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_key (UUID) - Surrogate key
    - mrn (VARCHAR(20))
    - age (INTEGER)
    - age_group (VARCHAR(20))
    - gender (SMALLINT)
    - blood_group (blood_group_type)
    - marital_status (SMALLINT)
    - insurance_type (SMALLINT)
    - geographic_region (VARCHAR(100))
    - chronic_conditions (TEXT[])
    - risk_score (NUMERIC(5,2))
    - patient_segment (VARCHAR(50))
    - valid_from (DATE)
    - valid_to (DATE)
    - is_current (BOOLEAN)

dw_dim_time

- Purpose: Time dimension for analytics
- Attributes:
    - time_id (UUID PK)
    - full_date (DATE)
    - day_of_week (SMALLINT) - 1=Sunday, 2=Monday, etc.
    - day_name (VARCHAR(10))
    - day_of_month (SMALLINT)
    - day_of_year (SMALLINT)
    - week_of_year (SMALLINT)
    - month (SMALLINT)
    - month_name (VARCHAR(10))
    - quarter (SMALLINT)
    - year (SMALLINT)
    - is_weekend (BOOLEAN)
    - is_holiday (BOOLEAN)
    - holiday_name (VARCHAR(50))
    - fiscal_period (VARCHAR(20))

dw_dim_doctor

- Purpose: Healthcare provider dimensions
- Attributes:
    - doctor_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - doctor_key (UUID) - Surrogate key
    - doctor_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - specialty (VARCHAR(100))
    - qualification (VARCHAR(100))
    - experience_years (INTEGER)
    - department (VARCHAR(100))
    - performance_rating (percentage_type)
    - patient_satisfaction_score (NUMERIC(4,2))
    - case_complexity_index (NUMERIC(5,2))
    - valid_from (DATE)
    - valid_to (DATE)
    - is_current (BOOLEAN)

dw_dim_department

- Purpose: Departmental dimensions
- Attributes:
    - department_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - department_key (UUID) - Surrogate key
    - department_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - department_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - department_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Non-Clinical
    - cost_center (VARCHAR(50))
    - budget_amount (currency_type)
    - staff_count (INTEGER)
    - bed_count (INTEGER)
    - service_lines (TEXT[])
    - performance_metrics (JSONB)
    - valid_from (DATE)
    - valid_to (DATE)
    - is_current (BOOLEAN)

dw_dim_facility

- Purpose: Facility and location dimensions
- Attributes:
    - facility_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - facility_key (UUID) - Surrogate key
    - facility_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - facility_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - facility_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Hospital, 2=Clinic, 3=Diagnostic Center
    - location (VARCHAR(255))
    - city (VARCHAR(100))
    - state (VARCHAR(100))
    - country (VARCHAR(100))
    - bed_capacity (INTEGER)
    - accreditation_status (VARCHAR(50))
    - operational_status (SMALLINT)
    - valid_from (DATE)
    - valid_to (DATE)
    - is_current (BOOLEAN)

dw_etl_logs

- Purpose: ETL process monitoring
- Attributes:
    - etl_log_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - process_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - source_system (VARCHAR(50))
    - start_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - end_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - duration_seconds (INTEGER)
    - records_processed (INTEGER)
    - records_inserted (INTEGER)
    - records_updated (INTEGER)
    - records_deleted (INTEGER)
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Success, 2=Warning, 3=Error
    - error_message (TEXT)
    - load_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Full, 2=Incremental
    - batch_id (UUID)

dw_report_definitions

- Purpose: Report template management
- Attributes:
    - report_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - report_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - report_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Financial, 3=Operational, 4=Quality
    - report_frequency (SMALLINT) - 1=Daily, 2=Weekly, 3=Monthly, 4=Quarterly
    - data_sources (TEXT[])
    - report_parameters (JSONB)
    - report_definition (JSONB) - Columns, filters, sorts
    - delivery_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Email, 2=Portal, 3=Export
    - recipient_list (TEXT[])
    - last_generated (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - next_schedule (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - created_by (UUID FK)
    - updated_by (UUID FK)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

Star Schema Relationships:

- All fact tables → dw_dim_time (1)
- All fact tables → dw_dim_patient (1)
- All fact tables → dw_dim_doctor (1)
- All fact tables → dw_dim_department (1)
- All fact tables → dw_dim_facility (1)

Snowflake Relationships:

- dw_dim_patient → geographic regions (1)
- dw_dim_doctor → specialties (1)
- dw_dim_department → cost centers (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: CASCADE for dimension updates
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for surrogate key management

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: ALL operational domains
- Required by: Executive dashboards, Departmental reporting
- Integrates with: All source systems via ETL

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: Dimensional modeling (star schema)

- Fact tables: Degenerated dimensions
- Dimension tables: Type 2 SCD support
- Aggregate tables: Pre-computed metrics

Data Types:

- JSONB for flexible metric storage
- Arrays for multi-value dimensions
- Surrogate keys for dimension management
- Generated columns for calculated fields

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- Fact tables: Extremely Large (billions of rows)
- Dimension tables: Large (millions of rows)
- Aggregate tables: Medium (pre-computed summaries)
- ETL logs: Large (process monitoring)

Indexing Strategy:

- Columnar storage for fact tables
- Bitmap indexes on dimension keys
- Partitioning by time dimensions
- Aggregate tables for common queries

Partitioning:

- All fact tables: Range partitioning by time_id (monthly)
- dw_dim_time: Natural partitioning by year
- ETL logs: Range partitioning by start_time (daily)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Data Analysts: Read access for reporting
- Department Heads: Departmental data access
- Executives: Aggregated data access
- ETL Engineers: Write access for loading

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Department-level data restrictions
- Row-level security for patient data

Sensitive Data:

- Aggregated data only (no PHI)
- Anonymized patient identifiers
- Role-based access to detailed data

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10+ years historical data
- Real-time data streaming integration
- Advanced predictive analytics
- Machine learning integration

Cross-domain Impact:

- Single source of truth for analytics
- Performance monitoring across organization
- Strategic decision support
- Continuous improvement through metrics

## Domain 23: Telemedicine

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-platform Support: Web, mobile, and dedicated device compatibility
- Real-time Communication: Video, audio, and chat capabilities with recording
- Scheduling Integration: Seamless integration with appointment systems
- Clinical Documentation: Full EHR integration during consultations
- Technical Requirements: Bandwidth monitoring and quality assurance
- Security Compliance: HIPAA-compliant video conferencing and data storage
- Payment Integration: Support for telemedicine-specific billing codes

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive virtual care delivery platform with scheduling, consultation, and documentation capabilities.

Sub-modules:

- Virtual Consultation Management
- Technical Infrastructure
- Clinical Documentation
- Payment Processing
- Quality Assurance
- Device Management

Entity List:

- telemedicine_providers
- virtual_consultations
- consultation_participants
- telemedicine_Recordings
- technical_metrics
- telemedicine_billing
- device_registry
- network_quality_logs

### 3. Entity Design

telemedicine_providers

- Purpose: Licensed telemedicine service providers
- Attributes:
    - provider_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - provider_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - provider_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Hospital, 2=Clinic, 3=Individual, 4=Group
    - license_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - license_states (TEXT[]) - States where licensed
    - service_specialties (TEXT[]) - Specialties offered
    - platform_integration (JSONB) - API keys, configurations
    - is_hipaa_compliant (BOOLEAN)
    - support_availability (JSONB) - Support hours and contacts
    - performance_rating (percentage_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

virtual_consultations

- Purpose: Telemedicine consultation management
- Attributes:
    - consultation_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK) - Link to appointment system
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - provider_id (UUID FK)
    - consultation_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Video, 2=Audio, 3=Chat, 4=Hybrid
    - scheduled_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - duration_minutes (INTEGER) - Generated
    - consultation_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled, 5=No Show
    - waiting_time_minutes (INTEGER) - Time patient waited
    - consultation_notes (TEXT)
    - clinical_documentation (JSONB) - Vitals, assessments, plans
    - technical_issues (BOOLEAN)
    - issue_description (TEXT)
    - patient_satisfaction (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - follow_up_required (BOOLEAN)
    - follow_up_date (DATE)

consultation_participants

- Purpose: Participants in telemedicine sessions
- Attributes:
    - participant_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK)
    - participant_role (SMALLINT) - 1=Patient, 2=Doctor, 3=Nurse, 4=Interpreter, 5=Family
    - join_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - leave_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - participation_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - device_type (VARCHAR(50)) - Mobile, Desktop, Tablet
    - platform (VARCHAR(50)) - iOS, Android, Web
    - connection_quality (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - is_primary (BOOLEAN) - Primary participant

telemedicine_recordings

- Purpose: Consultation recording management
- Attributes:
    - recording_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - recording_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Video, 2=Audio, 3=Chat Log, 4=Screen Share
    - start_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - end_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - duration_seconds (INTEGER)
    - file_size_mb (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - storage_path (VARCHAR(500))
    - storage_encrypted (BOOLEAN)
    - retention_period_days (SMALLINT)
    - deletion_date (DATE) - Auto-calculated
    - access_count (INTEGER)
    - last_accessed (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - consent_obtained (BOOLEAN)
    - consent_form_id (UUID FK)

technical_metrics

- Purpose: Technical performance monitoring
- Attributes:
    - metric_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - participant_id (UUID FK)
    - metric_timestamp (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - network_latency_ms (INTEGER)
    - jitter_ms (INTEGER)
    - packet_loss_percentage (percentage_type)
    - bandwidth_mbps (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - video_quality (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - audio_quality (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - cpu_usage_percentage (percentage_type)
    - memory_usage_mb (INTEGER)
    - device_battery_percentage (percentage_type)
    - connectivity_type (VARCHAR(50)) - WiFi, 4G, 5G, Ethernet

telemedicine_billing

- Purpose: Telemedicine-specific billing
- Attributes:
    - billing_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - consultation_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - provider_id (UUID FK)
    - billing_code (VARCHAR(50)) - CPT codes for telemedicine
    - base_charge (currency_type)
    - technology_fee (currency_type)
    - total_charge (currency_type)
    - insurance_covered (currency_type)
    - patient_responsibility (currency_type)
    - billing_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Submitted, 3=Paid, 4=Denied
    - denial_reason (TEXT)
    - payment_date (DATE)
    - telemedicine_specific_discount (currency_type)

device_registry

- Purpose: Patient and provider device management
- Attributes:
    - device_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK)
    - device_type (VARCHAR(50)) - Smartphone, Tablet, Computer, Medical Device
    - device_model (VARCHAR(100))
    - operating_system (VARCHAR(50))
    - os_version (VARCHAR(20))
    - app_version (VARCHAR(20))
    - device_identifier (VARCHAR(100)) - IMEI, MAC, etc.
    - last_seen (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - is_compliant (BOOLEAN) - Meets technical requirements
    - compliance_issues (TEXT[])
    - registration_date (DATE)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

network_quality_logs

- Purpose: Network performance historical data
- Attributes:
    - log_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK)
    - location (VARCHAR(255))
    - network_provider (VARCHAR(100))
    - test_timestamp (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - download_speed_mbps (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - upload_speed_mbps (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - ping_ms (INTEGER)
    - network_stability (percentage_type)
    - recommended_quality (SMALLINT) - 1=Poor, 2=Fair, 3=Good, 4=Excellent
    - test_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Automatic, 2=Manual
    - is_optimal_for_telemed (BOOLEAN)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- virtual_consultations (1) → consultation_participants (N)
- virtual_consultations (1) → telemedicine_recordings (N)
- virtual_consultations (1) → technical_metrics (N)
- virtual_consultations (1) → telemedicine_billing (N)
- users (1) → device_registry (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- consultation_participants (N) → virtual_consultations (1)
- technical_metrics (N) → consultation_participants (1)
- telemedicine_billing (N) → telemedicine_providers (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: CASCADE for consultation-related records
- ON UPDATE: RESTRICT for completed consultations

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Appointment Scheduling, Billing
- Required by: Remote patient care
- Integrates with: EHR, Payment gateways, Video platforms

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with JSONB for flexible data

- Clinical documentation in JSONB
- Technical metrics with structured logging
- Device information with version tracking

Data Types:

- JSONB for clinical and technical data
- Arrays for multi-value fields
- Generated columns for durations
- Specialized types for network metrics

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- virtual_consultations: Large (thousands daily)
- technical_metrics: Very Large (frequent sampling)
- telemedicine_recordings: Large (storage-intensive)
- network_quality_logs: Medium (periodic testing)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on virtual_consultations (scheduled_start, consultation_status)
- Index on technical_metrics (consultation_id, metric_timestamp)
- Partial indexes for active consultations and recent recordings
- GIN index on JSONB clinical documentation

Partitioning:

- virtual_consultations: Range partitioning by scheduled_start (monthly)
- technical_metrics: Range partitioning by metric_timestamp (daily)
- telemedicine_recordings: Range partitioning by creation date

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Patients: Own consultation access
- Providers: Their consultation management
- IT Staff: Technical monitoring
- Administrators: Full access with audit

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient-provider data segregation
- Recording access controls

Sensitive Data:

- Video recordings highly protected
- Patient health information encrypted
- Network security metrics secured

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10,000+ daily consultations
- 4K video streaming capabilities
- AI-based quality optimization
- Global low-latency infrastructure

Cross-domain Impact:

- Expands healthcare accessibility
- Reduces physical infrastructure needs
- Creates new revenue streams
- Enhances patient convenience

## Domain 24: Mortuary Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Sensitive Handling: Respectful and dignified management of deceased patients
- Legal Compliance: Adherence to local burial laws and regulations
- Chain of Custody: Complete tracking from death declaration to final disposition
- Family Coordination: Support for family notifications and arrangements
- Multi-faith Support: Accommodation for various religious and cultural practices
- Forensic Cases: Special handling for medico-legal cases
- Inventory Management: Tracking of mortuary equipment and supplies

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive management of deceased patients with respect, dignity, and legal compliance.

Sub-modules:

- Deceased Patient Management
- Body Storage & Tracking
- Release Management
- Funeral Coordination
- Forensic Case Management
- Mortuary Inventory

Entity List:

- deceased_patients
- body_storage
- body_movements
- release_authorizations
- funeral_services
- forensic_cases
- mortuary_inventory
- mortuary_equipment

### 3. Entity Design

deceased_patients

- Purpose: Master record for deceased patients
- Attributes:
    - deceased_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - death_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - death_location (SMALLINT) - 1=Hospital, 2=ER, 3=ICU, 4=Ward, 5=External
    - death_cause (TEXT) - Preliminary cause
    - death_certificate_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - death_certificate_issued (BOOLEAN)
    - death_certificate_issue_date (DATE)
    - pronouncing_doctor_id (UUID FK)
    - time_of_pronouncement (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - is_forensic_case (BOOLEAN)
    - forensic_case_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - religious_affiliation (VARCHAR(100))
    - cultural_practices (TEXT[])
    - next_of_kin_notified (BOOLEAN)
    - next_of_kin_notification_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - family_contact_id (UUID FK) - Link to emergency contacts

body_storage

- Purpose: Mortuary storage management
- Attributes:
    - storage_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - deceased_id (UUID FK)
    - storage_unit_id (UUID FK)
    - storage_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Refrigerated, 2=Freezer, 3=Temporary
    - storage_location (VARCHAR(100)) - Room, rack, shelf
    - storage_start_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - storage_end_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - storage_duration_hours (INTEGER) - Generated
    - temperature_celsius (NUMERIC(4,1))
    - humidity_percentage (percentage_type)
    - condition_on_arrival (TEXT)
    - condition_notes (TEXT)
    - cleaning_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Required, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed
    - sanitization_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

body_movements

- Purpose: Body movement tracking
- Attributes:
    - movement_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - deceased_id (UUID FK)
    - from_location (VARCHAR(255))
    - to_location (VARCHAR(255))
    - movement_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Admission, 2=Transfer, 3=Release, 4=Viewing
    - movement_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - moved_by (UUID FK)
    - received_by (UUID FK)
    - transport_method (VARCHAR(100))
    - seal_number (VARCHAR(100)) - For forensic cases
    - seal_intact (BOOLEAN)
    - movement_notes (TEXT)

release_authorizations

- Purpose: Body release authorization
- Attributes:
    - release_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - deceased_id (UUID FK)
    - release_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Family, 2=Funeral Home, 3=Government, 4=Other
    - recipient_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - recipient_relationship (VARCHAR(100))
    - recipient_contact (phone_type) recipient_identification (VARCHAR(100))
    - identification_type (VARCHAR(50))
    - release_authorized_by (UUID FK)
    - authorization_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - release_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - release_conditions (TEXT)
    - receipt_acknowledged (BOOLEAN)
    - receipt_signature (BYTEA)
    - receipt_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

funeral_services

- Purpose: Funeral service coordination
- Attributes:
    - service_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - deceased_id (UUID FK)
    - funeral_home_id (UUID FK)
    - service_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Viewing, 2=Funeral, 3=Memorial, 4=Cremation, 5=Burial
    - service_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - service_location (VARCHAR(255))
    - religious_official (VARCHAR(100))
    - family_requests (TEXT)
    - special_arrangements (TEXT[])
    - flowers_received (BOOLEAN)
    - obituary_published (BOOLEAN)
    - service_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=Completed, 3=Cancelled
    - service_notes (TEXT)

forensic_cases

- Purpose: Medico-legal case management
- Attributes:
    - forensic_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - deceased_id (UUID FK)
    - case_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - investigating_agency (VARCHAR(255))
    - investigating_officer (VARCHAR(100))
    - officer_contact (phone_type)
    - case_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Homicide, 2=Accident, 3=Suicide, 4=Unknown
    - evidence_collected (BOOLEAN)
    - evidence_description (TEXT)
    - autopsy_required (BOOLEAN)
    - autopsy_performed (BOOLEAN)
    - autopsy_findings (TEXT)
    - hold_release (BOOLEAN) - Requires agency release
    - release_authorization (VARCHAR(100))
    - case_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Closed, 3=Pending

mortuary_inventory

- Purpose: Mortuary supplies management
- Attributes:
    - inventory_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - item_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - item_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Body Bag, 2=Shroud, 3=Identification Tag, 4=Preservative
    - current_stock (INTEGER)
    - reorder_level (INTEGER)
    - last_restock_date (DATE)
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - storage_conditions (TEXT)
    - supplier_id (UUID FK)
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - is_critical (BOOLEAN)

mortuary_equipment

- Purpose: Mortuary equipment maintenance
- Attributes:
    - equipment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - equipment_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - equipment_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Refrigerator, 2=Freezer, 3=Autopsy Table, 4=Viewing Equipment
    - serial_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - installation_date (DATE)
    - last_maintenance_date (DATE)
    - next_maintenance_date (DATE)
    - temperature_range (VARCHAR(50))
    - operational_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Operational, 2=Maintenance, 3=Out of Service
    - maintenance_notes (TEXT)
    - service_provider (VARCHAR(255))

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- deceased_patients (1) → body_storage (N)
- deceased_patients (1) → body_movements (N)
- deceased_patients (1) → release_authorizations (N)
- deceased_patients (1) → funeral_services (N)
- deceased_patients (1) → forensic_cases (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- body_storage (N) → mortuary_equipment (1) [storage units]
- funeral_services (N) → funeral_homes (1) [external reference]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for deceased records
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Legal, Administrative
- Integrates with: Government systems, Funeral homes

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with sensitive data handling

- Chain of custody tracking
- Legal compliance enforcement
- Cultural practice accommodation

Data Types:

- BYTEA for signatures and seals
- Arrays for cultural practices
- Specialized types for temperature
- JSONB for flexible documentation

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- deceased_patients: Medium (hundreds annually)
- body_movements: Medium (multiple per case)
- release_authorizations: Small (1-2 per case)
- forensic_cases: Small (percentage of cases)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on deceased_patients (death_datetime, is_forensic_case)
- Index on body_storage (storage_start_time, storage_unit_id)
- Partial indexes for active cases and recent deaths
- GIN index on cultural practices arrays

Partitioning:

- deceased_patients: Range partitioning by death_datetime (yearly)
- body_movements: Range partitioning by movement_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Mortuary Staff: Daily operations
- Medical Directors: Authorization
- Legal Team: Forensic cases
- Administrators: Oversight

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Sensitive case restrictions
- Role-based access to forensic data

Sensitive Data:

- Deceased information protected
- Forensic details highly secured
- Family information confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for mass casualty incidents
- Digital autopsy integration
- Electronic death registration
- Family portal for arrangements

Cross-domain Impact:

- Essential for legal compliance
- Critical for community trust
- Sensitive operational area
- Integration with government systems

## Domain 25: CSSD / Sterilization

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Instrument Tracking: Every instrument tracked with unique identifiers (barcode/RFID)
- Sterilization Validation: Biological and chemical indicator monitoring
- Cycle Management: Complete autoclave cycle tracking with parameters
- Quality Assurance: Mandatory quality checks at each process step
- Inventory Management: Real-time instrument availability tracking
- Maintenance Scheduling: Preventive maintenance for sterilization equipment
- Infection Control: Strict adherence to sterilization protocols and standards

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Central Sterile Supply Department management with complete instrument tracking, sterilization validation, and quality assurance.

Sub-modules:

- Instrument Catalog
- Sterilization Cycles
- Quality Control
- Inventory Management
- Equipment Maintenance
- Distribution Management

Entity List:

- cssd_instruments
- instrument_sets
- sterilization_cycles
- biological_indicators
- chemical_indicators
- quality_checks
- cssd_inventory
- equipment_maintenance
- instrument_distribution

### 3. Entity Design

cssd_instruments

- Purpose: Surgical instrument master catalog
- Attributes:
    - instrument_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - instrument_code (VARCHAR(50)) - Unique identifier
    - instrument_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - instrument_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Scalpel, 2=Forceps, 3=Scissors, 4=Retractor, 5=Clamp
    - material (VARCHAR(100)) - Stainless steel, titanium, etc.
    - size (VARCHAR(50))
    - manufacturer (VARCHAR(255))
    - manufacture_date (DATE)
    - expected_life_cycles (INTEGER)
    - current_cycle_count (INTEGER)
    - sterilization_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Steam, 2=ETO, 3=Plasma, 4=Chemical
    - sterilization_parameters (JSONB) - Time, temperature, pressure
    - is_critical (BOOLEAN) - Critical for surgery
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - retirement_reason (TEXT)
    - retirement_date (DATE)

instrument_sets

- Purpose: Pre-packaged instrument sets
- Attributes:
    - set_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - set_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - set_name (VARCHAR(255)) - Cardiac set, Ortho set, etc.
    - set_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Basic, 2=Specialty, 3=Emergency, 4=Custom
    - instrument_ids (UUID[]) - Instruments in set
    - expected_instruments (INTEGER)
    - current_instruments (INTEGER)
    - set_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Complete, 2=Incomplete, 3=Retired
    - last_sterilization_date (DATE)
    - next_sterilization_date (DATE)
    - is_available (BOOLEAN)

sterilization_cycles

- Purpose: Sterilization cycle tracking
- Attributes:
    - cycle_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - autoclave_id (UUID FK)
    - cycle_number (VARCHAR(50))
    - cycle_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Steam, 2=Dry, 3=ETO, 4=Plasma
    - start_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - end_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - duration_minutes (INTEGER) - Generated
    - temperature_celsius (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - pressure_psi (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - load_configuration (JSONB) - Instrument types and quantities
    - operator_id (UUID FK)
    - biological_indicator_id (UUID FK)
    - chemical_indicator_id (UUID FK)
    - cycle_status (SMALLINT) - 1=In Progress, 2=Completed, 3=Failed, 4=Aborted
    - failure_reason (TEXT)
    - quality_check_passed (BOOLEAN)

biological_indicators

- Purpose: Biological monitoring
- Attributes:
    - bio_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - cycle_id (UUID FK)
    - lot_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - organism_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Geobacillus stearothermophilus, etc.
    - incubation_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - incubation_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - incubation_temperature (NUMERIC(4,1))
    - result (SMALLINT) - 1=Pass, 2=Fail, 3=Inconclusive
    - result_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - tested_by (UUID FK)
    - verification_by (UUID FK)
    - verification_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

chemical_indicators

- Purpose: Chemical monitoring
- Attributes:
    - chem_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - cycle_id (UUID FK)
    - indicator_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Class 1-6 indicators
    - lot_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - expected_color_change (VARCHAR(50))
    - actual_color_change (VARCHAR(50))
    - result (SMALLINT) - 1=Pass, 2=Fail
    - checked_by (UUID FK)
    - check_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

quality_checks

- Purpose: Quality assurance checks
- Attributes:
    - check_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - instrument_id (UUID FK)
    - set_id (UUID FK)
    - check_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Visual, 2=Functional, 3=Cleaning, 4=Sterility
    - check_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - checked_by (UUID FK)
    - check_criteria (JSONB) - Specific check parameters
    - result (SMALLINT) - 1=Pass, 2=Fail, 3=Conditional
    - failure_details (TEXT)
    - corrective_action (TEXT)
    - recheck_required (BOOLEAN)
    - recheck_date (DATE)

cssd_inventory

- Purpose: Real-time inventory tracking
- Attributes:
    - inventory_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - instrument_id (UUID FK)
    - set_id (UUID FK)
    - current_location (SMALLINT) - 1=CSSD, 2=OR, 3=Ward, 4=Cleaning, 5=Sterilization
    - status (SMALLINT) - 1=Clean, 2=Soiled, 3=Sterile, 4=Quarantine, 5=Maintenance
    - last_sterilization_date (DATE)
    - sterilization_expiry_date (DATE)
    - is_available (BOOLEAN) - Generated
    - current_user_id (UUID FK) - Who has it
    - last_movement_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - movement_reason (TEXT)

equipment_maintenance

- Purpose: Sterilization equipment maintenance
- Attributes:
    - maintenance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - equipment_id (UUID FK)
    - maintenance_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Preventive, 2=Corrective, 3=Calibration
    - scheduled_date (DATE)
    - performed_date (DATE)
    - performed_by (UUID FK)
    - maintenance_details (TEXT)
    - parts_replaced (TEXT[])
    - calibration_results (JSONB)
    - next_maintenance_date (DATE)
    - maintenance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=Completed, 3=Overdue
    - downtime_hours (NUMERIC(4,1))

instrument_distribution

- Purpose: Instrument issuance tracking
- Attributes:
    - distribution_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - instrument_id (UUID FK)
    - set_id (UUID FK)
    - from_department (UUID FK)
    - to_department (UUID FK)
    - requested_by (UUID FK)
    - issued_by (UUID FK)
    - issued_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - received_by (UUID FK)
    - received_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - expected_return_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_return_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - return_condition (SMALLINT) - 1=Clean, 2=Soiled, 3=Damaged
    - damage_details (TEXT)
    - distribution_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Issued, 2=In Use, 3=Returned, 4=Overdue

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- cssd_instruments (1) → quality_checks (N)
- instrument_sets (1) → quality_checks (N)
- sterilization_cycles (1) → biological_indicators (N)
- sterilization_cycles (1) → chemical_indicators (N)
- equipment (1) → equipment_maintenance (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- biological_indicators (N) → sterilization_cycles (1)
- chemical_indicators (N) → sterilization_cycles (1)
- instrument_distribution (N) → departments (1) [from/to]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active instruments
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure, Asset Management
- Required by: Operating Theatre, Emergency, IPD
- Integrates with: Inventory, Maintenance systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with instrument tracking

- Unique instrument identification
- Sterilization cycle validation
- Quality assurance enforcement

Data Types:

- JSONB for flexible parameters
- Arrays for instrument sets
- Generated columns for availability
- Specialized types for scientific measurements

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- cssd_instruments: Large (thousands of instruments)
- sterilization_cycles: Medium (hundreds daily)
- quality_checks: Large (multiple per instrument)
- cssd_inventory: Large (real-time tracking)

Indexing Strategy:

- Unique index on instrument codes
- Index on sterilization_cycles (cycle_status, start_datetime)
- Partial indexes for available instruments and active cycles
- GIN index on JSONB parameters

Partitioning:

- sterilization_cycles: Range partitioning by start_datetime (monthly)
- quality_checks: Range partitioning by check_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- CSSD Technicians: Daily operations
- Quality Officers: Validation and checks
- Surgeons: Instrument preference management
- Administrators: Oversight and reporting

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Department-level instrument visibility
- Quality data access controls

Sensitive Data:

- Sterilization validation records protected
- Equipment performance data secured
- Quality assurance reports confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10,000+ instruments
- RFID-based tracking integration
- Real-time location systems
- Predictive maintenance analytics

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for patient safety
- Essential for surgical operations
- Major infection control component
- Regulatory compliance requirement

---
----
## Domain 26: Patient Transport

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Internal Movement Only: Wheelchair, stretcher, and escort services within facility
- Priority Management: Emergency vs routine transport prioritization
- Real-time Tracking: Current location and status of transport requests
- SLA Compliance: Service level agreement monitoring for response times
- Multi-mode Transport: Support for different transport methods
- Integration with Clinical Systems: Coordination with nurse calls and clinical workflows
- Resource Optimization: Efficient allocation of transport staff and equipment

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Efficient management of internal patient transportation with real-time tracking and priority-based scheduling.

Sub-modules:

- Transport Request Management
- Transport Resource Allocation
- Real-time Location Tracking
- Performance Monitoring
- Equipment Management
- Staff Scheduling

Entity List:

- transport_requests
- transport_assignments
- transport_resources
- transport_staff
- transport_equipment
- location_tracking
- transport_sla
- performance_metrics

### 3. Entity Design

transport_requests

- Purpose: Patient transport request management
- Attributes:
    - request_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - request_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Wheelchair, 2=Stretcher, 3=Escort, 4=Emergency
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=Stat, 4=Emergency
    - from_location (UUID FK) - Department/room
    - to_location (UUID FK) - Department/room
    - requested_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - required_arrival_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - clinical_urgency (TEXT)
    - special_requirements (TEXT[]) - Oxygen, monitor, isolation, etc.
    - requested_by (UUID FK) - Nurse/doctor
    - request_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Assigned, 3=In Progress, 4=Completed, 5=Cancelled
    - cancellation_reason (TEXT)
    - cancelled_by (UUID FK)

transport_assignments

- Purpose: Staff and resource assignment
- Attributes:
    - assignment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - request_id (UUID FK)
    - staff_id (UUID FK)
    - equipment_id (UUID FK)
    - assigned_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - accepted_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - start_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - completion_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_duration_minutes (INTEGER) - Generated
    - assignment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Assigned, 2=Accepted, 3=In Progress, 4=Completed
    - delay_reason (TEXT)
    - distance_meters (INTEGER) - Estimated distance
    - route_notes (TEXT)

transport_resources

- Purpose: Transport staff management
- Attributes:
    - staff_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK) - Link to users table
    - staff_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Porter, 2=Transport Tech, 3=Nurse Aide
    - certification_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Basic, 2=Advanced, 3=Critical Care
    - current_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Assigned, 3=Break, 4=Off-duty
    - current_location (UUID FK) - Department/room
    - shift_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - shift_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - assigned_requests_count (INTEGER)
    - completed_requests_count (INTEGER)
    - performance_rating (percentage_type)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

transport_equipment

- Purpose: Transport equipment inventory
- Attributes:
    - equipment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - equipment_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Wheelchair, 2=Stretcher, 3=Transport Monitor, 4=Oxygen Tank
    - equipment_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - manufacturer (VARCHAR(255))
    - model (VARCHAR(100))
    - purchase_date (DATE)
    - last_maintenance_date (DATE)
    - next_maintenance_date (DATE)
    - current_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=In Use, 3=Maintenance, 4=Retired
    - current_location (UUID FK)
    - battery_level (percentage_type) - For powered equipment
    - sanitization_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Clean, 2=Soiled, 3=Being Cleaned
    - last_sanitization (TIMESTAMPTZ)

location_tracking

- Purpose: Real-time location monitoring
- Attributes:
    - track_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - request_id (UUID FK)
    - staff_id (UUID FK)
    - equipment_id (UUID FK)
    - location_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Department, 2=Room, 3=GPS Coordinates
    - location_id (UUID FK) - Department/room reference
    - gps_latitude (NUMERIC(10,8))
    - gps_longitude (NUMERIC(11,8))
    - timestamp (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - movement_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Stationary, 2=Moving, 3=Waiting
    - estimated_arrival_minutes (INTEGER)
    - signal_strength (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale

transport_sla

- Purpose: Service level agreement tracking
- Attributes:
    - sla_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - request_type (SMALLINT)
    - priority (SMALLINT)
    - target_response_minutes (INTEGER)
    - target_completion_minutes (INTEGER)
    - acceptable_variance_minutes (INTEGER)
    - effective_date (DATE)
    - review_date (DATE)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - sla_notes (TEXT)

performance_metrics

- Purpose: Transport performance monitoring
- Attributes:
    - metric_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - staff_id (UUID FK)
    - metric_date (DATE)
    - total_assignments (INTEGER)
    - on_time_completions (INTEGER)
    - delayed_completions (INTEGER)
    - average_response_time (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - average_completion_time (NUMERIC(5,1))
    - patient_satisfaction (NUMERIC(3,1)) - 1-5 scale
    - incidents_reported (INTEGER)
    - performance_score (percentage_type)
    - improvement_areas (TEXT[])

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- transport_requests (1) → transport_assignments (N)
- transport_requests (1) → location_tracking (N)
- transport_resources (1) → transport_assignments (N)
- transport_equipment (1) → transport_assignments (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- location_tracking (N) → departments (1) [locations]
- transport_assignments (N) → transport_requests (1)
- performance_metrics (N) → transport_resources (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active assignments
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Clinical departments, Nursing
- Integrates with: Nurse call systems, Real-time location systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with real-time tracking

- Location data optimization
- Performance metric calculations
- SLA compliance tracking

Data Types:

- Numeric for precise coordinates
- Arrays for special requirements
- Generated columns for durations
- JSONB for flexible data

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- transport_requests: Large (thousands daily)
- location_tracking: Very Large (frequent updates)
- transport_assignments: Medium (1:1 with requests)
- performance_metrics: Medium (daily aggregates)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on transport_requests (request_status, priority)
- Index on location_tracking (timestamp, staff_id)
- Partial indexes for active requests and available staff
- GIN index on special requirements arrays

Partitioning:

- transport_requests: Range partitioning by requested_datetime (daily)
- location_tracking: Range partitioning by timestamp (hourly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Transport Staff: Assignment management
- Nurses: Request creation
- Supervisors: Performance monitoring
- Patients: Status checking

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Department-level request visibility
- Patient privacy protection

Sensitive Data:

- Patient movement patterns protected
- Staff performance data secured
- Real-time location information controlled

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 500+ concurrent transports
- IoT device integration
- Predictive demand forecasting
- Mobile app for real-time updates

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for patient flow efficiency
- Reduces clinical staff burden
- Improves patient experience
- Enhances operational coordination

---
## Domain 27: Visitor Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-type Visitors: Patients, family, vendors, contractors, and dignitaries
- Access Control: Time-bound access with department restrictions
- Health Screening: COVID-19 and other health screening protocols
- ICU Restrictions: Special rules for intensive care units
- Blacklist Management: Security risk visitor tracking
- Integration with Security: Badge printing and access control systems
- Compliance Tracking: Adherence to hospital visitation policies

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive visitor management with access control, health screening, and security monitoring.

Sub-modules:

- Visitor Registration
- Access Authorization
- Health Screening
- Badge Management
- Blacklist Processing
- Visit Logging

Entity List:

- visitor_registrations
- visitor_categories
- access_passes
- health_screenings
- blacklisted_visitors
- visit_logs
- department_rules
- security_alerts

### 3. Entity Design

visitor_registrations

- Purpose: Visitor master registration
- Attributes:
    - visitor_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visitor_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Family, 2=Friend, 3=Vendor, 4=Contractor, 5=Official
    - first_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - last_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - identification_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Driver License, 2=Passport, 3=National ID
    - identification_number (VARCHAR(100)) - Encrypted
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - contact_email (email_type)
    - emergency_contact (VARCHAR(255))
    - emergency_phone (phone_type)
    - photograph (BYTEA) - Visitor photo
    - registration_date (DATE)
    - registration_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Kiosk, 2=Desk, 3=Online
    - is_blacklisted (BOOLEAN)
    - blacklist_reason (TEXT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

visitor_categories

- Purpose: Visitor type classification
- Attributes:
    - category_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - category_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - category_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Immediate Family, Extended Family, Vendor, etc.
    - max_visitors_per_patient (SMALLINT)
    - visiting_hours_start (TIME)
    - visiting_hours_end (TIME)
    - max_visit_duration_minutes (INTEGER)
    - requires_approval (BOOLEAN)
    - approval_department (UUID FK)
    - special_instructions (TEXT)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

access_passes

- Purpose: Visitor access authorization
- Attributes:
    - pass_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visitor_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK) - Visitor is visiting
    - visit_purpose (SMALLINT) - 1=General Visit, 2=Delivery, 3=Meeting, 4=Emergency
    - authorized_departments (UUID[]) - Allowed departments
    - restricted_departments (UUID[]) - Prohibited departments
    - valid_from (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - valid_until (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - pass_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Expired, 3=Revoked, 4=Used
    - issued_by (UUID FK)
    - issue_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - revocation_reason (TEXT)
    - revoked_by (UUID FK)
    - revoked_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - badge_printed (BOOLEAN)
    - badge_number (VARCHAR(50))

health_screenings

- Purpose: Visitor health screening
- Attributes:
    - screening_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visitor_id (UUID FK)
    - screening_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - temperature_celsius (NUMERIC(4,1))
    - has_fever (BOOLEAN) - Generated: temperature > 38°C
    - has_cough (BOOLEAN)
    - has_breathing_difficulty (BOOLEAN)
    - recent_travel (BOOLEAN)
    - contact_with_covid (BOOLEAN)
    - screening_questions (JSONB) - Additional health questions
    - screening_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Pass, 2=Fail, 3=Conditional
    - conditional_instructions (TEXT)
    - screened_by (UUID FK) - Staff or automated
    - screening_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Automated Kiosk, 2=Staff, 3=Mobile App

blacklisted_visitors

- Purpose: Security risk management
- Attributes:
    - blacklist_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visitor_id (UUID FK)
    - blacklist_reason (SMALLINT) - 1=Security Risk, 2=Behavioral, 3=Theft, 4=Other
    - blacklist_details (TEXT)
    - blacklist_start (DATE)
    - blacklist_end (DATE) - Null for permanent
    - blacklisted_by (UUID FK)
    - alert_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Inform, 2=Warning, 3=Alert, 4=Restrict
    - notification_sent (BOOLEAN)
    - notification_sent_to (UUID[]) - Security staff notified
    - is_active (BOOLEAN) - Generated: blacklist_end IS NULL OR blacklist_end > CURRENT_DATE

visit_logs

- Purpose: Visitor movement tracking
- Attributes:
    - log_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visitor_id (UUID FK)
    - pass_id (UUID FK)
    - entry_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - exit_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - visit_duration_minutes (INTEGER) - Generated
    - entry_point (UUID FK) - Gate/entrance
    - exit_point (UUID FK) - Gate/exit
    - visited_departments (UUID[])
    - current_location (UUID FK) - Real-time tracking
    - visit_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Completed, 3=Overstayed
    - security_checks (SMALLINT) - Number of security checks
    - incidents_reported (BOOLEAN)
    - incident_details (TEXT)

department_rules

- Purpose: Department-specific visitation rules
- Attributes:
    - rule_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - visitor_category_id (UUID FK)
    - max_visitors (SMALLINT)
    - visiting_hours (JSONB) - Department-specific hours
    - special_requirements (TEXT[]) - PPE, appointments, etc.
    - emergency_override (BOOLEAN)
    - override_approval_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Charge Nurse, 2=Department Head, 3=Security
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - last_updated (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - updated_by (UUID FK)

security_alerts

- Purpose: Security incident tracking
- Attributes:
    - alert_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visitor_id (UUID FK)
    - alert_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Unauthorized Access, 2=Overstay, 3=Suspicious Behavior, 4=Blacklist Match
    - alert_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - alert_location (UUID FK)
    - alert_severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Critical
    - alert_description (TEXT)
    - action_taken (TEXT)
    - responded_by (UUID FK)
    - response_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - alert_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Resolved, 3=Escalated
    - escalation_level (SMALLINT)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- visitor_registrations (1) → access_passes (N)
- visitor_registrations (1) → health_screenings (N)
- visitor_registrations (1) → visit_logs (N)
- access_passes (1) → visit_logs (N)
- departments (1) → department_rules (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- visit_logs (N) → security_points (1) [entry/exit]
- department_rules (N) → visitor_categories (1)
- security_alerts (N) → security_staff (1) [responded_by]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active visits
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Security, Nursing
- Integrates with: Access control systems, Security systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with security enforcement

- Visitor identification protection
- Access control validation
- Real-time tracking optimization

Data Types:

- JSONB for flexible screening and rules
- Arrays for department access
- Generated columns for health status
- BYTEA for visitor photographs

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- visitor_registrations: Large (thousands of visitors)
- visit_logs: Very Large (all visitor movements)
- access_passes: Medium (multiple per visitor)
- security_alerts: Small (incident-based)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on visitor_registrations (is_blacklisted, is_active)
- Index on visit_logs (entry_datetime, visit_status)
- Partial indexes for active visits and current alerts
- GIN index on JSONB screening data

Partitioning:

- visit_logs: Range partitioning by entry_datetime (daily)
- access_passes: Range partitioning by valid_from (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Security Staff: Visitor management
- Nurses: Visit authorization
- Registration Staff: Visitor processing
- Administrators: Rule configuration

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Department-level access rules
- Sensitive visitor data protection

Sensitive Data:

- Visitor identification encrypted
- Blacklist information secured
- Security alert details restricted

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 10,000+ daily visitors
- Facial recognition integration
- Advanced analytics on visitor patterns
- Mobile pre-registration capabilities

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for facility security
- Enhances patient privacy
- Improves visitor experience
- Supports infection control

## Domain 28: Asset Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Lifecycle Tracking: Complete asset lifecycle from procurement to disposal
- Maintenance Scheduling: Preventive and corrective maintenance planning
- Depreciation Calculation: Financial depreciation and book value tracking
- Location Tracking: Real-time asset location monitoring
- Warranty Management: Warranty and service contract tracking
- Calibration Requirements: Medical equipment calibration compliance
- Integration with Procurement: Seamless handoff from procurement to asset management

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive management of hospital assets including medical equipment, IT assets, and facility equipment with maintenance and financial tracking.

Sub-modules:

- Asset Catalog
- Asset Lifecycle
- Maintenance Management
- Depreciation Tracking
- Location Management
- Warranty & Calibration
- Disposal Management

Entity List:

- asset_master
- asset_categories
- asset_locations
- maintenance_schedules
- maintenance_work_orders
- depreciation_calculations
- warranty_contracts
- calibration_records
- asset_disposals

### 3. Entity Design

asset_master

- Purpose: Core asset inventory
- Attributes:
    - asset_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - asset_tag (VARCHAR(50)) - Unique identifier
    - asset_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - asset_description (TEXT)
    - category_id (UUID FK)
    - manufacturer (VARCHAR(255))
    - model (VARCHAR(100))
    - serial_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - purchase_date (DATE)
    - purchase_cost (currency_type)
    - current_value (currency_type)
    - useful_life_years (SMALLINT)
    - depreciation_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Straight Line, 2=Declining Balance
    - current_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Operational, 2=Maintenance, 3=Retired, 4=Disposed
    - location_id (UUID FK)
    - assigned_to_department (UUID FK)
    - assigned_to_user (UUID FK)
    - is_critical (BOOLEAN)
    - requires_calibration (BOOLEAN)
    - calibration_frequency_months (SMALLINT)
    - last_calibration_date (DATE)
    - next_calibration_date (DATE)

asset_categories

- Purpose: Asset classification
- Attributes:
    - category_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - category_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - category_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Medical, IT, Facility, Furniture
    - parent_category_id (UUID FK)
    - depreciation_rate (percentage_type)
    - maintenance_frequency_months (SMALLINT)
    - expected_life_years (SMALLINT)
    - replacement_cost (currency_type)
    - insurance_required (BOOLEAN)
    - calibration_required (BOOLEAN)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)

asset_locations

- Purpose: Asset location tracking
- Attributes:
    - location_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - location_code (VARCHAR(20))
    - location_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - location_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Room, 2=Department, 3=Storage, 4=External
    - parent_location_id (UUID FK)
    - gps_coordinates (VARCHAR(100))
    - responsible_person (UUID FK)
    - contact_extension (VARCHAR(20))
    - is_tracked (BOOLEAN) - Real-time tracking enabled
    - last_inventory_date (DATE)
    - location_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Inactive, 3=Under Maintenance

maintenance_schedules

- Purpose: Preventive maintenance planning
- Attributes:
    - schedule_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - asset_id (UUID FK)
    - schedule_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Preventive, 2=Predictive, 3=Condition-based
    - frequency_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Daily, 2=Weekly, 3=Monthly, 4=Quarterly, 5=Yearly
    - frequency_value (SMALLINT)
    - estimated_duration_minutes (INTEGER)
    - maintenance_procedure (TEXT)
    - required_parts (TEXT[])
    - required_tools (TEXT[])
    - safety_precautions (TEXT[])
    - last_performed_date (DATE)
    - next_due_date (DATE)
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - created_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)

maintenance_work_orders

- Purpose: Maintenance execution tracking
- Attributes:
    - work_order_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - asset_id (UUID FK)
    - schedule_id (UUID FK)
    - work_order_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=Emergency, 3=Corrective
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Critical
    - reported_date (DATE)
    - reported_by (UUID FK)
    - issue_description (TEXT)
    - assigned_technician (UUID FK)
    - scheduled_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - downtime_hours (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - parts_used (JSONB)
    - labor_cost (currency_type)
    - parts_cost (currency_type)
    - total_cost (currency_type)
    - work_performed (TEXT)
    - work_order_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    - quality_check_passed (BOOLEAN)
    - checked_by (UUID FK)

depreciation_calculations

- Purpose: Financial depreciation tracking
- Attributes:
    - depreciation_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - asset_id (UUID FK)
    - calculation_date (DATE)
    - depreciation_method (SMALLINT)
    - depreciation_rate (percentage_type)
    - depreciation_amount (currency_type)
    - accumulated_depreciation (currency_type)
    - book_value (currency_type)
    - remaining_life_years (SMALLINT)
    - is_fully_depreciated (BOOLEAN)
    - calculated_by (UUID FK)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - journal_entry_number (VARCHAR(100))

warranty_contracts

- Purpose: Warranty and service contract management
- Attributes:
    - warranty_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - asset_id (UUID FK)
    - contract_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - vendor_id (UUID FK)
    - warranty_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Manufacturer, 2=Extended, 3=Service Contract
    - start_date (DATE)
    - end_date (DATE)
    - coverage_details (TEXT)
    - excluded_items (TEXT[])
    - service_response_hours (SMALLINT)
    - contact_information (TEXT)
    - contract_value (currency_type)
    - renewal_reminder_date (DATE)
    - contract_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Expired, 3=Cancelled
    - notes (TEXT)

calibration_records

- Purpose: Equipment calibration history
- Attributes:
    - calibration_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - asset_id (UUID FK)
    - calibration_date (DATE)
    - calibration_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=After Repair, 3=Pre-use
    - calibration_standard (VARCHAR(100))
    - calibration_method (TEXT)
    - before_calibration_readings (JSONB)
    - after_calibration_readings (JSONB)
    - calibration_result (SMALLINT) - 1=Pass, 2=Fail, 3=Adjustment Made
    - adjustment_details (TEXT)
    - calibrated_by (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - certificate_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - next_calibration_date (DATE)
    - calibration_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Valid, 2=Expired, 3=Suspended

asset_disposals

- Purpose: Asset disposal management
- Attributes:
    - disposal_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - asset_id (UUID FK)
    - disposal_date (DATE)
    - disposal_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Sale, 2=Donation, 3=Recycle, 4=Scrap, 5=Transfer
    - disposal_reason (TEXT)
    - approved_by (UUID FK)
    - disposal_value (currency_type)
    - disposal_cost (currency_type)
    - net_gain_loss (currency_type)
    - recipient_details (TEXT)
    - certificate_of_disposal (BYTEA)
    - disposal_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Approved, 2=Completed, 3=Cancelled
    - last_updated (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- asset_master (1) → maintenance_schedules (N)
- asset_master (1) → maintenance_work_orders (N)
- asset_master (1) → depreciation_calculations (N)
- asset_master (1) → warranty_contracts (N)
- asset_master (1) → calibration_records (N)
- asset_master (1) → asset_disposals (N)
- asset_categories (1) → asset_master (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- maintenance_work_orders (N) → asset_locations (1) [where performed]
- warranty_contracts (N) → procurement_vendors (1)
- asset_disposals (N) → financial_accounts (1) [gain/loss]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active assets
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Procurement, Finance, Core Infrastructure
- Required by: Clinical departments, Facilities
- Integrates with: Financial systems, Maintenance systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with financial tracking

- Depreciation calculations
- Maintenance history
- Lifecycle management

Data Types:

- JSONB for readings and parts
- Arrays for required items
- currency_type for financial amounts
- Generated columns for calculated values

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- asset_master: Large (thousands of assets)
- maintenance_work_orders: Very Large (maintenance history)
- calibration_records: Large (regular calibrations)
- depreciation_calculations: Medium (periodic calculations)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on asset_master (asset_tag, current_status)
- Index on maintenance_work_orders (scheduled_start, work_order_status)
- Partial indexes for active maintenance and critical assets
- GIN index on JSONB data

Partitioning:

- maintenance_work_orders: Range partitioning by reported_date (monthly)
- calibration_records: Range partitioning by calibration_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Asset Managers: Lifecycle management
- Maintenance Technicians: Work order execution
- Finance: Depreciation tracking
- Department Heads: Asset assignments

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Department-level asset visibility
- Financial data access controls

Sensitive Data:

- Financial values protected
- Maintenance procedures secured
- Vendor contract details confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 50,000+ assets
- IoT sensor integration
- Predictive maintenance AI
- Barcode/RFID integration

Cross-domain Impact:

- Critical for operational continuity
- Major financial investment tracking
- Regulatory compliance requirement
- Patient safety through equipment reliability

## Domain 29: Facility Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-building Campus: Support for hospitals with multiple buildings and wings
- Room & Bed Management: Comprehensive room inventory with status tracking
- Maintenance Workflows: Preventive and corrective maintenance for facilities
- Energy Management: Utilities consumption tracking and optimization
- Space Utilization: Monitoring and optimization of space usage
- Safety Compliance: Fire safety, building codes, and regulatory compliance
- Vendor Management: External service provider coordination

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive management of hospital facilities including buildings, rooms, maintenance, and utilities.

Sub-modules:

- Building & Wing Management
- Room & Bed Inventory
- Maintenance Management
- Utilities Tracking
- Space Utilization
- Safety Compliance
- Vendor Management

Entity List:

- buildings
- wings
- rooms
- beds
- facility_maintenance
- utility_consumption
- space_utilization
- safety_inspections
- facility_vendors

### 3. Entity Design

buildings

- Purpose: Building master data
- Attributes:
    - building_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - building_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - building_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - building_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Hospital, 2=Clinic, 3=Administration, 4=Support
    - address (TEXT)
    - construction_date (DATE)
    - renovation_date (DATE)
    - total_floors (SMALLINT)
    - total_area_sqft (NUMERIC(10,2))
    - occupancy_certificate (VARCHAR(100))
    - fire_safety_rating (VARCHAR(50))
    - accessibility_compliant (BOOLEAN)
    - building_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Operational, 2=Under Construction, 3=Renovation, 4=Closed
    - emergency_contacts (JSONB)

wings

- Purpose: Building wing/section management
- Attributes:
    - wing_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - building_id (UUID FK)
    - wing_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - wing_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - wing_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Administrative, 3=Service, 4=Research
    - floor_number (SMALLINT)
    - total_rooms (SMALLINT)
    - total_beds (SMALLINT)
    - wing_in_charge (UUID FK)
    - contact_extension (VARCHAR(20))
    - special_features (TEXT[]) - ICU, Isolation, Pediatric, etc.
    - wing_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Inactive, 3=Renovation

rooms

- Purpose: Room inventory management
- Attributes:
    - room_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - wing_id (UUID FK)
    - room_number (VARCHAR(20))
    - room_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Patient Room, 2=Operation Theatre, 3=ICU, 4=Consultation, 5=Utility
    - room_subtype (VARCHAR(100)) - Single, Double, Isolation, etc.
    - floor_area_sqft (NUMERIC(8,2))
    - bed_capacity (SMALLINT)
    - current_occupancy (SMALLINT)
    - room_features (TEXT[]) - AC, Oxygen, Monitor, etc.
    - accessibility_features (TEXT[]) - Wheelchair, Handrails, etc.
    - maintenance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Clean, 2=Needs Cleaning, 3=Under Maintenance
    - room_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Occupied, 3=Out of Service
    - last_cleaning (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - next_cleaning (TIMESTAMPTZ)

beds

- Purpose: Bed inventory management
- Attributes:
    - bed_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - room_id (UUID FK)
    - bed_number (VARCHAR(10))
    - bed_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Regular, 2=ICU, 3=Pediatric, 4=Bariatric
    - bed_features (TEXT[]) - Electric, Manual, Trendelenburg, etc.
    - mattress_type (VARCHAR(100))
    - purchase_date (DATE)
    - last_maintenance (DATE)
    - next_maintenance (DATE)
    - bed_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Occupied, 3=Cleaning, 4=Maintenance
    - current_patient_id (UUID FK)
    - is_isolated (BOOLEAN)
    - isolation_type (VARCHAR(100))

facility_maintenance

- Purpose: Facility maintenance tracking
- Attributes:
    - maintenance_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - building_id (UUID FK)
    - wing_id (UUID FK)
    - room_id (UUID FK)
    - maintenance_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Preventive, 2=Corrective, 3=Emergency
    - maintenance_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Electrical, 2=Plumbing, 3=HVAC, 4=Structural
    - reported_date (DATE)
    - reported_by (UUID FK)
    - issue_description (TEXT)
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Critical
    - assigned_vendor (UUID FK)
    - assigned_technician (UUID FK)
    - scheduled_date (DATE)
    - completed_date (DATE)
    - downtime_hours (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - cost (currency_type)
    - maintenance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Reported, 2=Assigned, 3=In Progress, 4=Completed
    - work_performed (TEXT)

utility_consumption

- Purpose: Utilities usage tracking
- Attributes:
    - utility_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - building_id (UUID FK)
    - utility_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Electricity, 2=Water, 3=Gas, 4=Steam
    - meter_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - reading_date (DATE)
    - previous_reading (NUMERIC(12,2))
    - current_reading (NUMERIC(12,2))
    - consumption_units (NUMERIC(12,2)) - Generated
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - total_cost (currency_type) - Generated
    - peak_consumption (NUMERIC(12,2))
    - baseline_consumption (NUMERIC(12,2))
    - variance_percentage (percentage_type) - Generated
    - reading_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Manual, 2=Automated, 3=Estimated
    - verified_by (UUID FK)

space_utilization

- Purpose: Space usage analytics
- Attributes:
    - utilization_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - room_id (UUID FK)
    - measurement_date (DATE)
    - measurement_period (SMALLINT) - 1=Hourly, 2=Daily, 3=Weekly, 4=Monthly
    - occupancy_hours (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - available_hours (NUMERIC(6,2))
    - utilization_percentage (percentage_type) - Generated
    - patient_count (INTEGER)
    - staff_count (INTEGER)
    - equipment_count (INTEGER)
    - heat_map_data (JSONB)
    - peak_utilization_time (TIME)
    - low_utilization_time (TIME)
    - recommendations (TEXT[])

safety_inspections

- Purpose: Safety compliance tracking
- Attributes:
    - inspection_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - building_id (UUID FK)
    - inspection_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Fire Safety, 2=Electrical, 3=Structural, 4=Accessibility
    - inspection_date (DATE)
    - inspector_id (UUID FK)
    - inspection_standard (VARCHAR(100)) - NFPA, OSHA, Local codes
    - items_inspected (TEXT[])
    - passed_items (INTEGER)
    - failed_items (INTEGER)
    - compliance_percentage (percentage_type) - Generated
    - corrective_actions (TEXT[])
    - next_inspection_date (DATE)
    - inspection_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=Completed, 3=Follow-up Required
    - certificate_number (VARCHAR(100))

facility_vendors

- Purpose: Facility service vendors
- Attributes:
    - vendor_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - vendor_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Housekeeping, 2=Maintenance, 3=Security, 4=Landscaping
    - vendor_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - contract_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - service_scope (TEXT)
    - service_level_agreement (JSONB)
    - contract_start (DATE)
    - contract_end (DATE)
    - contact_person (VARCHAR(100))
    - contact_phone (phone_type)
    - emergency_contact (phone_type)
    - performance_rating (percentage_type)
    - vendor_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Suspended, 3=Terminated

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- buildings (1) → wings (N)
- wings (1) → rooms (N)
- rooms (1) → beds (N)
- buildings (1) → facility_maintenance (N)
- buildings (1) → utility_consumption (N)
- buildings (1) → safety_inspections (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- facility_maintenance (N) → facility_vendors (1)
- utility_consumption (N) → buildings (1)
- safety_inspections (N) → users (1) [inspector]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for occupied rooms
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Core Infrastructure, Asset Management
- Required by: Clinical operations, Patient management
- Integrates with: Maintenance, Utilities providers

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with spatial relationships

- Hierarchical structure (Building → Wing → Room → Bed)
- Utilization calculations
- Maintenance tracking

Data Types:

- JSONB for flexible data structures
- Arrays for features and items
- Generated columns for calculations
- Specialized types for measurements

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- rooms: Large (thousands of rooms)
- beds: Large (thousands of beds)
- facility_maintenance: Large (maintenance history)
- utility_consumption: Medium (periodic readings)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on rooms (room_status, room_type)
- Index on beds (bed_status, room_id)
- Partial indexes for occupied spaces and active maintenance
- GIN index on JSONB data

Partitioning:

- facility_maintenance: Range partitioning by reported_date (monthly)
- utility_consumption: Range partitioning by reading_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Facility Managers: Building operations
- Maintenance Staff: Work order execution
- Safety Officers: Compliance monitoring
- Department Heads: Space allocation

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Building-level access restrictions
- Safety data access controls

Sensitive Data:

- Security system details protected
- Maintenance procedures secured
- Vendor contract terms confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100+ buildings
- Smart building integration
- Energy management systems
- Real-time space utilization tracking

Cross-domain Impact:

- Foundation for clinical operations
- Major cost center for hospital
- Regulatory compliance critical
- Patient experience dependent on facilities
