## Domain 30: Emergency Department

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Triage System: Mandatory patient prioritization using standardized scales (e.g., ESI, CTAS)
- Fast-track Patients: Separate workflow for minor injuries and illnesses
- Resuscitation Capability: Critical care resuscitation beds with advanced monitoring
- Ambulance Integration: Real-time EMS coordination and handoff
- Behavioral Health: Specialized area for psychiatric emergencies
- Disposition Tracking: Admission, discharge, or transfer decision tracking
- Quality Metrics: Door-to-doctor time, length of stay, left without being seen

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive emergency care management with triage, rapid treatment, and disposition coordination.

Sub-modules:

- Triage & Registration
- Treatment Area Management
- Clinical Documentation
- Disposition Management
- Quality Metrics
- Resource Coordination

Entity List:

- ed_visits
- triage_assessments
- ed_beds
- treatment_areas
- clinical_encounters
- disposition_decisions
- quality_metrics
- resource_utilization

### 3. Entity Design

ed_visits

- Purpose: Emergency department visit master
- Attributes:
    - visit_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - visit_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Sequential ED visit number
    - arrival_mode (SMALLINT) - 1=Ambulance, 2=Walk-in, 3=Police, 4=Transfer
    - arrival_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - triage_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - first_provider_contact_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - bed_assignment_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - disposition_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - exit_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - visit_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Triaged, 2=Waiting, 3=In Treatment, 4=Disposition, 5=Completed
    - emergency_severity (SMALLINT) - ESI level 1-5
    - chief_complaint (TEXT)
    - ambulance_id (UUID FK) - If arrived by ambulance
    - ems_crew_id (UUID FK)
    - is_fast_track_eligible (BOOLEAN)
    - fast_track_reason (TEXT)

triage_assessments

- Purpose: Standardized triage documentation
- Attributes:
    - triage_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - triage_nurse_id (UUID FK)
    - triage_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - vital_signs (JSONB) - BP, HR, RR, Temp, SpO2
    - pain_score (SMALLINT) - 0-10 scale
    - gcs_score (SMALLINT) - Glasgow Coma Scale
    - trauma_score (SMALLINT) - If traumatic injury
    - respiratory_distress (BOOLEAN)
    - hemodynamic_instability (BOOLEAN)
    - mental_status_changes (BOOLEAN)
    - fever (BOOLEAN) - >38°C
    - pain_severity (SMALLINT) - 1=Mild, 2=Moderate, 3=Severe
    - mechanism_of_injury (TEXT) - For trauma
    - allergies (TEXT[])
    - medications (TEXT[])
    - triage_notes (TEXT)
    - esi_level (SMALLINT) - 1-5
    - triage_completed (BOOLEAN)

ed_beds

- Purpose: ED treatment bed management
- Attributes:
    - bed_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - bed_number (VARCHAR(10))
    - bed_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Resuscitation, 2=Acute, 3=Fast-track, 4=Psych, 5=Holding
    - treatment_area_id (UUID FK)
    - monitoring_capability (TEXT[]) - Cardiac, BP, SpO2, EtCO2
    - isolation_capable (BOOLEAN)
    - critical_care_equipped (BOOLEAN)
    - current_patient_id (UUID FK)
    - bed_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Occupied, 3=Cleaning, 4=Maintenance
    - last_cleaning (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - next_cleaning (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - turnover_time_minutes (INTEGER) - Average cleaning time

treatment_areas

- Purpose: ED physical area management
- Attributes:
    - area_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - area_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - area_name (VARCHAR(100)) - Resuscitation, Major Trauma, Fast-track, etc.
    - area_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Clinical, 2=Procedure, 3=Waiting, 4=Support
    - total_beds (SMALLINT)
    - available_beds (SMALLINT) - Generated
    - nurse_station_id (UUID FK)
    - area_supervisor (UUID FK)
    - special_equipment (TEXT[])
    - isolation_capable (BOOLEAN)
    - trauma_capable (BOOLEAN)
    - area_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Operational, 2=Closed, 3=Overcrowded

clinical_encounters

- Purpose: ED provider patient encounters
- Attributes:
    - encounter_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - provider_id (UUID FK)
    - provider_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Physician, 2=PA, 3=NP, 4=Resident
    - encounter_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - encounter_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - encounter_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - subjective (TEXT) - Patient history
    - objective (TEXT) - Physical exam
    - assessment (TEXT) - Diagnosis/impression
    - plan (TEXT) - Treatment plan
    - procedures_performed (TEXT[])
    - medications_administered (TEXT[])
    - tests_ordered (TEXT[])
    - encounter_status (SMALLINT) - 1=In Progress, 2=Completed, 3=Documented
    - signed_by (UUID FK)
    - signed_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

disposition_decisions

- Purpose: Patient disposition management
- Attributes:
    - disposition_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - disposition_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Discharge, 2=Admit, 3=Transfer, 4=AMA, 5=Death
    - decision_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - decision_by (UUID FK)
    - admitting_department (UUID FK) - If admission
    - transfer_facility (VARCHAR(255)) - If transfer
    - transfer_reason (TEXT)
    - discharge_instructions (TEXT)
    - follow_up_instructions (TEXT)
    - death_certificate_required (BOOLEAN)
    - coroner_notified (BOOLEAN)
    - disposition_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Completed, 3=Delayed
    - delay_reason (TEXT)

quality_metrics

- Purpose: ED performance tracking
- Attributes:
    - metric_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - door_to_triage_minutes (INTEGER)
    - door_to_doctor_minutes (INTEGER)
    - door_to_disposition_minutes (INTEGER)
    - door_to_disposition_minutes (INTEGER)
    - length_of_stay_minutes (INTEGER)
    - left_without_being_seen (BOOLEAN)
    - lwbs_reason (TEXT)
    - ambulance_offload_time_minutes (INTEGER)
    - boarding_time_minutes (INTEGER) - If admission pending
    - patient_satisfaction_score (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - medication_errors (BOOLEAN)
    - fall_incidents (BOOLEAN)
    - quality_notes (TEXT)

resource_utilization

- Purpose: ED resource tracking
- Attributes:
    - utilization_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - visit_id (UUID FK)
    - nursing_staff_count (SMALLINT)
    - physician_staff_count (SMALLINT)
    - support_staff_count (SMALLINT)
    - imaging_studies_count (SMALLINT)
    - lab_tests_count (SMALLINT)
    - medications_administered_count (SMALLINT)
    - procedures_performed_count (SMALLINT)
    - consultations_requested_count (SMALLINT)
    - resource_intensity_score (NUMERIC(5,2))
    - cost_of_care (currency_type)
    - reimbursement_amount (currency_type)
    - utilization_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- ed_visits (1) → triage_assessments (N)
- ed_visits (1) → clinical_encounters (N)
- ed_visits (1) → disposition_decisions (N)
- ed_visits (1) → quality_metrics (N)
- treatment_areas (1) → ed_beds (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- ed_beds (N) → ed_visits (1) [current patient]
- clinical_encounters (N) → ed_visits (1)
- disposition_decisions (N) → departments (1) [admitting]

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active visits
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Clinical OPD
- Required by: Inpatient, Laboratory, Radiology
- Integrates with: Ambulance, Pharmacy, Billing

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with performance optimization

- Triage data structured for rapid access
- Time intervals calculated
- Resource tracking integrated

Data Types:

- JSONB for clinical assessments
- Arrays for procedures and medications
- Generated columns for time metrics
- Specialized types for clinical scores

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- ed_visits: Very Large (thousands daily in large ED)
- clinical_encounters: Large (multiple per visit)
- triage_assessments: Large (1:1 with visits)
- quality_metrics: Medium (1:1 with visits)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on ed_visits (arrival_datetime, visit_status)
- Index on ed_beds (bed_status, treatment_area_id)
- Partial indexes for active visits and available beds
- GIN index on JSONB clinical data

Partitioning:

- ed_visits: Range partitioning by arrival_datetime (daily)
- clinical_encounters: Range partitioning by encounter_start (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- ED Physicians: Patient care documentation
- Triage Nurses: Initial assessment
- ED Technicians: Procedure support
- Administrators: Quality monitoring

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient privacy protection
- Role-based access to sensitive cases

Sensitive Data:

- Trauma cases protected
- Psychiatric emergencies secured
- Quality improvement data confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 500+ daily ED visits
- Real-time bed tracking
- Predictive patient flow analytics
- Integration with tele-triage

Cross-domain Impact:

- Hospital front door and safety net
- Major revenue generator
- Quality indicator for hospital
- Critical for community health

---
## Domain 31: Operating Theatre (OT)

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Scheduling Complexity: Multiple resources (surgeons, rooms, equipment) coordination
- Surgical Safety: WHO surgical safety checklist compliance
- Inventory Tracking: Implants, instruments, and consumables management
- Anesthesia Management: Anesthesia records and medication tracking
- Case Cart System: Pre-procedure instrument and supply preparation
- Sterility Assurance: Strict adherence to sterilization protocols
- Post-op Recovery: PACU (Post-Anesthesia Care Unit) management

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive surgical suite management with scheduling, safety protocols, and inventory control.

Sub-modules:

- OT Scheduling
- Surgical Safety
- Inventory Management
- Anesthesia Records
- Case Cart Preparation
- Sterility Assurance
- PACU Management

Entity List:

- ot_rooms
- surgical_cases
- surgical_teams
- safety_checklists
- implant_inventory
- anesthesia_records
- case_carts
- pacu_records

### 3. Entity Design

ot_rooms

- Purpose: Operating room inventory and capabilities
- Attributes:
    - room_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - room_number (VARCHAR(10))
    - room_type (SMALLINT) - 1=General, 2=Cardiac, 3=Neuro, 4=Ortho, 5=Day Care
    - room_size_sqft (NUMERIC(8,2))
    - air_changes_per_hour (SMALLINT)
    - temperature_control (BOOLEAN)
    - humidity_control (BOOLEAN)
    - special_equipment (TEXT[]) - Microscopes, Navigation, Robotics
    - isolation_capable (BOOLEAN)
    - lead_lined (BOOLEAN) - For radiology
    - room_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=In Use, 3=Cleaning, 4=Maintenance
    - last_sterilization (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - next_available (TIMESTAMPTZ)

surgical_cases

- Purpose: Surgical case scheduling and tracking
- Attributes:
    - case_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - case_number (VARCHAR(20)) - Sequential case number
    - scheduled_date (DATE)
    - scheduled_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - scheduled_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - case_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - procedure_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Elective, 2=Emergency, 3=Urgent
    - specialty (UUID FK) - Surgical specialty
    - primary_procedure (VARCHAR(255))
    - secondary_procedures (TEXT[])
    - anesthesia_type (SMALLINT) - 1=General, 2=Regional, 3=Local, 4=MAC
    - case_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Cancelled
    - cancellation_reason (TEXT)
    - estimated_blood_loss_ml (INTEGER)
    - complications (TEXT[])

surgical_teams

- Purpose: Surgical team composition
- Attributes:
    - team_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - case_id (UUID FK)
    - surgeon_id (UUID FK)
    - assistant_surgeon_id (UUID FK)
    - anesthesiologist_id (UUID FK)
    - circulating_nurse_id (UUID FK)
    - scrub_nurse_id (UUID FK)
    - tech_staff (UUID[])
    - team_role_confirmations (JSONB) - Role verification
    - team_briefing_completed (BOOLEAN)
    - briefing_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - debriefing_completed (BOOLEAN)
    - debriefing_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)

safety_checklists

- Purpose: WHO surgical safety checklist
- Attributes:
    - checklist_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - case_id (UUID FK)
    - sign_in_completed (BOOLEAN) - Before anesthesia
    - sign_in_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - sign_in_by (UUID FK)
    - time_out_completed (BOOLEAN) - Before incision
    - time_out_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - time_out_by (UUID FK)
    - sign_out_completed (BOOLEAN) - Before patient leaves
    - sign_out_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - sign_out_by (UUID FK)
    - checklist_items (JSONB) - Specific checklist items
    - compliance_percentage (percentage_type)
    - safety_concerns (TEXT[])
    - concerns_addressed (BOOLEAN)

implant_inventory

- Purpose: Surgical implants tracking
- Attributes:
    - implant_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - implant_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Prosthesis, 2=Plate, 3=Screw, 4=Mesh, 5=Stent
    - implant_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - manufacturer (VARCHAR(255))
    - model (VARCHAR(100))
    - size (VARCHAR(50))
    - lot_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - serial_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - expiry_date (DATE)
    - unit_cost (currency_type)
    - current_stock (INTEGER)
    - reorder_level (INTEGER)
    - sterilization_required (BOOLEAN)
    - sterilization_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Sterile, 2=Needs Sterilization
    - implant_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Available, 2=Reserved, 3=Used, 4=Expired

anesthesia_records

- Purpose: Anesthesia administration tracking
- Attributes:
    - record_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - case_id (UUID FK)
    - anesthesiologist_id (UUID FK)
    - anesthesia_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - anesthesia_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - anesthesia_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - medications_administered (JSONB) - Drugs, doses, times
    - vital_signs_log (JSONB) - Continuous monitoring
    - complications (TEXT[])
    - blood_products (JSONB) - Transfusions
    - fluid_balance (JSONB) - Input/output
    - recovery_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Awake, 2=Sedated, 3=Agitated
    - pacu_ready (BOOLEAN)
    - record_completed (BOOLEAN)
    - signed_by (UUID FK)

case_carts

- Purpose: Pre-procedure instrument and supply preparation
- Attributes:
    - cart_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - case_id (UUID FK)
    - prepared_by (UUID FK)
    - preparation_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - instruments (JSONB) - Instrument list with counts
    - supplies (JSONB) - Sutures, drapes, dressings
    - implants (JSONB) - Planned implants
    - medications (JSONB) - Surgery medications
    - cart_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Preparing, 2=Ready, 3=In Use, 4=Returned
    - verification_by (UUID FK)
    - verification_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - missing_items (TEXT[])
    - extra_items (TEXT[])

pacu_records

- Purpose: Post-anesthesia care unit management
- Attributes:
    - pacu_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - case_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - admission_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - discharge_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - pacu_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - initial_alertness (SMALLINT) - 1=Alert, 2=Drowsy, 3=Unresponsive
    - pain_scores (JSONB) - Serial pain assessments
    - vital_signs (JSONB) - Continuous monitoring
    - oxygen_requirement (BOOLEAN)
    - oxygen_liters_min (NUMERIC(4,1))
    - complications (TEXT[])
    - discharge_ready (BOOLEAN)
    - discharge_criteria_met (JSONB) - Aldrete score etc.
    - discharged_to (SMALLINT) - 1=Ward, 2=ICU, 3=Home
    - pacu_nurse_id (UUID FK)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- surgical_cases (1) → surgical_teams (N)
- surgical_cases (1) → safety_checklists (N)
- surgical_cases (1) → anesthesia_records (N)
- surgical_cases (1) → case_carts (N)
- surgical_cases (1) → pacu_records (N)
- ot_rooms (1) → surgical_cases (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- surgical_teams (N) → surgical_cases (1)
- anesthesia_records (N) → surgical_cases (1)
- case_carts (N) → surgical_cases (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active cases
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Inventory, CSSD
- Required by: Billing, Clinical departments
- Integrates with: Pharmacy, Laboratory, Radiology

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with surgical workflow optimization

- Case scheduling complexity handled
- Inventory tracking with lot control
- Safety compliance enforced

Data Types:

- JSONB for clinical data and medications
- Arrays for procedures and complications
- Generated columns for durations
- Specialized types for medical measurements

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- surgical_cases: Large (hundreds weekly)
- anesthesia_records: Large (detailed documentation)
- case_carts: Medium (1:1 with cases)
- safety_checklists: Medium (1:1 with cases)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on surgical_cases (scheduled_date, case_status)
- Index on ot_rooms (room_status, next_available)
- Partial indexes for active cases and available rooms
- GIN index on JSONB clinical data

Partitioning:

- surgical_cases: Range partitioning by scheduled_date (monthly)
- anesthesia_records: Range partitioning by anesthesia_start (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Surgeons: Case scheduling and documentation
- Anesthesiologists: Anesthesia records
- Nurses: Safety checklist compliance
- Inventory Staff: Implant management

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Surgeon-specific case visibility
- Sensitive implant cost protection

Sensitive Data:

- Surgical complications protected
- Implant cost information secured
- Patient safety data confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100+ daily surgeries
- Robotic surgery integration
- Real-time inventory tracking
- Advanced analytics on surgical outcomes

Cross-domain Impact:

- Major revenue center for hospital
- Critical for patient outcomes
- High-cost resource utilization
- Quality and safety focus area

## Domain 32: Dental Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Comprehensive Dental Care: Support for general dentistry and specialties
- Dental Charting: Standardized tooth numbering and condition tracking
- Radiology Integration: Dental X-rays and imaging management
- Prosthetics Management: Crowns, bridges, dentures, and implants tracking
- Appointment Scheduling: Procedure-based scheduling with duration estimates
- Insurance Coordination: Dental-specific insurance and claim processing
- Medical-Dental Integration: Connection to general medical records

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Comprehensive dental care management with clinical documentation, procedure tracking, and dental-specific workflow support.

Sub-modules:

- Dental Charting
- Procedure Management
- Dental Radiology
- Prosthetics Lab
- Appointment Scheduling
- Insurance Processing
- Treatment Planning

Entity List:

- dental_patients
- dental_charts
- dental_procedures
- procedure_codes
- dental_appointments
- dental_radiographs
- prosthetics_work
- treatment_plans
- insurance_preauthorizations

### 3. Entity Design

dental_patients

- Purpose: Dental-specific patient information
- Attributes:
    - dental_patient_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK) - Link to main patient table
    - dental_insurance_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - insurance_group_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - insurance_effective_date (DATE)
    - insurance_expiry_date (DATE)
    - annual_maximum (currency_type)
    - remaining_benefit (currency_type)
    - deductible_met (currency_type)
    - last_dental_visit (DATE)
    - next_recall_date (DATE)
    - oral_hygiene_rating (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - medical_dental_alert (TEXT[]) - Antibiotic prophylaxis, bleeding risk, etc.
    - preferred_dentist_id (UUID FK)

dental_charts

- Purpose: Dental condition charting
- Attributes:
    - chart_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - charting_date (DATE)
    - charting_dentist_id (UUID FK)
    - tooth_data (JSONB) - Condition per tooth (FDI notation)
    - periodontal_status (JSONB) - Pocket depths, bleeding, mobility
    - occlusion_type (VARCHAR(50)) - Class I, II, III
    - oral_lesions (TEXT[])
    - soft_tissue_status (TEXT)
    - charting_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Manual, 2=Digital, 3=Voice
    - chart_completed (BOOLEAN)
    - reviewed_by (UUID FK)
    - review_date (DATE)

dental_procedures

- Purpose: Dental procedure execution
- Attributes:
    - procedure_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_id (UUID FK)
    - procedure_code (VARCHAR(50)) - CDT code
    - procedure_description (VARCHAR(255))
    - teeth_involved (VARCHAR(100)) - FDI numbers
    - surfaces_involved (VARCHAR(50)) - M, D, B, L, O
    - anesthesia_used (BOOLEAN)
    - anesthesia_type (VARCHAR(100))
    - anesthesia_amount_ml (NUMERIC(4,2))
    - procedure_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - procedure_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - procedure_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - complications (TEXT[])
    - postoperative_instructions (TEXT)
    - procedure_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Planned, 2=Completed, 3=Partially Completed
    - performed_by (UUID FK)

procedure_codes

- Purpose: Dental procedure catalog
- Attributes:
    - code_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - cdt_code (VARCHAR(50)) - Current Dental Terminology
    - procedure_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - procedure_category (SMALLINT) - 1=Diagnostic, 2=Preventive, 3=Restorative, 4=Endo, 5=Perio, 6=Prostho, 7=Oral Surgery, 8=Ortho
    - default_fee (currency_type)
    - estimated_duration_minutes (SMALLINT)
    - requires_preauthorization (BOOLEAN)
    - frequency_limitation (VARCHAR(100)) - e.g., "Once per 5 years"
    - age_limitations (VARCHAR(100))
    - is_active (BOOLEAN)
    - material_options (TEXT[]) - Amalgam, Composite, Ceramic, etc.

dental_appointments

- Purpose: Dental appointment management
- Attributes:
    - appointment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - dentist_id (UUID FK)
    - hygienist_id (UUID FK)
    - appointment_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Exam, 2=Cleaning, 3=Restorative, 4=Emergency, 5=Consultation
    - scheduled_date (DATE)
    - scheduled_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - scheduled_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_start (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - actual_end (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - appointment_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - chair_number (VARCHAR(10))
    - procedures_planned (UUID[]) - Planned procedures
    - procedures_completed (UUID[]) - Actual procedures
    - appointment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Scheduled, 2=Confirmed, 3=In Progress, 4=Completed, 5=Cancelled
    - cancellation_reason (TEXT)
    - recall_required (BOOLEAN)
    - recall_interval_months (SMALLINT)

dental_radiographs

- Purpose: Dental imaging management
- Attributes:
    - radiograph_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - radiograph_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Bitewing, 2=Periapical, 3=Panoramic, 4=Cephalometric, 5=CBCT
    - teeth_imaged (VARCHAR(100))
    - exposure_date (DATE)
    - exposure_time_seconds (NUMERIC(4,2))
    - kvp (NUMERIC(4,1)) - Kilovolt peak
    - ma (NUMERIC(4,1)) - Milliamperage
    - image_file_path (VARCHAR(500))
    - image_quality (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - interpretation (TEXT)
    - interpreted_by (UUID FK)
    - interpretation_date (DATE)
    - findings (TEXT[])
    - radiation_dose (NUMERIC(8,4)) - μSv
    - retake_required (BOOLEAN)
    - retake_reason (TEXT)

prosthetics_work

- Purpose: Dental laboratory work tracking
- Attributes:
    - labwork_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - procedure_id (UUID FK)
    - lab_type (SMALLINT) - 1=In-house, 2=External
    - work_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Crown, 2=Bridge, 3=Denture, 4=Implant, 5=Night Guard
    - material (VARCHAR(100)) - Porcelain, Zirconia, Acrylic, etc.
    - shade (VARCHAR(50))
    - sent_to_lab_date (DATE)
    - expected_return_date (DATE)
    - actual_return_date (DATE)
    - lab_cost (currency_type)
    - lab_instructions (TEXT)
    - try_in_date (DATE)
    - try_in_notes (TEXT)
    - delivery_date (DATE)
    - adjustment_required (BOOLEAN)
    - adjustment_notes (TEXT)
    - warranty_period_months (SMALLINT)
    - labwork_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Sent, 2=In Progress, 3=Ready, 4=Delivered

treatment_plans

- Purpose: Comprehensive treatment planning
- Attributes:
    - plan_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - plan_date (DATE)
    - planned_by (UUID FK)
    - treatment_phases (JSONB) - Phase 1, Phase 2, etc.
    - procedures_planned (UUID[]) - Procedure codes
    - estimated_total_fee (currency_type)
    - estimated_total_time_minutes (INTEGER)
    - priority_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Urgent, 2=High, 3=Medium, 4=Low
    - acceptance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=Accepted, 3=Partial, 4=Declined
    - acceptance_date (DATE)
    - declined_reason (TEXT)
    - completed_percentage (percentage_type)
    - review_date (DATE)
    - plan_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Completed, 3=Archived

insurance_preauthorizations

- Purpose: Dental insurance preauthorization
- Attributes:
    - preauth_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - procedure_id (UUID FK)
    - insurance_company (VARCHAR(255))
    - submitted_date (DATE)
    - approval_date (DATE)
    - approved_amount (currency_type)
    - approved_procedure (VARCHAR(255))
    - preauth_number (VARCHAR(100))
    - expiration_date (DATE)
    - xrays_submitted (BOOLEAN)
    - xray_submission_date (DATE)
    - preauth_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Submitted, 2=Approved, 3=Denied, 4=Pending
    - denial_reason (TEXT)
    - appeal_filed (BOOLEAN)
    - appeal_date (DATE)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- dental_patients (1) → dental_charts (N)
- dental_patients (1) → dental_procedures (N)
- dental_patients (1) → dental_appointments (N)
- dental_patients (1) → dental_radiographs (N)
- dental_patients (1) → treatment_plans (N)
- dental_appointments (1) → dental_procedures (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- dental_procedures (N) → procedure_codes (1)
- prosthetics_work (N) → dental_procedures (1)
- insurance_preauthorizations (N) → dental_procedures (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for completed procedures
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Billing, Radiology
- Required by: Clinical documentation, Insurance processing
- Integrates with: Main EHR, Laboratory systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with dental-specific requirements

- Tooth-level condition tracking
- Procedure code standardization
- Insurance preauthorization workflow

Data Types:

- JSONB for dental charting and periodontal status
- Arrays for teeth and surfaces
- Generated columns for durations and calculations
- Specialized types for dental measurements

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- dental_procedures: Large (thousands monthly)
- dental_charts: Medium (periodic charting)
- dental_radiographs: Medium (imaging studies)
- treatment_plans: Medium (per patient)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on dental_appointments (scheduled_date, appointment_status)
- Index on dental_procedures (procedure_date, patient_id)
- Partial indexes for upcoming appointments and active treatment
- GIN index on JSONB dental chart data

Partitioning:

- dental_appointments: Range partitioning by scheduled_date (monthly)
- dental_procedures: Range partitioning by procedure_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Dentists: Clinical documentation and procedures
- Hygienists: Preventive care and charting
- Front Desk: Appointment scheduling
- Insurance Coordinators: Preauthorization management

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Provider-specific patient visibility
- Sensitive financial data protection

Sensitive Data:

- Dental insurance information protected
- Clinical radiographs secured
- Treatment cost estimates confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for multi-location dental practices
- Digital impression integration
- CAD/CAM system integration
- Teledentistry capabilities

Cross-domain Impact:

- Expands hospital service offerings
- Creates new revenue stream
- Improves patient comprehensive care
- Enhances referral network

## Domain 33: Electronic Medical Records (EMR/EHR)

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Comprehensive Health Record: Lifetime patient health information repository
- Interoperability Standards: HL7 FHIR, C-CDA, and SMART on FHIR compliance
- Clinical Decision Support: Real-time alerts and recommendations
- Patient Portal Integration: Secure patient access to health information
- Data Liquidity: Seamless data exchange between care settings
- Audit Trail: Complete access and modification history
- Consent Management: Granular consent for data sharing and use

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Centralized, interoperable electronic health record system supporting comprehensive patient care across all settings.

Sub-modules:

- Clinical Data Repository
- Health Information Exchange
- Clinical Decision Support
- Patient Portal Integration
- Data Standardization
- Consent Management
- Audit & Compliance

Entity List:

- emr_patient_records
- clinical_documents
- fhir_resources
- cds_rules
- patient_portal_access
- data_exchange_logs
- consent_management
- clinical_audit_trail

### 3. Entity Design

emr_patient_records

- Purpose: Master patient health record
- Attributes:
    - record_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - record_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Inactive, 3=Merged, 4=Archived
    - record_creation_date (DATE)
    - last_updated_date (DATE)
    - problem_list (JSONB) - Active problems with onset dates
    - allergy_list (JSONB) - Allergies with severity and reactions
    - medication_list (JSONB) - Current medications with details
    - immunization_list (JSONB) - Vaccination history
    - surgical_history (JSONB) - Past surgical procedures
    - family_history (JSONB) - Family medical history
    - social_history (JSONB) - Smoking, alcohol, lifestyle
    - advance_directives (JSONB) - Living will, DNR, healthcare proxy
    - care_team (UUID[]) - Current care team members
    - patient_summary (TEXT) - Clinical summary
    - data_quality_score (percentage_type)

clinical_documents

- Purpose: Structured clinical documentation
- Attributes:
    - document_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - document_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Consultation, 2=Progress, 3=Discharge, 4=Operative, 5=Procedure
    - document_date (DATE)
    - author_id (UUID FK)
    - author_role (VARCHAR(100))
    - encounter_id (UUID FK)
    - document_title (VARCHAR(255))
    - document_content (TEXT)
    - structured_data (JSONB) - FHIR-compliant structured data
    - document_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Draft, 2=Completed, 3=Amended, 4=Deleted
    - verification_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Preliminary, 2=Final, 3=Amended, 4=Corrected
    - confidentiality_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Normal, 2=Restricted, 3=Very Restricted
    - fhir_compliant (BOOLEAN)
    - cda_document (BYTEA) - C-CDA formatted document
    - digital_signature (BYTEA)
    - signature_timestamp (TIMESTAMPTZ)

fhir_resources

- Purpose: FHIR resource storage and management
- Attributes:
    - resource_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - resource_type (VARCHAR(100)) - Patient, Observation, Condition, etc.
    - fhir_version (VARCHAR(10)) - R4, STU3, etc.
    - resource_json (JSONB) - Complete FHIR resource
    - resource_metadata (JSONB) - Version, lastUpdated, profile
    - resource_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Current, 2=Historical, 3=Entered-in-error
    - created_date (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - last_updated (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - version_id (VARCHAR(100))
    - last_modified_by (UUID FK)
    - interaction_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Create, 2=Read, 3=Update, 4=Delete
    - bundle_id (UUID) - For transaction bundles

cds_rules

- Purpose: Clinical decision support rules
- Attributes:
    - rule_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - rule_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - rule_description (TEXT)
    - rule_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Alert, 2=Reminder, 3=Recommendation, 4=Order Set
    - clinical_domain (VARCHAR(100)) - Medication, Lab, Radiology, etc.
    - trigger_events (TEXT[]) - Events that trigger the rule
    - rule_condition (TEXT) - Logic condition
    - action_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Display, 2=Email, 3=Order, 4=Document
    - action_content (TEXT)
    - severity_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Critical
    - evidence_level (SMALLINT) - 1-5 based on evidence strength
    - activation_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Inactive, 3=Testing
    - effective_date (DATE)
    - expiration_date (DATE)
    - last_evaluated (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - success_rate (percentage_type)

patient_portal_access

- Purpose: Patient portal access management
- Attributes:
    - access_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - portal_user_id (UUID FK)
    - access_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Basic, 2=Standard, 3=Full
    - granted_features (TEXT[]) - Records, Messaging, Appointments, etc.
    - access_grant_date (DATE)
    - access_review_date (DATE)
    - last_access_date (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - access_count (INTEGER)
    - consent_version (VARCHAR(20))
    - security_questions (JSONB)
    - mfa_enabled (BOOLEAN)
    - mfa_method (VARCHAR(50))
    - access_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Suspended, 3=Revoked
    - suspension_reason (TEXT)

data_exchange_logs

- Purpose: Health information exchange tracking
- Attributes:
    - exchange_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - direction (SMALLINT) - 1=Outbound, 2=Inbound
    - exchange_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Query, 2=Push, 3=Subscription
    - exchange_standard (VARCHAR(50)) - FHIR, C-CDA, HL7v2, etc.
    - source_system (VARCHAR(255))
    - destination_system (VARCHAR(255))
    - exchange_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - documents_exchanged (TEXT[])
    - exchange_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Success, 2=Partial, 3=Failed
    - error_message (TEXT)
    - bytes_transferred (INTEGER)
    - processing_time_ms (INTEGER)
    - security_context (JSONB) - TLS, certificates, authentication
    - purpose_of_use (VARCHAR(100)) - Treatment, Payment, Operations

consent_management

- Purpose: Granular consent management
- Attributes:
    - consent_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - consent_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Treatment, 2=Research, 3=Data Sharing, 4=Marketing
    - consent_scope (JSONB) - Specific data elements covered
    - consent_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Granted, 2=Denied, 3=Partial, 4=Revoked
    - effective_date (DATE)
    - expiration_date (DATE)
    - revocation_date (DATE)
    - revocation_reason (TEXT)
    - consent_capture_method (SMALLINT) - 1=Paper, 2=Digital, 3=Verbal
    - witness_required (BOOLEAN)
    - witness_id (UUID FK)
    - reconsent_required (BOOLEAN)
    - reconsent_date (DATE)
    - consent_version (VARCHAR(20))
    - audit_trail (JSONB) - Consent history

clinical_audit_trail

- Purpose: Comprehensive access auditing
- Attributes:
    - audit_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - user_id (UUID FK)
    - user_role (VARCHAR(100))
    - access_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - access_type (SMALLINT) - 1=View, 2=Create, 3=Update, 4=Delete, 5=Export
    - accessed_resource (VARCHAR(100))
    - resource_id (UUID)
    - access_point (VARCHAR(100)) - Workstation, IP, Application
    - action_performed (TEXT)
    - changes_made (JSONB) - Before/after values
    - access_reason (TEXT)
    - override_reason (TEXT) - If breaking glass used
    - compliance_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Compliant, 2=Questionable, 3=Violation
    - audit_reviewed (BOOLEAN)
    - reviewed_by (UUID FK)
    - review_date (DATE)

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- emr_patient_records (1) → clinical_documents (N)
- emr_patient_records (1) → fhir_resources (N)
- emr_patient_records (1) → data_exchange_logs (N)
- emr_patient_records (1) → consent_management (N)
- emr_patient_records (1) → clinical_audit_trail (N)
- patients (1) → patient_portal_access (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- clinical_documents (N) → encounters (1)
- fhir_resources (N) → emr_patient_records (1)
- data_exchange_logs (N) → external_systems (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for patient records
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for record updates

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: ALL clinical domains
- Required by: ALL care providers
- Integrates with: ALL internal and external systems

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with FHIR compliance

- JSONB for flexible clinical data
- FHIR resource standardization
- Granular consent management

Data Types:

- JSONB for structured clinical data
- Arrays for consent scopes and features
- Generated columns for data quality
- Specialized types for audit tracking

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- fhir_resources: Extremely Large (millions of resources)
- clinical_audit_trail: Extremely Large (all access events)
- clinical_documents: Very Large (all clinical documentation)
- data_exchange_logs: Large (all exchange transactions)

Indexing Strategy:

- GIN indexes on all JSONB columns
- Index on fhir_resources (resource_type, patient_id)
- Index on clinical_audit_trail (access_datetime, patient_id)
- Partial indexes for active records and recent access

Partitioning:

- fhir_resources: Range partitioning by created_date (monthly)
- clinical_audit_trail: Range partitioning by access_datetime (daily)
- clinical_documents: Range partitioning by document_date (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Clinicians: Patient data access
- Patients: Own data access via portal
- Administrators: System configuration
- Auditors: Access review and monitoring

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Patient-level data segmentation
- Role-based data access controls
- Emergency break-glass procedures

Sensitive Data:

- PHI protection with encryption
- Audit trails for all access
- Consent enforcement
- Data minimization principles

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for millions of patients
- Real-time data exchange
- AI/ML integration for clinical insights
- Blockchain for consent management

Cross-domain Impact:

- Foundation of digital healthcare
- Enables interoperability
- Supports value-based care
- Facilitates research and innovation

---
## Domain 34: Nurse Station Management

### 1. Assumptions & Clarifications

- Multi-station Support: Multiple nurse stations per floor/department
- Real-time Patient Monitoring: Centralized view of patient statuses
- Task Management: Nursing task assignment and tracking
- Shift Handover: Structured shift change documentation
- Communication Hub: Inter-departmental communication center
- Resource Coordination: Equipment and supply management at station level
- Emergency Response: Rapid response team coordination

### 2. Domain Breakdown

Purpose: Centralized nursing operations management with real-time patient monitoring, task coordination, and communication hub functionality.

Sub-modules:

- Station Configuration
- Patient Assignment
- Task Management
- Shift Handover
- Communication Log
- Resource Inventory
- Emergency Response

Entity List:

- nurse_stations
- patient_assignments
- nursing_tasks
- shift_handovers
- station_communications
- station_inventory
- emergency_response_logs
- nursing_rounds

### 3. Entity Design

nurse_stations

- Purpose: Nurse station configuration and management
- Attributes:
    - station_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_code (VARCHAR(10))
    - station_name (VARCHAR(100))
    - station_type (SMALLINT) - 1=IPD, 2=ICU, 3=Emergency, 4=Specialty
    - physical_location (VARCHAR(255))
    - floor_number (SMALLINT)
    - wing_id (UUID FK)
    - department_id (UUID FK)
    - station_in_charge (UUID FK)
    - contact_extension (VARCHAR(20))
    - coverage_hours (JSONB) - 24/7, day shift, etc.
    - bed_count (SMALLINT)
    - patient_capacity (SMALLINT)
    - station_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Under Staffed, 3=Closed
    - special_capabilities (TEXT[]) - Isolation, Monitoring, Procedure
    - last_audit_date (DATE)

patient_assignments

- Purpose: Patient to nurse assignment
- Attributes:
    - assignment_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - primary_nurse_id (UUID FK)
    - secondary_nurse_id (UUID FK)
    - assignment_date (DATE)
    - assignment_reason (SMALLINT) - 1=Admission, 2=Transfer, 3=Staff Change
    - acuity_level (SMALLINT) - 1=Low, 2=Medium, 3=High, 4=Critical
    - special_instructions (TEXT)
    - assignment_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Transfer Pending, 3=Discharged
    - expected_discharge_date (DATE)
    - nurse_to_patient_ratio (NUMERIC(3,1))
    - last_assessment (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - next_assessment (TIMESTAMPTZ)

nursing_tasks

- Purpose: Nursing task management and tracking
- Attributes:
    - task_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_id (UUID FK)
    - patient_id (UUID FK)
    - task_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Medication, 2=Vitals, 3=Hygiene, 4=Assessment, 5=Procedure
    - task_description (TEXT)
    - priority (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=Stat
    - scheduled_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - due_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - completed_time (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - time_taken_minutes (INTEGER) - Generated
    - assigned_to (UUID FK)
    - verified_by (UUID FK)
    - task_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Pending, 2=In Progress, 3=Completed, 4=Missed, 5=Delegated
    - task_outcome (TEXT)
    - complications (TEXT[])
    - task_category (VARCHAR(100)) - ADL, Clinical, Administrative

shift_handovers

- Purpose: Structured shift change documentation
- Attributes:
    - handover_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_id (UUID FK)
    - shift_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Morning, 2=Evening, 3=Night
    - handing_over_nurse (UUID FK)
    - taking_over_nurse (UUID FK)
    - handover_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - handover_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - patient_summaries (JSONB) - Each patient's status
    - pending_tasks (UUID[]) - Uncompleted tasks
    - critical_events (TEXT[]) - Events needing attention
    - medications_due (TEXT[]) - Next shift medications
    - handover_status (SMALLINT) - 1=In Progress, 2=Completed, 3=Acknowledged
    - quality_rating (SMALLINT) - 1-5 scale
    - missed_information (TEXT[])

station_communications

- Purpose: Station communication log
- Attributes:
    - communication_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_id (UUID FK)
    - communication_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Phone, 2=Intercom, 3=Messaging, 4=Face-to-Face
    - direction (SMALLINT) - 1=Incoming, 2=Outgoing
    - from_party (VARCHAR(255))
    - to_party (VARCHAR(255))
    - subject (VARCHAR(255))
    - message_content (TEXT)
    - communication_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - urgency (SMALLINT) - 1=Routine, 2=Urgent, 3=Emergency
    - action_required (BOOLEAN)
    - action_taken (TEXT)
    - follow_up_needed (BOOLEAN)
    - follow_up_by (UUID FK)
    - communication_status (SMALLINT) - 1=New, 2=In Progress, 3=Resolved

station_inventory

- Purpose: Nurse station inventory management
- Attributes:
    - inventory_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_id (UUID FK)
    - item_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Medication, 2=Supply, 3=Equipment, 4=Forms
    - item_name (VARCHAR(255))
    - item_code (VARCHAR(50))
    - current_stock (INTEGER)
    - par_level (INTEGER)
    - reorder_level (INTEGER)
    - last_restock (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - next_restock (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - storage_location (VARCHAR(100))
    - temperature_control (BOOLEAN)
    - controlled_substance (BOOLEAN)
    - cs_log_reference (UUID FK) - Controlled substance log
    - inventory_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Adequate, 2=Low, 3=Out of Stock
    - last_count_date (DATE)

emergency_response_logs

- Purpose: Emergency response tracking
- Attributes:
    - response_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_id (UUID FK)
    - emergency_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Code Blue, 2=Rapid Response, 3=Stroke, 4=Trauma
    - trigger_reason (TEXT)
    - activation_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - first_responder (UUID FK)
    - response_team (UUID[])
    - patient_condition (TEXT)
    - interventions_performed (TEXT[])
    - response_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - outcome (SMALLINT) - 1=Stabilized, 2=Transfer, 3=Deceased, 4=Ongoing
    - debriefing_conducted (BOOLEAN)
    - debriefing_notes (TEXT)
    - quality_improvement (TEXT[])
    - response_status (SMALLINT) - 1=Active, 2=Completed, 3=Documentation Pending

nursing_rounds

- Purpose: Structured nursing rounds
- Attributes:
    - round_id (UUID PK)
    - tenant_id (UUID FK)
    - branch_id (UUID FK)
    - station_id (UUID FK)
    - round_type (SMALLINT) - 1=Hourly, 2=Clinical, 3=Safety, 4=Leadership
    - round_datetime (TIMESTAMPTZ)
    - conducted_by (UUID FK)
    - patients_visited (UUID[])
    - round_findings (JSONB) - Per patient findings
    - issues_identified (TEXT[])
    - actions_taken (TEXT[])
    - escalation_required (BOOLEAN)
    - escalated_to (UUID FK)
    - round_duration (INTEGER) - Generated
    - round_status (SMALLINT) - 1=In Progress, 2=Completed, 3=Partial
    - quality_indicators (JSONB) - Pain, position, placement, personal items

### 4. Relationship Mapping

1:N Relationships:

- nurse_stations (1) → patient_assignments (N)
- nurse_stations (1) → nursing_tasks (N)
- nurse_stations (1) → shift_handovers (N)
- nurse_stations (1) → station_communications (N)
- nurse_stations (1) → station_inventory (N)
- nurse_stations (1) → emergency_response_logs (N)
- nurse_stations (1) → nursing_rounds (N)

N:1 Relationships:

- patient_assignments (N) → nurse_stations (1)
- nursing_tasks (N) → nurse_stations (1)
- emergency_response_logs (N) → rapid_response_teams (1)

Cascade Rules:

- ON DELETE: RESTRICT for active stations
- ON UPDATE: CASCADE for status changes

Cross-domain Dependencies:

- Depends on: Patient Management, Clinical IPD, Staff Management
- Required by: Nursing operations, Patient care
- Integrates with: Emergency response, Pharmacy, Laboratory

### 5. Database Schema Strategy

Normalization: 3NF with real-time operations

- Task management optimization
- Inventory tracking
- Communication logging

Data Types:

- JSONB for patient summaries and findings
- Arrays for tasks and communications
- Generated columns for durations
- Specialized types for clinical data

### 6. Performance Considerations

Table Size:

- nursing_tasks: Very Large (thousands daily)
- station_communications: Large (all station communications)
- patient_assignments: Medium (patient-nurse assignments)
- shift_handovers: Medium (shift changes)

Indexing Strategy:

- Index on nursing_tasks (due_time, task_status)
- Index on patient_assignments (assignment_status, station_id)
- Partial indexes for pending tasks and active emergencies
- GIN index on JSONB clinical data

Partitioning:

- nursing_tasks: Range partitioning by scheduled_time (daily)
- station_communications: Range partitioning by communication_datetime (monthly)

### 7. Security Design

RBAC Considerations:

- Charge Nurses: Station management
- Staff Nurses: Task execution
- Nursing Supervisors: Oversight
- Support Staff: Inventory management

RLS Design:

- Tenant isolation on all tables
- Station-level data segregation
- Patient privacy protection

Sensitive Data:

- Patient assignment information protected
- Emergency response details secured
- Communication logs confidential

### 8. Scalability Notes

Future Growth:

- Support for 100+ nurse stations
- Real-time task tracking
- Mobile nursing integration
- Predictive workload forecasting

Cross-domain Impact:

- Central hub for nursing operations
- Critical for patient safety
- Major operational efficiency driver
- Quality improvement foundation

---

I'll design the remaining domains to complete the comprehensive Hospital ERP system. Given the extensive scope, I'll provide concise but complete designs for each domain.

- Waitlist Management: Automatic waitlist handling
- Overbooking Control: Intelligent overbooking prevention
- Recurring Appointments: Pattern-based recurring scheduling
- Cancellation Management: Cancellation reason tracking and analytics
